# 前后端接口兼容性清单

## 第三阶段进度（2026-11-16）

错题录入管理、图片上传裁剪、媒体访问、识别任务及人工确认已迁移，新增覆盖13条路径、17个方法路径组合。默认列表与签名规则保持兼容，可选分页/年级/知识点筛选和排序属于向后兼容扩展。真实模型及测试结果见《第三阶段迁移完成与验收报告》。下文完整审计清单仍包含后续未迁移业务。

## 账号认证迁移进度（2026-11-16）

Python已实现7个业务方法接口及本地管理员命令，并接入原/v1/compute处理器。真实MySQL与跨后端兼容测试通过，范围、命令和限制见《账号认证迁移完成与验收记录》。下文保留原审计与完整迁移设计，不代表所有业务已完成。

## 本轮校准依据

审计日期 2026-11-16，版本 v2。最终源码基线为远程 origin/main 的 `1e116f574a88bb009b618218df23f679adc7a5fe`，父提交为 `b7c684fa48a0c3df84eaacb9243e7138b05fcafa`。旧审计基线 `8dd7630fe88a566345baa67c91879d8edefb73e6` 与该主线分叉。本轮逐文件对比生产源码并复查改变的启动、部署、依赖和测试入口，校准证据见迁移审计校准报告。下文保留的源码摘录经字节对比后沿用。数据库实存 61 表属于上一轮本地环境的只读快照，远程初始化和升级代码定义 51 表，不能将本地快照视为每个部署的必备结构。

当前 main 仅保留两个 Python 测试文件，仓颉单元测试、前端测试目录和旧 smoke/verify 脚本已删除。下文涉及旧测试路径均为历史证据，应在隔离环境恢复或重建相应用例后再验收。迁移成功项仍为零。

统计注册路径 83 条。每条路径在新服务中保持原路径，并按下列方法分别注册。固定路径必须先于参数路径，保留 router.cj 的 exact → prefixes → patterns 匹配语义。

成功包装为 `{success:true,data:...,error:null,request_id:...}`。错误为 `{detail:"中文消息"}`，ApiError 保留业务状态码，DatabaseException 为 503，其他异常为 500。方法错误 405，JSON 格式错误 422，超限 413。FastAPI 需兼容异常处理器，避免自动 307、默认 422 错误结构或额外字段过滤改变现有行为。

## GET /api/v1/auth/register

- 新接口映射 `/api/v1/auth/register`，方法保持 GET
- 注册证据 `backend-cangjie/src/account_api.cj:164`，处理函数 `register`
- 认证 公开入口，当前实现未调用 currentUser
- 权限 遵循处理函数及所有权 helper，学生业务按 user_id/owner 限定
- 成功状态 200，错误状态证据 见内联处理与公共规则
- 请求路径参数 无
- 请求 JSON 直接字段 以以下源代码读取行为为准，可能无 JSON 或使用 multipart
- 响应结构直接构建字段 由返回 helper/数据库行构建，见调用闭包与 rowJson 类型字典
- 调用闭包 `register`

以下保留完整处理函数实现，包含分支参数、请求对象读取、验证、返回构造与错误规则。共享 helper 的 SQL、返回键及验证证据在功能清单逐函数索引。字段精确类型还需结合数据库报告中的 column_types 与算法 Pydantic 模型。

```cangjie

    public func register(path: String, handler: HttpRequestHandler): Unit {
        if (path.endsWith("*")) {
            prefixes.add((path[..path.size - 1], handler))
        } else if (path.contains("{")) {
            patterns.add((path.split("/"), handler))
        } else {
            if (exact.contains(path)) { throw IllegalArgumentException("重复路由") }
            exact.add(path, handler)
        }
    }

```

```cangjie

    public func login(context: HttpContext): JsonValue {
        requireMethod(context, "POST")
        let body = readJson(context)
        let email = emailField(body)
        let password = textField(body, "password", 256, trim: false)
        try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
            let rows = db.query("SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ?", [Some(email)]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty() || !["student","admin"].contains(rowText(rows[0],"role")) || !verifyPassword(password, rowText(rows[0], "password_hash"))) {
                throw ApiError(401, "账号或密码不正确")
            }
            return session(rows[0])
        }
        throw ApiError(503, "数据库暂时不可用")
    }

```

以下保留完整处理函数实现，包含分支参数、请求对象读取、验证、返回构造与错误规则。共享 helper 的 SQL、返回键及验证证据在功能清单逐函数索引。字段精确类型还需结合数据库报告中的 column_types 与算法 Pydantic 模型。

```cangjie

    public func resetPassword(context: HttpContext): JsonValue {
        requireMethod(context, "POST")
        let body = readJson(context)
        let email = emailField(body)
        let current = textField(body, "current_password", 256, trim: false)
        let replacement = textField(body, "new_password", 256, trim: false)
        let hash = hashPassword(replacement)
        try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
            db.transaction<Unit>({ tx =>
                let rows = tx.query("SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ? FOR UPDATE", [Some(email)]).rows
                if (rows.isEmpty() || !verifyPassword(current, rowText(rows[0], "password_hash"))) {
                    throw ApiError(401, "账号或当前密码不正确，无法修改密码")
                }
                tx.query("UPDATE users SET password_hash = ?, updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?",
                    [Some(hash), rows[0]["id"]])
                ()
            })
        }
        let result = JsonObject()
        result.put("email", JsonString(email))
        result.put("reset", JsonBool(true))
        return result
    }

```

以下保留完整处理函数实现，包含分支参数、请求对象读取、验证、返回构造与错误规则。共享 helper 的 SQL、返回键及验证证据在功能清单逐函数索引。字段精确类型还需结合数据库报告中的 column_types 与算法 Pydantic 模型。

```cangjie

    public func me(context: HttpContext): JsonValue {
        if (context.request.method != "GET" && context.request.method != "PATCH") {
            context.responseBuilder.header("Allow", "GET, PATCH")
            throw ApiError(405, "请求方法不支持")
        }
        try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
            let user = currentUser(context, db)
            if (context.request.method == "PATCH") {
                let body = readJson(context)
                taskStrict(body,["name","email","grade","current_password"])
                let name=if (body.get("name").isSome()) { textField(body,"name",100) } else { rowText(user,"name") }
                let email=if (body.get("email").isSome()) { emailField(body) } else { rowText(user,"email") }
                let grade=if (body.get("grade").isSome()) { textField(body,"grade",30) } else { "" }
                if (!grade.isEmpty() && (rowText(user,"role")!="student" || !["初一","初二","初三","高一","高二","高三"].contains(grade))) { throw ApiError(422,"请选择有效年级") }
                try {
                    return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
                        let current=tx.query("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id=? FOR UPDATE",[user["id"]]).rows[0]
                        if (email!=rowText(current,"email")) {
                            let password=textField(body,"current_password",256,trim:false)
                            if (!verifyPassword(password,rowText(current,"password_hash"))) { throw ApiError(422,"当前密码不正确，邮箱未修改") }
                        }
                        tx.query("UPDATE users SET name=?,email=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[Some(name),Some(email),user["id"]])
                        if (!grade.isEmpty()) {
                            tx.query("UPDATE student_profiles SET grade=?,preferences=JSON_SET(COALESCE(preferences,JSON_OBJECT()),'$.experience_version',COALESCE(JSON_EXTRACT(preferences,'$.experience_version'),0)+1),updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE user_id=?",[Some(grade),user["id"]])
                        }
                        userJson(tx.query("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id=?",[user["id"]]).rows[0])
                    })
                } catch (error: DatabaseException) {
                    if (error.code==1062) { throw ApiError(409,"这个邮箱已被使用，请换一个邮箱") }
                    throw error
                }
            }
            return userJson(user)
        }
        throw ApiError(503, "数据库暂时不可用")
    }

```

- 调用闭包 `academicSummary`, `analysisFingerprint`, `close`, `constantEqual`, `controlTransaction`, `credentialTag`, `currentUser`, `decodeBase64`, `diagnosisFingerprint`, `firstJson`, `fromEnvironment`, `hmac256`, `jsonText`, `learningTasks`, `mediaUrl`, `newId`, `optionalText`, `query`, `queryArray`, `queryLast`, `randomBytes`, `read`, `readJson`, `readToken`, `requireMethod`, `rowJson`, `rowText`, `runeCount`, `sha256`, `shortTitle`, `sqlValue`, `stripWhitespace`, `taskAtLimit`, `taskFingerprint`, `taskLimitView`, `taskOwner`, `taskPublic`, `taskRow`, `taskSnapshot`, `taskSource`, `taskSourceEqual`, `taskStoppedView`, `taskStrict`, `taskValidateVersion`, `textField`, `transaction`, `tutorAnswerText`, `tutorArray`, `tutorDataSnapshot`, `tutorFlag`, `tutorNumber`, `tutorObject`, `tutorText`, `unixNow`, `unpaddedBase64`, `urlBase64`, `zw_equal`, `zw_hmac`, `zw_media_close`, `zw_media_open`, `zw_media_stat`, `zw_mysql_transaction`, `zw_random`, `zw_sha256`, `zw_stmt_affected`, `zw_stmt_bind`, `zw_stmt_close`, `zw_stmt_column_name`, `zw_stmt_columns`, `zw_stmt_copy`, `zw_stmt_error`, `zw_stmt_execute`, `zw_stmt_length`, `zw_stmt_next`, `zw_stmt_param_count`, `zw_stmt_prepare`, `zw_stmt_value`

以下保留完整处理函数实现，包含分支参数、请求对象读取、验证、返回构造与错误规则。共享 helper 的 SQL、返回键及验证证据在功能清单逐函数索引。字段精确类型还需结合数据库报告中的 column_types 与算法 Pydantic 模型。

```cangjie

public func learningTasks(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let owner=taskOwner(context,db,accounts)
        if (context.request.method=="GET") {
            let result=JsonArray()
            let form=try { Form(context.request.url.rawQuery ?? "") } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422,"筛选条件不正确") }
            let questionId=queryLast(form,"question_id")
            for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM learning_tasks WHERE user_id=? AND (?='' OR question_id=?) ORDER BY updated_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 50",[Some(owner),Some(questionId),Some(questionId)]).rows) {
                let item=taskPublic(rowJson("learning_tasks",row),accounts)
                let titles=db.query("SELECT question_text FROM questions WHERE id=?",[row["question_id"]]).rows
                item.put("question_title",JsonString(if (titles.isEmpty()) { "本题学习" } else { academicSummary(rowText(titles[0],"question_text"),60) }))
                result.add(item)
            }
            return result
        }
        requireMethod(context,"POST"); let body=readJson(context); taskStrict(body,["question_id","session_id","request_id"])
        let questionId=textField(body,"question_id",36); let requestId=textField(body,"request_id",80)
        let sessionId=optionalText(body,"session_id","",36)
        let digest=sha256((questionId+"|"+sessionId).toArray())
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let snapshot=taskSnapshot(tx,questionId,owner); let question=tutorObject(snapshot,"question")
            let previous=tx.query("SELECT task_id,body_hash FROM learning_task_requests WHERE user_id=? AND request_id=?",[Some(owner),Some(requestId)]).rows
            if (!previous.isEmpty()) {
                if (rowText(previous[0],"body_hash")!=digest) { throw ApiError(409,"同一请求编号不能用于不同任务") }
                let task=taskRow(tx,rowText(previous[0],"task_id"),owner,lock:true); taskValidateVersion(tx,task,snapshot); return taskPublic(task,accounts)
            }
            if (tutorText(question,"status")=="archived" || !tutorFlag(question,"confirmed") || !(snapshot["analysis"] is JsonObject) || stripWhitespace(tutorText(tutorObject(snapshot,"analysis"),"final_answer")).isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(409,"请先核对题目并完成分析，再开始订正学习") }
            if (!sessionId.isEmpty() && tx.query("SELECT id FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND question_id=?",[Some(sessionId),Some(owner),Some(questionId)]).rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"关联学习会话不存在") }
            var id=""; var sourceOrigin="legacy_revalidated"
            let active=tx.query("SELECT * FROM learning_tasks WHERE user_id=? AND question_id=? AND status IN ('queued','running','waiting_student','needs_review','failed') ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE",[Some(owner),Some(questionId)]).rows
            if (!active.isEmpty()) {
                let task=rowJson("learning_tasks",active[0]); if (taskValidateVersion(tx,task,snapshot)) { id=jsonText(task,"id") }
            }
            if (id.isEmpty()) {
                id=newId(); sourceOrigin="created"; let event=JsonObject(); event.put("kind",JsonString("start")); event.put("text",JsonString(""))
                tx.query("INSERT INTO learning_tasks (id,user_id,question_id,session_id,input_fingerprint,status,step_id,checkpoint,student_view,pending_action,active_request,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,'queued',?,JSON_OBJECT(),?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(id),Some(owner),Some(questionId),if (sessionId.isEmpty()) { None } else { Some(sessionId) },Some(taskFingerprint(snapshot)),Some(newId()),Some(taskStoppedView("正在准备这一步").toString()),Some(event.toString()),Some("__initial__")])
            }
            taskSource(tx,taskRow(tx,id,owner),snapshot,origin:sourceOrigin)
            tx.query("INSERT INTO learning_task_requests (user_id,request_id,task_id,body_hash,created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(owner),Some(requestId),Some(id),Some(digest)])
            return taskPublic(taskRow(tx,id,owner),accounts)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

public func readLearningTask(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-1]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let owner=taskOwner(context,db,accounts); let initial=taskRow(db,id,owner)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let snapshot=taskSnapshot(tx,jsonText(initial,"question_id"),owner); let task=taskRow(tx,id,owner,lock:true)
            taskValidateVersion(tx,task,snapshot); return taskPublic(task,accounts)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie
public func learningTaskAction(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts, cancel!: Bool=false): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let owner=taskOwner(context,db,accounts); let initial=taskRow(db,id,owner); let body=readJson(context,defaultEmpty:cancel)
        if (!cancel) { taskStrict(body,["kind","text","version","step_id","input_fingerprint","request_id","handwriting_id","handwriting_confirmed","visual_values"]) }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let snapshot=taskSnapshot(tx,jsonText(initial,"question_id"),owner); let task=taskRow(tx,id,owner,lock:true)
            if (cancel) {
                if (!["completed","cancelled","stale"].contains(jsonText(task,"status"))) {
                    tx.query("UPDATE learning_tasks SET status='cancelled',cancel_requested=1,version=version+1,lease_token=NULL,lease_until=NULL,student_view=?,exit_reason='student_cancelled',updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(taskStoppedView("已结束这次学习，之前的记录仍在").toString()),Some(id)])
                    tx.query("UPDATE learning_task_actions SET status='cancelled',updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE task_id=? AND status='pending'",[Some(id)])
                }
                return taskPublic(taskRow(tx,id,owner),accounts)
            }
            let requestId=textField(body,"request_id",80); let visualValues=taskVisualValues(body); let values=JsonArray(); for (key in ["kind","text","version","step_id","input_fingerprint","handwriting_id","handwriting_confirmed"]) { values.add(body.get(key) ?? if (key=="text") { JsonString("") } else { JsonNull() }) }; let visualHash=JsonArray(); for (key in ["delta","k","b","a","h"]) { visualHash.add(visualValues.get(key) ?? JsonNull()) }; if (body.get("visual_values").isSome()) { values.add(visualHash) }; let digest=sha256(values.toString().toArray())
            if (requestId=="__initial__") { throw ApiError(422,"请求编号不可用") }
            let prior=tx.query("SELECT body_hash FROM learning_task_actions WHERE task_id=? AND request_id=?",[Some(id),Some(requestId)]).rows
            if (!prior.isEmpty()) {
                if (rowText(prior[0],"body_hash")!=digest) { throw ApiError(409,"同一请求编号不能修改提交内容") }
                taskValidateVersion(tx,task,snapshot); return taskPublic(task,accounts)
            }
            if (!taskValidateVersion(tx,task,snapshot)) { return taskPublic(task,accounts) }
            let status=jsonText(task,"status")
            if (!["waiting_student","needs_review","failed"].contains(status)) { throw ApiError(409,"任务仍在处理或已结束，请先刷新状态") }
            let version=body.get("version") ?? JsonNull()
            if (!(version is JsonInt) || !jsonEquivalent(version,task["version"]) || textField(body,"step_id",36)!=jsonText(task,"step_id") || textField(body,"input_fingerprint",64)!=jsonText(task,"input_fingerprint")) { throw ApiError(409,"这一步已更新，请保留内容并刷新后再提交") }
            let kind=textField(body,"kind",30)
            if (!["answer","hint","reveal","resume","use_text","visualize","observe_visualization","close_visualization"].contains(kind)) { throw ApiError(422,"不支持的学习动作") }
            validateTaskVisualAction(task,body,kind,visualValues)
            let text=if (kind=="observe_visualization") { textField(body,"text",1200) } else { optionalText(body,"text","",12000) }
            if (["answer","observe_visualization"].contains(kind) && stripWhitespace(text).isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422,"请先写下你的回答") }
            if (taskAtLimit(task)) { throw ApiError(409,"这次学习已到上限，请结束后回原题重开") }
            let event=JsonObject(); event.put("kind",JsonString(kind)); event.put("text",JsonString(text))
            if (kind=="observe_visualization") { event.put("visual_values",visualValues) }
            attachTaskImage(tx,task,body,event)
            if (kind=="resume" && status=="failed" && tutorText(tutorObject(task,"pending_action"),"kind")=="recognize_handwriting") {
                event.put("kind",JsonString("recognize_handwriting")); event.put("handwriting_id",tutorObject(task,"pending_action")["handwriting_id"])
            }
            tx.query("INSERT INTO learning_task_actions (id,task_id,request_id,body_hash,kind,input_version,input_step_id,body,status,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,'pending',UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(id),Some(requestId),Some(digest),Some(kind),sqlValue(version),sqlValue(task["step_id"]),Some(event.toString())])
            tx.query("UPDATE learning_tasks SET status='queued',version=version+1,pending_action=?,active_request=?,lease_token=NULL,lease_until=NULL,exit_reason='',updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(event.toString()),Some(requestId),Some(id)])
            return taskPublic(taskRow(tx,id,owner),accounts)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

public func uploadTaskHandwriting(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
    let written=JsonObject()
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let owner=taskOwner(context,db,accounts); let initial=taskRow(db,id,owner); let upload=readImageUpload(context)
        let body=JsonObject()
        for ((key,value) in upload.fields) {
            if (!["request_id","version","step_id","input_fingerprint"].contains(key)) { throw ApiError(422,"上传包含不支持的字段") }
            body.put(key,JsonString(value))
        }
        let requestId=textField(body,"request_id",80); let step=textField(body,"step_id",36); let fingerprint=textField(body,"input_fingerprint",64)
        if (requestId=="__initial__") { throw ApiError(422,"请求编号不可用") }
        let version=try { Int64.parse(textField(body,"version",12)) } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422,"步骤版本无效") }
        let bytes=upload.bytes
        let extension=if (bytesMatch(bytes,[0x89,0x50,0x4e,0x47,0x0d,0x0a,0x1a,0x0a],0)) { "png" } else if (bytesMatch(bytes,[0xff,0xd8,0xff],0)) { "jpg" } else if (bytesMatch(bytes,"RIFF".toArray(),0) && bytesMatch(bytes,"WEBP".toArray(),8)) { "webp" } else { throw ApiError(422,"请上传JPEG、PNG或WEBP作答照片") }
        let checksum=sha256(bytes); let values=JsonArray(); for (value in [version.toString(),step,fingerprint,checksum]) { values.add(JsonString(value)) }; let digest=sha256(values.toString().toArray())
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let snapshot=taskSnapshot(tx,jsonText(initial,"question_id"),owner); let task=taskRow(tx,id,owner,lock:true)
            let prior=tx.query("SELECT body_hash FROM learning_task_actions WHERE task_id=? AND request_id=?",[Some(id),Some(requestId)]).rows
            if (!prior.isEmpty()) {
                if (rowText(prior[0],"body_hash")!=digest) { throw ApiError(409,"同一上传编号不能更换照片或步骤") }
                taskValidateVersion(tx,task,snapshot); return taskPublic(task,accounts)
            }
            if (!taskValidateVersion(tx,task,snapshot)) { return taskPublic(task,accounts) }
            if (!["waiting_student","needs_review"].contains(tutorText(task,"status")) || tutorText(tutorObject(task,"student_view"),"input_kind")!="text") { throw ApiError(409,"请先回到当前可作答步骤再上传") }
            if (version!=tutorNumber(task,"version") || step!=jsonText(task,"step_id") || fingerprint!=jsonText(task,"input_fingerprint")) { throw ApiError(409,"学习步骤已更新，请刷新后重新上传") }
            if (taskAtLimit(task)) { throw ApiError(409,"这次学习已到上限，请结束后回原题重开") }
            let imageId=newId(); let path="uploads/answers/${owner}/learning_task/${id}/${imageId}.${extension}"
            written.put("path",JsonString(path)); writeMedia(path,bytes)
            tx.query("INSERT INTO learning_task_images (id,task_id,request_id,input_fingerprint,upload_step_id,original_path,sha256,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(imageId),Some(id),Some(requestId),Some(fingerprint),Some(step),Some(path),Some(checksum)])
            let event=JsonObject(); event.put("kind",JsonString("recognize_handwriting")); event.put("text",JsonString("")); event.put("handwriting_id",JsonString(imageId))
            tx.query("INSERT INTO learning_task_actions (id,task_id,request_id,body_hash,kind,input_version,input_step_id,body,status,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,'recognize_handwriting',?,?,?,'pending',UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(id),Some(requestId),Some(digest),Some(version.toString()),Some(step),Some(event.toString())])
            tx.query("UPDATE learning_tasks SET status='queued',version=version+1,pending_action=?,active_request=?,lease_token=NULL,lease_until=NULL,exit_reason='',updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(event.toString()),Some(requestId),Some(id)])
            return taskPublic(taskRow(tx,id,owner),accounts)
        })
    } catch (error: Exception) { if (!tutorText(written,"path").isEmpty()) { removeMedia(tutorText(written,"path")) }; throw error }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

- 响应结构直接构建字段 由返回 helper/数据库行构建，见调用闭包与 rowJson 类型字典
- 调用闭包 `analysisFingerprint`, `auditSelfCheck`, `close`, `constantEqual`, `controlTransaction`, `credentialTag`, `currentUser`, `decodeBase64`, `diagnosisFingerprint`, `firstJson`, `fromEnvironment`, `hmac256`, `jsonEquivalent`, `jsonText`, `learningSelfCheck`, `newId`, `query`, `queryArray`, `randomBytes`, `read`, `readJson`, `readToken`, `rowJson`, `rowText`, `selfCheckPublic`, `selfCheckRow`, `sha256`, `sqlValue`, `stripWhitespace`, `taskFingerprint`, `taskOwner`, `taskRow`, `taskSnapshot`, `taskStoppedView`, `taskStrict`, `taskValidateVersion`, `textField`, `transaction`, `tutorArray`, `tutorDataSnapshot`, `tutorFlag`, `tutorNumber`, `tutorObject`, `tutorText`, `unixNow`, `unpaddedBase64`, `urlBase64`, `zw_equal`, `zw_hmac`, `zw_media_close`, `zw_media_open`, `zw_media_stat`, `zw_mysql_transaction`, `zw_random`, `zw_sha256`, `zw_stmt_affected`, `zw_stmt_bind`, `zw_stmt_close`, `zw_stmt_column_name`, `zw_stmt_columns`, `zw_stmt_copy`, `zw_stmt_error`, `zw_stmt_execute`, `zw_stmt_length`, `zw_stmt_next`, `zw_stmt_param_count`, `zw_stmt_prepare`, `zw_stmt_value`

以下保留完整处理函数实现，包含分支参数、请求对象读取、验证、返回构造与错误规则。共享 helper 的 SQL、返回键及验证证据在功能清单逐函数索引。字段精确类型还需结合数据库报告中的 column_types 与算法 Pydantic 模型。

```cangjie

public func learningSelfCheck(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    let method=context.request.method
    if (!["GET","PATCH","DELETE"].contains(method)) { throw ApiError(405,"此接口不支持该方法") }
    let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let owner=taskOwner(context,db,accounts); let initial=taskRow(db,id,owner)
        let body=if (method=="GET") { JsonObject() } else { readJson(context) }
        if (method!="GET") { taskStrict(body,if (method=="DELETE") { ["version"] } else { ["text","version","confirmed","input_fingerprint"] }) }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let snapshot=taskSnapshot(tx,jsonText(initial,"question_id"),owner); let task=taskRow(tx,id,owner,lock:true)
            let current=taskValidateVersion(tx,task,snapshot); let canSave=current && tutorText(task,"status")=="completed"
            let note=selfCheckRow(tx,task); let version=if (note.getFields().isEmpty()) { 0 } else { tutorNumber(note,"version") }
            if (method=="GET") { return selfCheckPublic(note,current,canSave) }
            let supplied=body.get("version") ?? JsonNull()
            if (!(supplied is JsonInt) || (supplied as JsonInt).getOrThrow().getValue()<0) { throw ApiError(422,"请提供要点版本") }
            if (method=="DELETE" && (note.getFields().isEmpty() || tutorFlag(note,"deleted"))) { return selfCheckPublic(note,current,canSave) }
            var text=""
            if (method=="PATCH") {
                if (!canSave) { throw ApiError(409,"请完成当前题目版本的学习，再保存自检要点") }
                let confirmation=body.get("confirmed") ?? JsonNull()
                if (!(confirmation is JsonBool) || !tutorFlag(body,"confirmed")) { throw ApiError(422,"请先确认这是你要保存的自检要点") }
                if (textField(body,"input_fingerprint",64)!=jsonText(task,"input_fingerprint")) { throw ApiError(409,"原题版本已变化，请重新核对") }
                text=textField(body,"text",300)
                if (stripWhitespace(text).isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422,"请写一句自检要点") }
                // Retrying an acknowledged-but-lost response does not create a new revision/audit.
                if (!note.getFields().isEmpty() && !tutorFlag(note,"deleted") && jsonText(note,"text")==text) { return selfCheckPublic(note,current,canSave) }
            }
            if (!jsonEquivalent(supplied,JsonInt(version))) { throw ApiError(409,"自检要点已在别处更新，请重新读取后再保存") }
            if (note.getFields().isEmpty()) {
                tx.query("INSERT INTO learning_self_checks (id,user_id,question_id,task_id,input_fingerprint,text,version,deleted,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,1,0,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(owner),sqlValue(task["question_id"]),Some(id),sqlValue(task["input_fingerprint"]),Some(text)])
            } else {
                tx.query("UPDATE learning_self_checks SET text=?,deleted=?,version=version+1,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(text),Some(if (method=="DELETE") { "1" } else { "0" }),sqlValue(note["id"])])
            }
            let saved=selfCheckRow(tx,task); auditSelfCheck(tx,task,saved,if (method=="DELETE") { "delete" } else { if (version==0) { "create" } else { "edit" } })
            return selfCheckPublic(saved,current,canSave)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

public func learningTaskHistory(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let owner=taskOwner(context,db,accounts); taskRow(db,id,owner)
        let items=JsonArray(); let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM learning_task_actions WHERE task_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 51",[Some(id)]).rows
        for (index in 0..rows.size) {
            if (index>=50) { break }
            let action=rowJson("learning_task_actions",rows[index]); let result=tutorObject(action,"result"); let item=JsonObject()
            for (key in ["id","kind","status","created_at"]) { item.put(key,action[key]) }
            item.put("text",JsonString(tutorText(tutorObject(action,"body"),"text")))
            // Only public feedback and the verdict/scope of this submitted attempt leave the server.
            item.put("feedback",JsonString(tutorText(tutorObject(result,"student_view"),"feedback")))
            let attempt=tutorObject(result,"attempt")
            item.put("phase",JsonString(tutorText(attempt,"phase"))); item.put("verdict",JsonString(tutorText(attempt,"verdict"))); item.put("assisted",JsonBool(tutorFlag(attempt,"assisted")))
            item.put("source",attempt.get("source") ?? JsonNull())
            let kind=tutorText(action,"kind"); let visual=tutorObject(tutorObject(result,"checkpoint"),"visualization")
            item.put("visualization",JsonNull())
            if (["visualize","observe_visualization","close_visualization"].contains(kind) && !visual.getFields().isEmpty()) {
                let summary=JsonObject(); summary.put("title",tutorObject(visual,"spec")["title"]); summary.put("relation_tex",visual["current_equation_tex"])
                let controls=StringBuilder()
                for (rawVariable in tutorArray(tutorObject(visual,"spec"),"interactive_variables").getItems()) {
                    let variable=(rawVariable as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let name=tutorText(variable,"name")
                    controls.append(tutorText(variable,"label")+"："+tutorObject(visual,"values")[name].toString()+"；")
                }
                summary.put("controls",JsonString(controls.toString())); item.put("visualization",summary); item.put("phase",visual["source_phase"]); item.put("assisted",JsonBool(true))
            }
            let imageId=tutorText(tutorObject(action,"body"),"handwriting_id")
            item.put("handwriting_image_url",JsonString(if (imageId.isEmpty()) { "" } else { accounts.mediaUrl("/"+jsonText(taskImage(db,taskRow(db,id,owner),imageId),"original_path")) }))
            items.add(item)
        }
        let output=JsonObject(); output.put("items",items); output.put("has_more",JsonBool(rows.size>50)); output.put("source_question",taskSourcePublic(db,id)); return output
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

```cangjie

public func learningTaskAction(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts, cancel!: Bool=false): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let owner=taskOwner(context,db,accounts); let initial=taskRow(db,id,owner); let body=readJson(context,defaultEmpty:cancel)
        if (!cancel) { taskStrict(body,["kind","text","version","step_id","input_fingerprint","request_id","handwriting_id","handwriting_confirmed","visual_values"]) }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let snapshot=taskSnapshot(tx,jsonText(initial,"question_id"),owner); let task=taskRow(tx,id,owner,lock:true)
            if (cancel) {
                if (!["completed","cancelled","stale"].contains(jsonText(task,"status"))) {
                    tx.query("UPDATE learning_tasks SET status='cancelled',cancel_requested=1,version=version+1,lease_token=NULL,lease_until=NULL,student_view=?,exit_reason='student_cancelled',updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(taskStoppedView("已结束这次学习，之前的记录仍在").toString()),Some(id)])
                    tx.query("UPDATE learning_task_actions SET status='cancelled',updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE task_id=? AND status='pending'",[Some(id)])
                }
                return taskPublic(taskRow(tx,id,owner),accounts)
            }
            let requestId=textField(body,"request_id",80); let visualValues=taskVisualValues(body); let values=JsonArray(); for (key in ["kind","text","version","step_id","input_fingerprint","handwriting_id","handwriting_confirmed"]) { values.add(body.get(key) ?? if (key=="text") { JsonString("") } else { JsonNull() }) }; let visualHash=JsonArray(); for (key in ["delta","k","b","a","h"]) { visualHash.add(visualValues.get(key) ?? JsonNull()) }; if (body.get("visual_values").isSome()) { values.add(visualHash) }; let digest=sha256(values.toString().toArray())
            if (requestId=="__initial__") { throw ApiError(422,"请求编号不可用") }
            let prior=tx.query("SELECT body_hash FROM learning_task_actions WHERE task_id=? AND request_id=?",[Some(id),Some(requestId)]).rows
            if (!prior.isEmpty()) {
                if (rowText(prior[0],"body_hash")!=digest) { throw ApiError(409,"同一请求编号不能修改提交内容") }
                taskValidateVersion(tx,task,snapshot); return taskPublic(task,accounts)
            }
            if (!taskValidateVersion(tx,task,snapshot)) { return taskPublic(task,accounts) }
            let status=jsonText(task,"status")
            if (!["waiting_student","needs_review","failed"].contains(status)) { throw ApiError(409,"任务仍在处理或已结束，请先刷新状态") }
            let version=body.get("version") ?? JsonNull()
            if (!(version is JsonInt) || !jsonEquivalent(version,task["version"]) || textField(body,"step_id",36)!=jsonText(task,"step_id") || textField(body,"input_fingerprint",64)!=jsonText(task,"input_fingerprint")) { throw ApiError(409,"这一步已更新，请保留内容并刷新后再提交") }
            let kind=textField(body,"kind",30)
            if (!["answer","hint","reveal","resume","use_text","visualize","observe_visualization","close_visualization"].contains(kind)) { throw ApiError(422,"不支持的学习动作") }
            validateTaskVisualAction(task,body,kind,visualValues)
            let text=if (kind=="observe_visualization") { textField(body,"text",1200) } else { optionalText(body,"text","",12000) }
            if (["answer","observe_visualization"].contains(kind) && stripWhitespace(text).isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422,"请先写下你的回答") }
            if (taskAtLimit(task)) { throw ApiError(409,"这次学习已到上限，请结束后回原题重开") }
            let event=JsonObject(); event.put("kind",JsonString(kind)); event.put("text",JsonString(text))
            if (kind=="observe_visualization") { event.put("visual_values",visualValues) }
            attachTaskImage(tx,task,body,event)
            if (kind=="resume" && status=="failed" && tutorText(tutorObject(task,"pending_action"),"kind")=="recognize_handwriting") {
                event.put("kind",JsonString("recognize_handwriting")); event.put("handwriting_id",tutorObject(task,"pending_action")["handwriting_id"])
            }
            tx.query("INSERT INTO learning_task_actions (id,task_id,request_id,body_hash,kind,input_version,input_step_id,body,status,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,'pending',UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(id),Some(requestId),Some(digest),Some(kind),sqlValue(version),sqlValue(task["step_id"]),Some(event.toString())])
            tx.query("UPDATE learning_tasks SET status='queued',version=version+1,pending_action=?,active_request=?,lease_token=NULL,lease_until=NULL,exit_reason='',updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(event.toString()),Some(requestId),Some(id)])
            return taskPublic(taskRow(tx,id,owner),accounts)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

## GET /api/v1/dashboard

- 新接口映射 `/api/v1/dashboard`，方法保持 GET
- 注册证据 `backend-cangjie/src/main.cj:32`，处理函数 `learningDashboard`
- 认证 Bearer 令牌，currentUser 校验用户及 credential

- 调用闭包 `canonicalMistakeLabel`, `close`, `cognitionPercent`, `constantEqual`, `controlTransaction`, `credentialTag`, `currentUser`, `dashboardNextStep`, `decodeBase64`, `editNumber`, `fromEnvironment`, `graphTopic`, `hmac256`, `insightPair`, `jsonText`, `learningDashboard`, `query`, `questionDisplayKey`, `readToken`, `requireMethod`, `rowJson`, `rowText`, `stripWhitespace`, `transaction`, `tutorAnswerText`, `tutorArray`, `tutorFlag`, `tutorObject`, `tutorText`, `unixNow`, `unpaddedBase64`, `urlBase64`, `zw_equal`, `zw_hmac`, `zw_media_close`, `zw_mysql_transaction`, `zw_stmt_affected`, `zw_stmt_bind`, `zw_stmt_close`, `zw_stmt_column_name`, `zw_stmt_columns`, `zw_stmt_copy`, `zw_stmt_error`, `zw_stmt_execute`, `zw_stmt_length`, `zw_stmt_next`, `zw_stmt_param_count`, `zw_stmt_prepare`, `zw_stmt_value`

以下保留完整处理函数实现，包含分支参数、请求对象读取、验证、返回构造与错误规则。共享 helper 的 SQL、返回键及验证证据在功能清单逐函数索引。字段精确类型还需结合数据库报告中的 column_types 与算法 Pydantic 模型。

```cangjie

func learningDashboard(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let questions=tx.query("SELECT id,question_text FROM questions WHERE user_id=? AND status<>'archived'",[user["id"]]).rows
            let uniqueQuestions=HashSet<String>()
            for (row in questions) { uniqueQuestions.add(questionDisplayKey(row["question_text"] ?? "",rowText(row,"id"))) }
            let reviewCount=Int64.parse(rowText(tx.query("SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM review_plans WHERE user_id=? AND status='pending' AND due_at<TIMESTAMP(DATE(UTC_TIMESTAMP()+INTERVAL 8 HOUR))+INTERVAL 16 HOUR",[user["id"]]).rows[0],"count"))
            let mastery=JsonArray(); let mistakes=JsonArray(); let discoveries=JsonArray()
            let seenPoints=HashSet<String>(); let seenMistakes=HashSet<String>(); var mastered: Int64=0
            for (row in tx.query("SELECT * FROM mastery_states WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY updated_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 8",[user["id"]]).rows) {
                let key=insightPair(rowText(row,"subject"),rowText(row,"knowledge_point"))
                if (seenPoints.contains(key)) { continue }; seenPoints.add(key)
                let item=rowJson("mastery_states",row); mastery.getItems().add(item)
                if (editNumber(item["mastery_score"])>=0.7) { mastered++ }
            }
            for (row in tx.query("SELECT * FROM misconception_states WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY probability DESC,id LIMIT 8",[user["id"]]).rows) {
                let label=canonicalMistakeLabel(rowText(row,"label")); let key=insightPair(rowText(row,"subject"),label)
                if (seenMistakes.contains(key)) { continue }; seenMistakes.add(key)
                let item=rowJson("misconception_states",row); item.put("label",JsonString(label)); mistakes.getItems().add(item)
                if (discoveries.getItems().size<2) {
                    let discovery=JsonObject(); discovery.put("kind",JsonString("attention")); discovery.put("title",JsonString(label)); discovery.put("detail",JsonString("这是目前最需要优先巩固的错误原因")); discovery.put("value",JsonInt(cognitionPercent(editNumber(item["probability"])))); discoveries.getItems().add(discovery)
                }
            }
            for (index in 0..mastery.getItems().size) {
                if (index>=2) { break }
                let item=(mastery.getItems()[index] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let stable=editNumber(item["mastery_score"])>=0.75
                let discovery=JsonObject(); discovery.put("kind",JsonString(if (stable) { "stable" } else { "progress" })); discovery.put("title",item["knowledge_point"]); discovery.put("detail",JsonString(if (stable) { "掌握状态已经比较稳定" } else { "继续完成相似题会更牢固" })); discovery.put("value",JsonInt(cognitionPercent(editNumber(item["mastery_score"])))); discoveries.getItems().add(discovery)
            }
            let sessions=tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE user_id=? AND status='active' ORDER BY updated_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[user["id"]]).rows
            var continuation: JsonValue=JsonNull()
            if (!sessions.isEmpty()) {
                let session=rowJson("tutor_sessions",sessions[0])
                let active=tx.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND status<>'archived'",[sessions[0]["question_id"],user["id"]]).rows
                if (!active.isEmpty() && !tutorFlag(tutorObject(session,"state"),"question_changed")) {
                    let item=JsonObject(); item.put("session_id",session["id"]); item.put("question_id",session["question_id"]); item.put("stage",session["stage"])
                    item.put("knowledge_point",JsonString(graphTopic(rowJson("questions",active[0])))); item.put("next_step",JsonString(dashboardNextStep(jsonText(session,"stage")))); continuation=item
                }
            }
            var streak: Int64=0; var expected: Int64 = -1
            let days=tx.query("SELECT DISTINCT DATEDIFF(DATE(UTC_TIMESTAMP()+INTERVAL 8 HOUR),DATE(created_at+INTERVAL 8 HOUR)) AS day_offset FROM questions WHERE user_id=? AND status<>'archived' AND created_at IS NOT NULL ORDER BY day_offset",[user["id"]]).rows
            for (row in days) {
                let day=Int64.parse(rowText(row,"day_offset"))
                if (streak==0) { if (day!=0 && day!=1) { break }; expected=day }
                if (day!=expected) { break }; streak++; expected++
            }
            let profiles=tx.query("SELECT grade FROM student_profiles WHERE user_id=? LIMIT 1",[user["id"]]).rows
            let grade: JsonValue=if (profiles.isEmpty()) { JsonString("初二") } else if (let Some(value) <- profiles[0]["grade"]) { JsonString(value) } else { JsonNull() }
            let hasData=!questions.isEmpty() || reviewCount>0 || !mastery.getItems().isEmpty() || !mistakes.getItems().isEmpty() || !(continuation is JsonNull)
            var minutes: Int64=0
            if (hasData) { minutes=reviewCount+if (continuation is JsonNull) { 2 } else { 5 }; if (minutes<3) { minutes=3 } }
            let result=JsonObject(); result.put("today_reviews",JsonInt(reviewCount)); result.put("weekly_questions",JsonInt(uniqueQuestions.size)); result.put("mastered_points",JsonInt(mastered)); result.put("streak_days",JsonInt(streak)); result.put("grade",grade); result.put("estimated_minutes",JsonInt(minutes))
            result.put("continue_learning",continuation); result.put("discoveries",discoveries); result.put("mastery",mastery); result.put("misconceptions",mistakes)
            result.put("ai_suggestion",JsonString(if (hasData) { "先弄懂最常出错的一步，再完成一道相似题。" } else { "拍下第一道错题，我会从你的解题步骤开始分析。" }))
            return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

func studentLearningPreferences(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    let method=context.request.method
    if (method!="GET" && method!="PATCH") { throw ApiError(405,"请求方法不支持") }
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        if (rowText(user,"role")!="student") { throw ApiError(403,"仅学生可以修改本人的学习方向") }
        let body=if (method=="PATCH") { readJson(context) } else { JsonObject() }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows=tx.query("SELECT * FROM student_profiles WHERE user_id=? FOR UPDATE",[user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"学生档案不存在") }
            let profile=rowJson("student_profiles",rows[0]); let preferences=tutorObject(profile,"preferences")
            let version=tutorNumber(preferences,"experience_version")
            if (method=="PATCH") {
                taskStrict(body,["grade","subject","minutes","goal","version"])
                if (!((body.get("version") ?? JsonNull()) is JsonInt) || tutorNumber(body,"version")!=version) { throw ApiError(409,"学习方向已变化，请重新读取后保存") }
                let grade=if (body.get("grade").isSome()) { textField(body,"grade",30) } else { tutorText(profile,"grade") }
                if (body.get("grade").isSome() && !(["初一","初二","初三","高一","高二","高三"].contains(grade))) { throw ApiError(422,"请选择初一至高三的年级") }
                let subject=textField(body,"subject",30)
                if (!(["语文","数学","英语","物理","化学","生物","历史","地理","道德与法治","思想政治"].contains(subject))) { throw ApiError(422,"请选择支持的学科") }
                let minutes=tutorNumber(body,"minutes")
                if (!((body.get("minutes") ?? JsonNull()) is JsonInt) || !([Int64(5),10,20].contains(minutes))) { throw ApiError(422,"请选择5、10或20分钟") }
                let goal=textField(body,"goal",30)
                if (!(["understand","practice","review"].contains(goal))) { throw ApiError(422,"请选择学习目标") }
                preferences.put("focus_subject",JsonString(subject)); preferences.put("daily_minutes",JsonInt(minutes)); preferences.put("focus_goal",JsonString(goal)); preferences.put("experience_version",JsonInt(version+1))
                tx.query("UPDATE student_profiles SET grade=?,preferences=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE user_id=?",[Some(grade),Some(preferences.toString()),user["id"]])
                profile.put("grade",JsonString(grade))
            }
            let result=JsonObject(); result.put("grade",profile["grade"]); result.put("subject",JsonString(tutorText(preferences,"focus_subject"))); result.put("minutes",JsonInt(if (tutorNumber(preferences,"daily_minutes")>0) { tutorNumber(preferences,"daily_minutes") } else { 10 })); result.put("goal",JsonString(if (tutorText(preferences,"focus_goal").isEmpty()) { "understand" } else { tutorText(preferences,"focus_goal") })); result.put("version",JsonInt(tutorNumber(preferences,"experience_version"))); return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie
func studentErrorSources(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    let form=insightQuery(context); let days=insightDays(form,90,365); let subject=queryLast(form,"subject")
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows=tx.query("SELECT q.* FROM questions q JOIN diagnoses d ON d.id=(SELECT d2.id FROM diagnoses d2 WHERE d2.question_id=q.id ORDER BY d2.created_at DESC,d2.id DESC LIMIT 1) WHERE q.user_id=? AND q.status<>'archived' AND q.confirmed=1 AND (?='' OR q.subject=?) AND d.created_at>=TIMESTAMPADD(DAY,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)) ORDER BY q.updated_at DESC,q.id DESC LIMIT 201",[user["id"],Some(subject),Some(subject),Some((-days).toString())]).rows
            let sources=JsonArray(); let seen=HashSet<String>(); var scanned: Int64=0
            for (row in rows) {
                if (scanned>=200) { break }; scanned++
                let key=questionDisplayKey(rowText(row,"question_text"),rowText(row,"id"))
                if (seen.contains(key)) { continue }; seen.add(key)
                let question=rowJson("questions",row)
                let diagnosis=firstJson(queryArray(tx,"diagnoses","SELECT * FROM diagnoses WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",rowText(row,"id")))
                let item=JsonObject()
                for (field in ["id","subject","grade","question_text","knowledge_points","updated_at"]) { item.put(field,question[field]) }
                item.put("diagnosis",diagnosis)
                let plans=tx.query("SELECT id FROM review_plans WHERE user_id=? AND question_id=? AND status='pending' AND due_at<TIMESTAMP(DATE(UTC_TIMESTAMP()+INTERVAL 8 HOUR))+INTERVAL 16 HOUR ORDER BY due_at,id LIMIT 1",[user["id"],row["id"]]).rows
                item.put("due_review_id",if (plans.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { JsonString(rowText(plans[0],"id")) })
                sources.add(item)
            }
            let result=JsonObject(); result.put("sources",sources); result.put("days",JsonInt(days)); result.put("limit",JsonInt(200)); result.put("truncated",JsonBool(rows.size>200)); return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

func learningAnalytics(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    let days = insightDays(insightQuery(context),14,365)
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let mastery = JsonArray(); let mistakes = JsonArray(); let trend = JsonArray()
            let seenPoints = HashSet<String>()
            for (row in tx.query("SELECT * FROM mastery_states WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY updated_at DESC,id DESC",[user["id"]]).rows) {
                let key = insightPair(rowText(row,"subject"),rowText(row,"knowledge_point"))
                if (seenPoints.contains(key)) { continue }; seenPoints.add(key)
                mastery.getItems().add(rowJson("mastery_states",row))
            }
            let seenMistakes = HashSet<String>()
            for (row in tx.query("SELECT * FROM misconception_states WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY probability DESC,id",[user["id"]]).rows) {
                let label = canonicalMistakeLabel(rowText(row,"label")); let key = insightPair(rowText(row,"subject"),label)
                if (seenMistakes.contains(key)) { continue }; seenMistakes.add(key)
                let item = rowJson("misconception_states",row); item.put("label",JsonString(label))
                for (field in ["successful_strategy","failed_strategy"]) {
                    let raw = tutorText(item,field)
                    item.put(field,JsonString(if (raw.isEmpty()) { "" } else { learningMethodLabel(raw) }))
                }
                mistakes.getItems().add(item)
            }
            // Keep the original rolling UTC window and group by UTC date, not local midnight.
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT DATE(created_at) AS day,AVG(new_value) AS average,COUNT(*) AS count FROM cognitive_updates WHERE user_id=? AND entity_type='mastery' AND created_at>=TIMESTAMPADD(DAY,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)) GROUP BY DATE(created_at) ORDER BY day",[user["id"],Some((1-days).toString())]).rows
            var evidenceCount: Int64 = 0
            for (row in rows) {
                let count = Int64.parse(rowText(row,"count")); evidenceCount += count
                let average = Float64.parse(rowText(row,"average"))
                let item = JsonObject(); item.put("date",JsonString(rowText(row,"day")))
                item.put("score",JsonFloat(Float64(Int64(average*1000.0+0.5))/10.0)); item.put("evidence_count",JsonInt(count)); trend.getItems().add(item)
            }
            let summary = JsonObject(); summary.put("knowledge_count",JsonInt(mastery.getItems().size)); summary.put("misconception_count",JsonInt(mistakes.getItems().size)); summary.put("evidence_count",JsonInt(evidenceCount))
            let result = JsonObject(); result.put("mastery",mastery); result.put("misconceptions",mistakes); result.put("trend",trend); result.put("summary",summary)
            return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

## GET /api/v1/analytics/cognition-graph

- 新接口映射 `/api/v1/analytics/cognition-graph`，方法保持 GET
- 注册证据 `backend-cangjie/src/main.cj:36`，处理函数 `readCognitionGraph`
- 认证 Bearer 令牌，currentUser 校验用户及 credential
- 权限 遵循处理函数及所有权 helper，学生业务按 user_id/owner 限定

- 请求路径参数 无
- 请求 JSON 直接字段 以以下源代码读取行为为准，可能无 JSON 或使用 multipart
- 响应结构直接构建字段 categories, confidence, days, evidence_count, filters, links, mapped_question_count, nodes, personal_mapping_count, subject
- 调用闭包 `academicSummary`, `canonicalMistakeLabel`, `close`, `cognitionPercent`, `connect`, `constantEqual`, `controlTransaction`, `credentialTag`, `currentUser`, `decodeBase64`, `editNumber`, `fromEnvironment`, `graphTopic`, `hmac256`, `insightDays`, `insightLearnerText`, `insightPair`, `insightQuery`, `json`, `jsonText`, `label`, `learningMethodLabel`, `limit`, `query`, `queryLast`, `questionDisplayKey`, `readCognitionGraph`, `readToken`, `requireMethod`, `rowJson`, `rowText`, `shortTitle`, `stripWhitespace`, `text`, `transaction`, `tutorAnswerText`, `tutorArray`, `tutorNumber`, `tutorText`, `unixNow`, `unpaddedBase64`, `urlBase64`, `visible`, `zw_equal`, `zw_hmac`, `zw_media_close`, `zw_mysql_transaction`, `zw_stmt_affected`, `zw_stmt_bind`, `zw_stmt_close`, `zw_stmt_column_name`, `zw_stmt_columns`, `zw_stmt_copy`, `zw_stmt_error`, `zw_stmt_execute`, `zw_stmt_length`, `zw_stmt_next`, `zw_stmt_param_count`, `zw_stmt_prepare`, `zw_stmt_value`

以下保留完整处理函数实现，包含分支参数、请求对象读取、验证、返回构造与错误规则。共享 helper 的 SQL、返回键及验证证据在功能清单逐函数索引。字段精确类型还需结合数据库报告中的 column_types 与算法 Pydantic 模型。

```cangjie

func readCognitionGraph(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    let form=insightQuery(context); let days=insightDays(form,90,3650); let subject=queryLast(form,"subject")
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let allUpdates=tx.query("SELECT * FROM cognitive_updates WHERE user_id=? AND created_at>=TIMESTAMPADD(DAY,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)) AND entity_type<>'note_evidence' ORDER BY created_at,id",[user["id"],Some((-days).toString())]).rows
            let allMappings=tx.query("SELECT * FROM user_question_knowledge_links WHERE user_id=? AND created_at>=TIMESTAMPADD(DAY,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)) ORDER BY created_at,id",[user["id"],Some((-days).toString())]).rows
            let referenced=HashSet<String>()
            for (row in allUpdates) { if (let Some(id) <- row["question_id"]) { if (!id.isEmpty()) { referenced.add(id) } } }
            for (row in allMappings) { referenced.add(rowText(row,"question_id")) }
            let questions=HashMap<String,JsonObject>(); let seen=HashSet<String>()
            for (row in tx.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE user_id=? AND status<>'archived' ORDER BY updated_at DESC,id DESC",[user["id"]]).rows) {
                let id=rowText(row,"id"); if (!referenced.contains(id)) { continue }
                let key=questionDisplayKey(rowText(row,"question_text"),id)
                if (seen.contains(key)) { continue }; seen.add(key)
                if (subject.isEmpty() || rowText(row,"subject")==subject) { questions[id]=rowJson("questions",row) }
            }
            let updates=ArrayList<JsonObject>(); let mappings=ArrayList<JsonObject>(); let mappedQuestions=HashSet<String>()
            for (row in allUpdates) {
                let id=row["question_id"] ?? ""
                if (id.isEmpty() || questions.contains(id)) { updates.add(rowJson("cognitive_updates",row)) }
            }
            for (row in allMappings) {
                let id=rowText(row,"question_id")
                if (subject.isEmpty() || questions.contains(id)) { mappings.add(rowJson("user_question_knowledge_links",row)); mappedQuestions.add(id) }
            }
            let cardsById=HashMap<String,JsonObject>(); let cardsByLabel=HashMap<String,JsonObject>()
            for (row in tx.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_points ORDER BY id").rows) {
                let card=rowJson("knowledge_points",row); let cardSubject=jsonText(card,"subject")
                if (!subject.isEmpty() && cardSubject!=subject) { continue }
                cardsById[jsonText(card,"id")]=card
                cardsByLabel[insightPair(cardSubject,jsonText(card,"name"))]=card
                cardsByLabel[insightPair(cardSubject,jsonText(card,"code"))]=card
            }
            let topics=HashMap<String,String>()
            for (update in updates) {
                let id=tutorText(update,"question_id"); let key=tutorText(update,"entity_key")
                if (!id.isEmpty() && tutorText(update,"entity_type")=="mastery" && !key.isEmpty() && !topics.contains(id)) { topics[id]=key }
            }
            for (mapping in mappings) {
                if (let Some(card) <- cardsById.get(jsonText(mapping,"knowledge_point_id"))) {
                    let id=jsonText(mapping,"question_id")
                    if (jsonText(mapping,"role")=="primary" || !topics.contains(id)) { topics[id]=jsonText(card,"name") }
                }
            }
            let graph=CognitionGraph(); let mistakesByCode=HashMap<String,String>()
            for (row in tx.query("SELECT * FROM mastery_states WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY updated_at,id",[user["id"]]).rows) {
                let item=rowJson("mastery_states",row); let rowSubject=jsonText(item,"subject"); let point=jsonText(item,"knowledge_point")
                if (!subject.isEmpty() && rowSubject!=subject) { continue }
                let card=cardsByLabel.get(insightPair(rowSubject,point))
                if (!subject.isEmpty() && card.isNone()) { continue }
                let key=if (let Some(found) <- card) { jsonText(found,"code") } else { point }
                let node=CognitionNode("knowledge:${rowSubject}:${key}",point,CognitionKind.Knowledge,cognitionPercent(editNumber(item["mastery_score"])))
                node.text("subject",rowSubject); node.text("detail",insightLearnerText(tutorText(item,"update_reason"))); node.value.put("confidence",item["confidence"]); graph.nodes[node.id]=node
            }
            for (row in tx.query("SELECT * FROM misconception_states WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY probability DESC,id",[user["id"]]).rows) {
                let item=rowJson("misconception_states",row); let rowSubject=jsonText(item,"subject")
                if (!subject.isEmpty() && rowSubject!=subject) { continue }
                let label=canonicalMistakeLabel(jsonText(item,"label")); let id="misconception:${rowSubject}:${label}"
                mistakesByCode[insightPair(rowSubject,jsonText(item,"code"))]=id
                if (graph.nodes.contains(id)) { continue }
                let node=CognitionNode(id,label,CognitionKind.Misconception,cognitionPercent(editNumber(item["probability"])))
                node.text("subject",rowSubject); node.text("status",jsonText(item,"status"))
                node.text("detail","已参考 ${tutorNumber(item,'evidence_count')} 次学习记录；有效方法："+learningMethodLabel(tutorText(item,"successful_strategy")))
                graph.nodes[id]=node
                let strategies=HashSet<String>()
                for (field in ["successful_strategy","failed_strategy"]) {
                    let strategy=tutorText(item,field)
                    if (strategy.isEmpty() || strategies.contains(strategy)) { continue }; strategies.add(strategy)
                    let strategyNode=CognitionNode("strategy:${strategy}",learningMethodLabel(strategy),CognitionKind.Strategy,35)
                    strategyNode.text("detail","学习过程中使用过的方法"); graph.nodes[strategyNode.id]=strategyNode
                    graph.connect(id,strategyNode.id,if (strategy==tutorText(item,"successful_strategy")) { "有效策略" } else { "待改进策略" })
                }
            }
            for ((id,question) in questions) {
                let topic=topics.get(id) ?? graphTopic(question)
                let title=if (topic=="错题学习" || topic=="其他题型") { "相关错题" } else { topic+"相关错题" }
                let node=CognitionNode("question:${id}",title,CognitionKind.Question,28)
                node.text("subject",jsonText(question,"subject")); node.text("detail",academicSummary(jsonText(question,"question_text"),120)); node.text("question_id",id); graph.nodes[node.id]=node
            }
            for (mapping in mappings) {
                let questionNode="question:"+jsonText(mapping,"question_id")
                if (!graph.nodes.contains(questionNode)) { continue }
                if (let Some(card) <- cardsById.get(jsonText(mapping,"knowledge_point_id"))) {
                    let id="knowledge:"+jsonText(card,"subject")+":"+jsonText(card,"code")
                    if (!graph.nodes.contains(id)) {
                        let node=CognitionNode(id,jsonText(card,"name"),CognitionKind.Knowledge,45)
                        node.text("subject",jsonText(card,"subject")); node.text("detail","由你的错题映射到共享知识点；依据："+tutorText(mapping,"evidence_text")); node.value.put("confidence",mapping["confidence"]); graph.nodes[id]=node
                    }
                    graph.connect(questionNode,id,if (jsonText(mapping,"role")=="primary") { "涉及知识点" } else { "需要先修" })
                }
            }
            for (update in updates) {
                let questionId=tutorText(update,"question_id"); let questionNode="question:${questionId}"
                if (let Some(question) <- questions.get(questionId)) {
                    let rowSubject=jsonText(question,"subject"); let key=jsonText(update,"entity_key")
                    if (jsonText(update,"entity_type")=="mastery") {
                        for ((id,node) in graph.nodes) {
                            if (node.kind.label()=="knowledge" && tutorText(node.value,"subject")==rowSubject && jsonText(node.value,"name")==key) { graph.connect(questionNode,id,"提供掌握证据"); break }
                        }
                    } else if (jsonText(update,"entity_type")=="misconception") {
                        if (let Some(target) <- mistakesByCode.get(insightPair(rowSubject,key))) { graph.connect(questionNode,target,"暴露错因") }
                    }
                }
            }
            for (row in tx.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_relations ORDER BY id").rows) {
                if (let Some(source) <- cardsById.get(rowText(row,"source_id"))) {
                    if (let Some(target) <- cardsById.get(rowText(row,"target_id"))) {
                        graph.connect("knowledge:"+jsonText(source,"subject")+":"+jsonText(source,"code"),"knowledge:"+jsonText(target,"subject")+":"+jsonText(target,"code"),rowText(row,"relation_type"))
                    }
                }
            }
            let (nodes,links)=graph.visible(); let filters=JsonObject()
            filters.put("subject",if (form.getAll("subject").isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { JsonString(subject) }); filters.put("days",JsonInt(days))
            let categories=JsonArray(); for (kind in [CognitionKind.Knowledge,CognitionKind.Misconception,CognitionKind.Question,CognitionKind.Strategy]) { categories.getItems().add(JsonString(kind.label())) }
            let result=JsonObject(); result.put("nodes",nodes); result.put("links",links); result.put("categories",categories); result.put("filters",filters)
            result.put("evidence_count",JsonInt(updates.size)); result.put("personal_mapping_count",JsonInt(mappings.size)); result.put("mapped_question_count",JsonInt(mappedQuestions.size))
            return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

- 响应结构直接构建字段 由返回 helper/数据库行构建，见调用闭包与 rowJson 类型字典
- 调用闭包 `fromEnvironment`, `insightQuery`, `knowledgeCards`, `query`, `queryLast`, `requireMethod`, `rowJson`, `rowText`, `runeCount`, `tutorAnswerText`, `tutorObject`, `zw_stmt_affected`, `zw_stmt_bind`, `zw_stmt_close`, `zw_stmt_column_name`, `zw_stmt_columns`, `zw_stmt_copy`, `zw_stmt_error`, `zw_stmt_execute`, `zw_stmt_length`, `zw_stmt_next`, `zw_stmt_param_count`, `zw_stmt_prepare`, `zw_stmt_value`

以下保留完整处理函数实现，包含分支参数、请求对象读取、验证、返回构造与错误规则。共享 helper 的 SQL、返回键及验证证据在功能清单逐函数索引。字段精确类型还需结合数据库报告中的 column_types 与算法 Pydantic 模型。

```cangjie

func knowledgeCards(context: HttpContext): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    let form = insightQuery(context); let subject = queryLast(form,"subject"); let grade = queryLast(form,"grade"); let misconception = queryLast(form,"misconception")
    let words = ArrayList<String>(); let word = StringBuilder()
    // Match Python Unicode split, including Chinese separators.
    for (rune in queryLast(form,"q").runes()) {
        let one = StringBuilder(); one.append(rune); let character = one.toString()
        if (pythonWhitespace.contains(character) || character == "，" || character == "、") {
            if (runeCount(word.toString()) >= 2 && words.size < 5) { words.add(word.toString()) }; word.reset()
        } else { word.append(rune) }
    }
    if (runeCount(word.toString()) >= 2 && words.size < 5) { words.add(word.toString()) }
    let sql = StringBuilder(); sql.append("SELECT * FROM knowledge_points WHERE 1=1")
    let params = ArrayList<Option<String>>()
    if (!subject.isEmpty()) { sql.append(" AND subject=?"); params.add(Some(subject)) }
    if (!words.isEmpty()) {
        sql.append(" AND (")
        for (index in 0..words.size) {
            if (index > 0) { sql.append(" OR ") }
            sql.append("(name LIKE ? OR code LIKE ? OR CAST(content AS CHAR) LIKE ?)")
            for (_ in 0..3) { params.add(Some("%"+words[index]+"%")) }
        }
        sql.append(")")
    }
    if (!grade.isEmpty()) { sql.append(" AND LOCATE(?,JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(content,'$.grade'))) > 0"); params.add(Some(grade)) }
    if (!misconception.isEmpty()) { sql.append(" AND LOCATE(?,JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(content,'$.common_misconceptions'))) > 0"); params.add(Some(misconception)) }
    sql.append(" ORDER BY subject,code LIMIT 50")
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let result = JsonArray()
        for (row in db.query(sql.toString(),params.toArray()).rows) {
            let item = rowJson("knowledge_points",row); let content = tutorObject(item,"content")
            if (!grade.isEmpty() && !tutorAnswerText(content.get("grade") ?? JsonString("")).contains(grade)) { continue }
            if (!misconception.isEmpty()) {
                let labels = StringBuilder()
                if (let Some(array) <- ((content.get("common_misconceptions") ?? JsonArray()) as JsonArray)) {
                    for (label in array.getItems()) { if (!labels.toString().isEmpty()) { labels.append(" ") }; labels.append(tutorAnswerText(label)) }
                }
                if (!labels.toString().contains(misconception)) { continue }
            }
            result.getItems().add(item)
        }
        return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

- 响应结构直接构建字段 chapter, code, default_grade, default_subject, grade, mastery, nodes, prerequisite_code, question_count, relations
- 调用闭包 `constantEqual`, `credentialTag`, `currentUser`, `decodeBase64`, `fromEnvironment`, `hmac256`, `insightPair`, `jsonText`, `personalKnowledgeFramework`, `query`, `readToken`, `requireMethod`, `rowJson`, `rowText`, `tutorObject`, `tutorText`, `unixNow`, `unpaddedBase64`, `urlBase64`, `zw_equal`, `zw_hmac`, `zw_stmt_affected`, `zw_stmt_bind`, `zw_stmt_close`, `zw_stmt_column_name`, `zw_stmt_columns`, `zw_stmt_copy`, `zw_stmt_error`, `zw_stmt_execute`, `zw_stmt_length`, `zw_stmt_next`, `zw_stmt_param_count`, `zw_stmt_prepare`, `zw_stmt_value`

以下保留完整处理函数实现，包含分支参数、请求对象读取、验证、返回构造与错误规则。共享 helper 的 SQL、返回键及验证证据在功能清单逐函数索引。字段精确类型还需结合数据库报告中的 column_types 与算法 Pydantic 模型。

```cangjie

func personalKnowledgeFramework(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let nodes=JsonArray(); let edges=JsonArray(); let ids=HashMap<String,String>()
        let counts=HashMap<String,Int64>()
        for (row in db.query("SELECT l.knowledge_point_id,COUNT(DISTINCT l.question_id) AS total FROM user_question_knowledge_links l JOIN questions q ON q.id=l.question_id AND q.user_id=l.user_id WHERE l.user_id=? AND q.status<>'archived' GROUP BY l.knowledge_point_id",[user["id"]]).rows) { counts[rowText(row,"knowledge_point_id")]=Int64.parse(rowText(row,"total")) }
        let mastery=HashMap<String,JsonObject>()
        for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM mastery_states WHERE user_id=?",[user["id"]]).rows) {
            let value=rowJson("mastery_states",row); mastery[insightPair(jsonText(value,"subject"),jsonText(value,"knowledge_point"))]=value
        }
        for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_points ORDER BY subject,code").rows) {
            let card=rowJson("knowledge_points",row); let content=tutorObject(card,"content"); let node=JsonObject(); let id=jsonText(card,"id"); let code=jsonText(card,"code")
            ids[id]=code
            for (key in ["code","subject","name"]) { node.put(key,card[key]) }
            node.put("grade",JsonString(tutorText(content,"grade"))); node.put("chapter",JsonString(tutorText(content,"chapter")))
            node.put("question_count",JsonInt(counts.get(id) ?? 0))
            node.put("mastery",if (let Some(state) <- mastery.get(insightPair(jsonText(card,"subject"),jsonText(card,"name")))) { state } else { JsonNull() })
            nodes.add(node)
        }
        for (row in db.query("SELECT source_id,target_id FROM knowledge_relations WHERE relation_type='prerequisite' ORDER BY source_id,target_id").rows) {
            if (let Some(source) <- ids.get(rowText(row,"source_id"))) {
                if (let Some(target) <- ids.get(rowText(row,"target_id"))) { let edge=JsonObject(); edge.put("code",JsonString(source)); edge.put("prerequisite_code",JsonString(target)); edges.add(edge) }
            }
        }
        let result=JsonObject(); result.put("nodes",nodes); result.put("relations",edges)
        let profiles=db.query("SELECT grade,preferences FROM student_profiles WHERE user_id=?",[user["id"]]).rows
        var defaultSubject="数学"; var defaultGrade=""
        if (!profiles.isEmpty()) {
            defaultGrade=rowText(profiles[0],"grade")
            let raw=rowText(profiles[0],"preferences")
            if (!raw.isEmpty()) {
                if (let Some(preferences) <- (JsonValue.fromStr(raw) as JsonObject)) {
                    let focus=tutorText(preferences,"focus_subject"); if (!focus.isEmpty()) { defaultSubject=focus }
                }
            }
        }
        result.put("default_subject",JsonString(defaultSubject)); result.put("default_grade",JsonString(defaultGrade)); return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"知识框架暂时无法读取")
}
```

```cangjie

func readKnowledgeCard(context: HttpContext): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/")
    let code = parts[parts.size-1]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_points WHERE code=? OR id=? LIMIT 1",[Some(code),Some(code)]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"知识卡不存在") }
            let result = rowJson("knowledge_points",rows[0]); let id = rows[0]["id"]
            for ((key,sql) in [("prerequisites","SELECT k.* FROM knowledge_relations r JOIN knowledge_points k ON k.id=r.target_id WHERE r.source_id=?"),("next_knowledge","SELECT k.* FROM knowledge_relations r JOIN knowledge_points k ON k.id=r.source_id WHERE r.target_id=?")]) {
                let values = JsonArray()
                for (row in tx.query(sql,[id]).rows) { values.getItems().add(rowJson("knowledge_points",row)) }
                result.put(key,values)
            }
            return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

func knowledgeLessons(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let code=parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        if (context.request.method=="GET") {
            let cards=lessonCards(db,code); let result=JsonArray()
            for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_lessons WHERE user_id=? AND knowledge_code=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 10",[user["id"],Some(code)]).rows) {
                let item=lessonRow(row); let record=JsonObject(); record.put("input_fingerprint",JsonString(rowText(row,"input_fingerprint")))
                item.put("source_current",JsonBool(lessonVersionMatches(record,cards,rowText(row,"prompt")))); result.add(item)
            }
            return result
        }
        requireMethod(context,"POST")
        let body=readJson(context); let prompt=stripWhitespace(optionalText(body,"prompt","",800)); let requestId=stripWhitespace(optionalText(body,"request_id","",80))
        if (requestId.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422,"缺少请求编号") }
        let cards=lessonCards(db,code); let sourceHash=lessonSourceVersion(cards); let fingerprint=sourceHash+":"+sha256(prompt.toArray())
        let previous=db.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_lessons WHERE user_id=? AND request_id=?",[user["id"],Some(requestId)]).rows
        if (!previous.isEmpty()) {
            let record=JsonObject(); record.put("input_fingerprint",JsonString(rowText(previous[0],"input_fingerprint")))
            if (rowText(previous[0],"knowledge_code")!=code || !lessonVersionMatches(record,cards,prompt)) { throw ApiError(409,"这次提问或知识内容已变化，请重新提问") }
            return lessonRow(previous[0])
        }
        let anchor=(cards.getItems()[0] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("knowledge_lesson")); input.put("subject",anchor["subject"]); input.put("grade",JsonString(tutorText(tutorObject(anchor,"content"),"grade"))); input.put("text",JsonString(prompt)); input.put("dialogue_cards",cards)
        let history=JsonArray(); let recent=db.query("SELECT prompt,result FROM knowledge_lessons WHERE user_id=? AND knowledge_code=? AND (input_fingerprint LIKE ? OR input_fingerprint LIKE ?) ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 3",[user["id"],Some(code),Some(sourceHash+":%"),Some(sha256(cards.toString().toArray())+":%")]).rows
        for (index in 0..recent.size) {
            let row=recent[recent.size-1-index]; let result=(JsonValue.fromStr(rowText(row,"result")) as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let turn=JsonObject(); turn.put("question",JsonString(rowText(row,"prompt"))); turn.put("explanation",JsonString(tutorText(result,"explanation"))); turn.put("check_question",JsonString(tutorText(result,"check_question"))); history.add(turn)
        }
        input.put("knowledge_history",history)
        let result=try { algorithmCompute(input,timeoutSeconds:30) } catch (_: ApiError) { throw ApiError(503,"讲解暂时没有完成，请稍后重试。可以先查看知识卡中的公式和示例。") }
        if ((result.get("can_update_learning") ?? JsonNull()).toString()!="false") { throw ApiError(503,"讲解结果未通过检查") }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let latest=lessonCards(tx,code)
            if (lessonSourceVersion(latest)!=sourceHash) { throw ApiError(409,"知识内容刚刚更新，请重新提问") }
            tx.query("INSERT INTO knowledge_lessons (id,user_id,knowledge_code,request_id,input_fingerprint,prompt,result,created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)) ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE id=id",[Some(newId()),user["id"],Some(code),Some(requestId),Some(fingerprint),Some(prompt),Some(result.toString())])
            let saved=tx.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_lessons WHERE user_id=? AND request_id=?",[user["id"],Some(requestId)]).rows[0]
            let savedRecord=JsonObject(); savedRecord.put("input_fingerprint",JsonString(rowText(saved,"input_fingerprint")))
            if (!lessonVersionMatches(savedRecord,latest,prompt) || rowText(saved,"knowledge_code")!=code) { throw ApiError(409,"请求编号已用于其他提问") }
            return lessonRow(saved)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"知识讲解暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

public func startRecognition(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts, synchronous: Bool): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context, "POST")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/")
    let id = parts[parts.size - 2]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context, db)
        let started = db.transaction<(JsonObject, Option<String>)>({ tx =>
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT status FROM questions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? FOR UPDATE", [Some(id), user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty() || rowText(rows[0], "status") == "archived") { throw ApiError(404, "题目不存在或已归档") }
            let active = tx.query("SELECT * FROM processing_jobs WHERE question_id = ? AND status IN ('pending','running') AND kind IN ('ingest','crop','ocr','reprocess') ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1", [Some(id)]).rows
            if (!active.isEmpty()) { return (rowJson("processing_jobs", active[0]), None) }
            let assets = tx.query("SELECT path FROM question_assets WHERE question_id = ? AND kind = 'crop' ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1", [Some(id)]).rows
            if (assets.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422, "请先框选题目，生成裁剪图片后再识别") }
            let jobId = newId()
            tx.query("INSERT INTO processing_jobs (id,question_id,kind,status,progress,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,'pending',0,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())", [Some(jobId), Some(id), Some(if (synchronous) { "ocr" } else { "reprocess" })])
            tx.query("UPDATE questions SET status = 'processing',updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?", [Some(id)])
            return (rowJson("processing_jobs", tx.query("SELECT * FROM processing_jobs WHERE id = ?", [Some(jobId)]).rows[0]), Some(rowText(assets[0], "path")))
        })
        let jobId = jsonText(started[0], "id")
        if (let Some(path) <- started[1]) {
            if (synchronous) { runRecognition(jobId, id, path, requireCrop: true) }
            else { spawn { runRecognition(jobId, id, path, requireCrop: true) } }
        }
        let result = JsonObject()
        if (synchronous) {
            result.put("job", rowJson("processing_jobs", db.query("SELECT * FROM processing_jobs WHERE id = ?", [Some(jobId)]).rows[0]))
            result.put("question", questionDetail(context, accounts, id))
        } else { result.put("job_id", JsonString(jobId)) }
        if (started[1].isNone()) { result.put("resumed", JsonBool(true)) }
        return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503, "数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie
            let active = tx.query("SELECT * FROM processing_jobs WHERE question_id = ? AND status IN ('pending','running') AND kind IN ('ingest','crop','ocr','reprocess') ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1", [Some(id)]).rows

            tx.query("UPDATE questions SET status = 'processing',updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?", [Some(id)])
```
