```cangjie

public func cropQuestion(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context, "POST")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/")
    let id = parts[parts.size - 2]
    let files = ArrayList<String>()
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context, db)
        let box = readCropBox(readJson(context))
        let result = db.transaction<(JsonObject, Option<String>)>({ tx =>
            let questions = tx.query("SELECT status FROM questions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? FOR UPDATE", [Some(id), user["id"]]).rows
            if (questions.isEmpty() || rowText(questions[0], "status") == "archived") { throw ApiError(404, "题目不存在或已归档") }
            let active = tx.query("SELECT id,status FROM processing_jobs WHERE question_id = ? AND kind IN ('ingest','crop','ocr','reprocess') AND status IN ('pending','running') ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1", [Some(id)]).rows
            let old = tx.query("SELECT * FROM question_assets WHERE question_id = ? AND kind = 'crop' ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1", [Some(id)]).rows
            if (!active.isEmpty()) {
                if (old.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(409, "已有识别任务正在处理，请先查看结果再调整范围") }
                let asset = rowJson("question_assets", old[0])
                let list = JsonArray(); list.getItems().add(asset)
                let stored = assetCrop(list)
                if (!(stored is JsonArray)) { throw ApiError(409, "已有识别任务正在处理") }
                let values = (stored as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()
                for (index in 0..4) {
                    let difference = Float64.parse(values[index].toString()) - box[index]
                    if (difference > 0.000001 || difference < -0.000001) { throw ApiError(409, "已有识别任务正在处理，请先查看结果再调整范围") }
                }
                asset.put("url", JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl("/" + rowText(old[0], "path"))))
                asset.put("job_id", JsonString(rowText(active[0], "id")))
                asset.put("status", JsonString(rowText(active[0], "status")))
                asset.put("resumed", JsonBool(true)); asset.put("crop_box", stored)
                return (asset, None)
            }
            let originals = tx.query("SELECT path FROM question_assets WHERE question_id = ? AND kind = 'original' ORDER BY created_at,id LIMIT 1", [Some(id)]).rows
            if (originals.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404, "原图不存在") }
            let input = JsonObject()
            input.put("operation", JsonString("crop")); input.put("box", cropNumbers(box))
            input.put("image_base64", JsonString(toBase64String(readMedia(rowText(originals[0], "path")))))
            let computed = algorithmCompute(input)
            if (jsonText(computed, "kind") != "crop") { throw ApiError(503, "裁剪结果无效") }
            let path = "uploads/${id}/crops/" + newId() + ".jpg"
            writeMedia(path, algorithmImage(computed)); files.add(path)
            let right = if (box[0] + box[2] > 1.0) { 1.0 } else { box[0] + box[2] }
            let bottom = if (box[1] + box[3] > 1.0) { 1.0 } else { box[1] + box[3] }
            let bbox = cropNumbers([box[0], box[1], right, bottom]).toString()
            let assetId = if (old.isEmpty()) { newId() } else { rowText(old[0], "id") }
            if (old.isEmpty()) {
                tx.query("INSERT INTO question_assets (id,question_id,kind,path,sha256,bbox,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'crop',?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())", [Some(assetId), Some(id), Some(path), Some(jsonText(computed, "sha256")), Some(bbox)])
            } else {
                tx.query("UPDATE question_assets SET path = ?,sha256 = ?,bbox = ?,updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?", [Some(path), Some(jsonText(computed, "sha256")), Some(bbox), Some(assetId)])
            }
            let jobId = newId()
            tx.query("INSERT INTO processing_jobs (id,question_id,kind,status,progress,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'crop','pending',0,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())", [Some(jobId), Some(id)])
            tx.query("UPDATE questions SET status = 'processing',updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?", [Some(id)])
            let asset = rowJson("question_assets", tx.query("SELECT * FROM question_assets WHERE id = ?", [Some(assetId)]).rows[0])
            asset.put("url", JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl("/" + path)))
            asset.put("geometry", computed["geometry"]); asset.put("original_preserved", JsonBool(true))
            asset.put("job_id", JsonString(jobId)); asset.put("status", JsonString("pending"))
            return (asset, Some(path))
        })
        files.clear()
        if (let Some(path) <- result[1]) {
            let jobId = jsonText(result[0], "job_id")
            spawn { runRecognition(jobId, id, path, requireCrop: true) }
        }
        return result[0]
    } finally { for (path in files) { try { removeMedia(path) } catch (_: Exception) {} } }
    throw ApiError(503, "数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

```cangjie
public func saveOcrEdits(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts, confirm: Bool): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context, "POST")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/")
    let id = parts[parts.size - 2]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context, db)
        let body = readJson(context, maxBytes: 8 * 1024 * 1024, defaultEmpty: true)
        let validated = validateQuestionEdit(body)
        db.transaction<Unit>({ tx =>
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? FOR UPDATE", [Some(id), user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404, "题目不存在或无权访问") }
            if (rowText(rows[0], "status") == "archived") { throw ApiError(409, "题目已归档，不能修改") }
            let existing = rowJson("questions", rows[0])
            let edits = JsonObject()
            for (key in ["question_text","subject","formulas","options","question_type","knowledge_points"]) {
                if (let Some(value) <- validated.get(key)) { edits.put(key, value) }
            }
            let steps = manualArray(body, "student_steps", 100)
            var answer = ""
            if (let Some(value) <- validated.get("student_answer")) {
                if (let Some(text) <- (value as JsonString)) { answer = stripWhitespace(text.getValue()) }
            }
            let typeName = ((edits.get("question_type") ?? existing["question_type"]) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
            var normalizedSteps = steps
            if (answer.isEmpty() && (typeName.contains("选择") || typeName.toAsciiLower().contains("choice") || typeName.toAsciiLower().contains("multiple"))) {
                if (let Some(inferred) <- choiceStepAnswer(steps, edits.get("options") ?? existing["options"])) {
                    answer = inferred; normalizedSteps = JsonArray()
                }
            }
            if (validated.get("student_answer").isSome()) { edits.put("student_answer", if (answer.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { JsonString(answer) }) }
            if (let Some(value) <- validated.get("grade")) {
                edits.put("grade", if (let Some(text) <- (value as JsonString)) { if (text.getValue().isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { JsonString(stripWhitespace(text.getValue())) } } else { JsonNull() })
            }
            var changed = false
            for ((key,value) in edits.getFields()) { if (!jsonEquivalent(existing[key],value)) { changed = true } }
            if (body.get("student_steps").isSome()) {
                let previous = tx.query("SELECT s.text FROM student_steps s WHERE s.work_id = (SELECT id FROM student_works WHERE question_id = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1) ORDER BY s.step_index", [Some(id)]).rows
                if (previous.size != normalizedSteps.getItems().size) { changed = true }
                else { for (index in 0..previous.size) { if (rowText(previous[index],"text") != stepText(normalizedSteps[index])) { changed = true } } }
                let work = newId()
                let currentAnswer = edits.get("student_answer") ?? existing["student_answer"]
                tx.query("INSERT INTO student_works (id,question_id,user_id,answer,explanation,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,'',UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))", [Some(work),Some(id),user["id"],Some(if (let Some(text) <- (currentAnswer as JsonString)) { text.getValue() } else { "" })])
                for (index in 0..normalizedSteps.getItems().size) {
                    let object = stepObject(normalizedSteps[index],false)
                    let text = stepText(object)
                    let raw = ((object.get("raw_text") ?? JsonString(text)) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
                    let bbox = editedBbox(object)
                    let confidence = editNumber(object.get("confidence") ?? JsonInt(1))
                    let source = ((object.get("source") ?? JsonString("user")) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
                    tx.query("INSERT INTO student_steps (id,work_id,step_index,text,bbox,raw_text,normalized_text,formula,confidence,source,user_confirmed,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,1,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))", [Some(newId()),Some(work),Some(index.toString()),Some(text),Some(bbox.toString()),Some(raw),Some(collapseWhitespace(text)),Some(if (text.contains("=")) { text } else { "" }),Some(confidence.toString()),Some(source)])
                }
            }
            if (body.get("teacher_marks").isSome()) {
                tx.query("DELETE FROM teacher_marks WHERE question_id = ?", [Some(id)])
                for (item in manualArray(body,"teacher_marks",100).getItems()) {
                    let object = stepObject(item,true)
                    tx.query("INSERT INTO teacher_marks (id,question_id,mark_type,comment,bbox,confidence,source,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,'user',UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())", [Some(newId()),Some(id),Some(((object.get("mark_type") ?? JsonString("comment")) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()),Some(((object.get("comment") ?? JsonString("")) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()),Some(editedBbox(object).toString()),Some(editNumber(object.get("confidence") ?? JsonInt(1)).toString())])
                }
            }
            let text = ((edits.get("question_text") ?? existing["question_text"]) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
            if (confirm && stripWhitespace(text).isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422, "请先补充题目文字再确认") }
            tx.query("UPDATE processing_jobs SET status = 'cancelled',error = '题目已人工核对，旧识别结果未应用',updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE question_id = ? AND kind IN ('ingest','crop','ocr','reprocess') AND status IN ('pending','running')", [Some(id)])
            if (changed) { invalidateQuestionLearning(tx,id) }
            let sql = StringBuilder(); let params = ArrayList<Option<String>>()
            sql.append("UPDATE questions SET updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP(),status = ?")
            params.add(Some(if (confirm) { "confirmed" } else { "ocr_draft" }))
            if (confirm) { sql.append(",confirmed = 1") }
            for ((key,value) in edits.getFields()) {
                sql.append(", `${key}` = ?")
                params.add(if (value is JsonNull) { None } else if (let Some(textValue) <- (value as JsonString)) { Some(textValue.getValue()) } else { Some(value.toString()) })
            }
            sql.append(" WHERE id = ?"); params.add(Some(id)); tx.query(sql.toString(),params.toArray())
            ()
        })
        return questionDetail(context,accounts,id)
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

- 请求 JSON 直接字段 以以下源代码读取行为为准，可能无 JSON 或使用 multipart

- 调用闭包 `algorithmCompute`, `analysisFingerprint`, `analysisSnapshot`, `analyzeQuestion`, `check`, `close`, `constantEqual`, `controlTransaction`, `credentialTag`, `currentUser`, `decodeBase64`, `editNumber`, `firstJson`, `fromEnvironment`, `hmac256`, `invalidateQuestionLearning`, `isClosed`, `jsonEquivalent`, `jsonText`, `knowledgeLabel`, `mediaRoot`, `newId`, `query`, `queryArray`, `randomBytes`, `read`, `readAt`, `readMedia`, `readToken`, `remaining`, `requireMethod`, `rowJson`, `rowText`, `runeCount`, `shortTitle`, `stripWhitespace`, `syncKnowledgeLinks`, `transaction`, `unixNow`, `unpaddedBase64`, `urlBase64`, `validStoragePath`, `zw_equal`, `zw_hmac`, `zw_media_close`, `zw_media_open`, `zw_media_read`, `zw_media_stat`, `zw_mysql_transaction`, `zw_random`, `zw_stmt_affected`, `zw_stmt_bind`, `zw_stmt_close`, `zw_stmt_column_name`, `zw_stmt_columns`, `zw_stmt_copy`, `zw_stmt_error`, `zw_stmt_execute`, `zw_stmt_length`, `zw_stmt_next`, `zw_stmt_param_count`, `zw_stmt_prepare`, `zw_stmt_value`

```cangjie

public func analyzeQuestion(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/")
    let id = parts[parts.size - 2]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        let snapshot = db.transaction<JsonObject>({ tx => analysisSnapshot(tx,id,user["id"]) })
        let expected = analysisFingerprint(snapshot)
        let question = (snapshot["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        let input = JsonObject()
        input.put("operation",JsonString("analyze")); input.put("text",question["question_text"])
        for (key in ["subject","question_type","student_answer","options"]) { input.put(key,question[key]) }
        input.put("grade",if (question["grade"] is JsonNull) { JsonString("") } else { question["grade"] })
        let steps = JsonArray()
        for (item in (snapshot["steps"] as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()) { steps.getItems().add((item as JsonObject).getOrThrow()["text"]) }
        input.put("student_steps",steps)
        let images = JsonArray(); let seen = HashSet<String>()
        for (item in (snapshot["assets"] as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()) {
            let path = jsonText((item as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),"path")
            if (!seen.contains(path)) { images.getItems().add(JsonString(toBase64String(readMedia(path)))); seen.add(path) }
        }
        input.put("images_base64",images)
        let computed = algorithmCompute(input)
        let analysis = (computed["analysis"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        let references = (analysis["reference_steps"] as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()
        let valid = (computed["reference_math_valid"] as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()
        if (valid.size != references.size || stripWhitespace(jsonText(analysis,"final_answer")).isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(503,"分析结果不完整") }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let current = analysisSnapshot(tx,id,user["id"])
            if (!jsonEquivalent(analysisFingerprint(current), expected)) { throw ApiError(409,"题目或作答已变化，请重新分析") }
            invalidateQuestionLearning(tx,id)
            let analysisId = newId()
            tx.query("INSERT INTO problem_analyses (id,question_id,subject,problem_type,known_conditions,goal,knowledge_points,prerequisites,final_answer,verified,raw_result,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())", [Some(analysisId),Some(id),Some(jsonText(analysis,"subject")),Some(jsonText(analysis,"problem_type")),Some(analysis["known_conditions"].toString()),Some(jsonText(analysis,"goal")),Some(analysis["knowledge_points"].toString()),Some(analysis["prerequisites"].toString()),Some(jsonText(analysis,"final_answer")),Some(if (analysis["answer_verified"].toString() == "true") { "1" } else { "0" }),Some(analysis.toString())])
            for (index in 0..references.size) {
                let text = (references[index] as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
                let equation = text.contains("=")
                tx.query("INSERT INTO reference_steps (id,analysis_id,step_index,text,expression,explanation,math_valid,uses_condition,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())", [Some(newId()),Some(analysisId),Some(index.toString()),Some(text),if (equation) { Some(text) } else { None },Some(if (equation) { "由题目条件进行等价变形" } else { "推理步骤" }),Some(if (valid[index].toString() == "true") { "1" } else { "0" }),Some(if (index == 0) { "1" } else { "0" })])
            }
            syncKnowledgeLinks(tx,question,analysis)
            tx.query("UPDATE questions SET status = 'analyzed',updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?", [Some(id)])
            let result = rowJson("problem_analyses",tx.query("SELECT * FROM problem_analyses WHERE id = ?",[Some(analysisId)]).rows[0])
            result.put("reference_steps",analysis["reference_steps"])
            return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

```cangjie

public func submitStudentWork(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/")
    let id = parts[parts.size - 2]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        let body = readJson(context,maxBytes: 8 * 1024 * 1024)
        let answer = (editText(body.get("answer") ?? JsonString(""),12000) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
        let explanation = (editText(body.get("explanation") ?? JsonString(""),12000) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
        let steps = manualArray(body,"steps",100)
        for (step in steps.getItems()) { editText(step,12000) }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT status FROM questions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? FOR UPDATE",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"题目不存在或无权访问") }
            if (rowText(rows[0],"status") == "archived") { throw ApiError(409,"题目已归档，不能提交作答") }
            invalidateQuestionLearning(tx,id)
            tx.query("UPDATE processing_jobs SET status = 'cancelled',error = '已提交新作答，旧识别结果未应用',updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE question_id = ? AND kind IN ('ingest','crop','ocr','reprocess') AND status IN ('pending','running')",[Some(id)])
            tx.query("UPDATE questions SET student_answer = ?,status = IF(confirmed,'confirmed','needs_confirmation'),updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?",[if (answer.isEmpty()) { None } else { Some(answer) },Some(id)])
            let workId = newId()
            tx.query("INSERT INTO student_works (id,question_id,user_id,answer,explanation,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(workId),Some(id),user["id"],Some(answer),Some(explanation)])
            for (index in 0..steps.getItems().size) {
                let text = (steps[index] as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
                tx.query("INSERT INTO student_steps (id,work_id,step_index,text,bbox,raw_text,normalized_text,formula,confidence,source,user_confirmed,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,JSON_ARRAY(),?,?,?,1,'user',1,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(newId()),Some(workId),Some(index.toString()),Some(text),Some(text),Some(collapseWhitespace(text)),Some(if (text.contains("=")) { text } else { "" })])
            }
            return rowJson("student_works",tx.query("SELECT * FROM student_works WHERE id = ?",[Some(workId)]).rows[0])
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

- 调用闭包 `constantEqual`, `credentialTag`, `currentUser`, `decodeBase64`, `firstJson`, `fromEnvironment`, `hmac256`, `jsonText`, `listReviews`, `mediaUrl`, `memoryQuestionVersion`, `query`, `queryArray`, `questionDisplayKey`, `readToken`, `requireMethod`, `reviewAttemptReceipt`, `reviewQuestion`, `rowJson`, `rowText`, `sha256`, `sqlValue`, `tutorFlag`, `tutorObject`, `tutorText`, `unixNow`, `unpaddedBase64`, `urlBase64`, `zw_equal`, `zw_hmac`, `zw_sha256`, `zw_stmt_affected`, `zw_stmt_bind`, `zw_stmt_close`, `zw_stmt_column_name`, `zw_stmt_columns`, `zw_stmt_copy`, `zw_stmt_error`, `zw_stmt_execute`, `zw_stmt_length`, `zw_stmt_next`, `zw_stmt_param_count`, `zw_stmt_prepare`, `zw_stmt_value`

```cangjie

public func listReviews(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts, history: Bool): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db); let result = JsonArray()
        if (history) {
            for (row in db.query("SELECT r.* FROM review_records r JOIN review_plans p ON p.id=r.plan_id WHERE p.user_id=? AND r.grade<>'pending' ORDER BY r.created_at DESC,r.id DESC",[user["id"]]).rows) {
                let record = rowJson("review_records",row); let plan = rowJson("review_plans",db.query("SELECT * FROM review_plans WHERE id=?",[row["plan_id"]]).rows[0])
                let questions = db.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id=? AND user_id=?",[sqlValue(plan["question_id"]),user["id"]]).rows
                let details = tutorObject(record,"grading_details"); let rawPath = tutorText(details,"handwriting_image_url"); let path = if (rawPath.isEmpty()) { "" } else { rawPath.split("?",2)[0] }
                if (!path.isEmpty()) { details.put("handwriting_image_url",JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl(path))) }
                record.put("plan",plan); record.put("question",if (questions.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { rowJson("questions",questions[0]) }); result.add(record)
            }
        } else {
            let seen = HashSet<String>()
            for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM review_plans WHERE user_id=? AND status='pending' AND due_at<TIMESTAMPADD(HOUR,16,DATE(TIMESTAMPADD(HOUR,8,UTC_TIMESTAMP()))) ORDER BY due_at,id",[user["id"]]).rows) {
                let plan = rowJson("review_plans",row); let question = reviewQuestion(db,plan,rowText(user,"id"))
                if (jsonText(question,"status") == "archived") { continue }
                let key = questionDisplayKey(jsonText(question,"question_text"),jsonText(question,"id")); if (seen.contains(key)) { continue }; seen.add(key)
                let visible = JsonObject(); for ((name,value) in question.getFields()) { if (name != "student_answer") { visible.put(name,value) } }
                let figures = queryArray(db,"question_assets","SELECT * FROM question_assets WHERE question_id=? AND kind='question_figure' ORDER BY created_at,id",jsonText(question,"id"))
                for (value in figures.getItems()) { let figure = (value as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); figure.put("url",JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl("/"+jsonText(figure,"path")))) }
                visible.put("question_figures",figures); plan.put("question",visible)
                let version=memoryQuestionVersion(db,question); plan.put("source_version",JsonString(version)); plan.put("pending_attempt",JsonNull())
                let pending=db.query("SELECT * FROM review_records WHERE plan_id=? AND grade='pending' AND COALESCE(JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(grading_details,'$.review_record_state')),'current')<>'superseded' ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[row["id"]]).rows
                if (!pending.isEmpty()) {
                    let record=rowJson("review_records",pending[0])
                    if (tutorText(tutorObject(record,"grading_details"),"memory_question_version")==version) { plan.put("pending_attempt",reviewAttemptReceipt(record,accounts)) }
                }
                if (tutorText(plan,"reason").toAsciiLower().contains("again")) { plan.put("reason",JsonString("本次作答需要重做，安排 1 天后再次复习")) }
                result.add(plan)
            }
        }
        return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

- 响应结构直接构建字段 due_at, is_due, items, knowledge_points, page, page_size, total
- 调用闭包 `constantEqual`, `credentialTag`, `currentUser`, `decodeBase64`, `fromEnvironment`, `hmac256`, `insightQuery`, `query`, `queryLast`, `readToken`, `requireMethod`, `rowText`, `searchReviewQuestions`, `stripWhitespace`, `unixNow`, `unpaddedBase64`, `urlBase64`, `zw_equal`, `zw_hmac`, `zw_stmt_affected`, `zw_stmt_bind`, `zw_stmt_close`, `zw_stmt_column_name`, `zw_stmt_columns`, `zw_stmt_copy`, `zw_stmt_error`, `zw_stmt_execute`, `zw_stmt_length`, `zw_stmt_next`, `zw_stmt_param_count`, `zw_stmt_prepare`, `zw_stmt_value`

以下保留完整处理函数实现，包含分支参数、请求对象读取、验证、返回构造与错误规则。共享 helper 的 SQL、返回键及验证证据在功能清单逐函数索引。字段精确类型还需结合数据库报告中的 column_types 与算法 Pydantic 模型。

```cangjie
func searchReviewQuestions(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    let form=insightQuery(context); let query=stripWhitespace(queryLast(form,"q")); let subject=queryLast(form,"subject")
    let scope=if (queryLast(form,"scope").isEmpty()) { "pending" } else { queryLast(form,"scope") }
    if (query.size>300 || subject.size>100 || !["pending","today","completed"].contains(scope)) { throw ApiError(422,"复习筛选条件无效") }
    let page=if (queryLast(form,"page").isEmpty()) { 1 } else { try { Int64.parse(queryLast(form,"page")) } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422,"页码无效") } }
    if (page<1 || page>10000) { throw ApiError(422,"页码无效") }
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        let criteria=StringBuilder(); criteria.append(" FROM review_plans p JOIN questions q ON q.id=p.question_id AND q.user_id=p.user_id WHERE p.user_id=? AND q.status<>'archived'")
        let params=ArrayList<Option<String>>(); params.add(user["id"])
        criteria.append(if (scope=="completed") { " AND p.status='completed'" } else { " AND p.status='pending'" })
        if (scope=="today") { criteria.append(" AND p.due_at<TIMESTAMPADD(HOUR,16,DATE(TIMESTAMPADD(HOUR,8,UTC_TIMESTAMP())))") }
        if (!subject.isEmpty()) { criteria.append(" AND q.subject=?"); params.add(Some(subject)) }
        if (!query.isEmpty()) {
            criteria.append(" AND (LOCATE(?,q.question_text)>0 OR LOCATE(?,CAST(q.knowledge_points AS CHAR))>0 OR LOCATE(?,p.reason)>0 OR LOCATE(?,q.title)>0)")
            for (_ in 0..4) { params.add(Some(query)) }
        }
        let total=db.query("SELECT COUNT(*) AS total"+criteria.toString(),params.toArray()).rows[0]
        let items=JsonArray()
        for (row in db.query("SELECT p.id,p.question_id,p.due_at,p.reason,p.status,q.subject,q.grade,q.title,q.question_text,q.knowledge_points,(p.due_at<TIMESTAMPADD(HOUR,16,DATE(TIMESTAMPADD(HOUR,8,UTC_TIMESTAMP())))) AS is_due"+criteria.toString()+" ORDER BY p.due_at "+(if (scope=="completed") { "DESC" } else { "ASC" })+",p.id LIMIT 20 OFFSET "+((page-1)*20).toString(),params.toArray()).rows) {
            let item=JsonObject()
            for (key in ["id","question_id","due_at","reason","status","subject","grade","title","question_text"]) { item.put(key,JsonString(rowText(row,key))) }
            item.put("due_at",JsonString(rowText(row,"due_at").replace(" ","T")+"+00:00"))
            item.put("knowledge_points",JsonValue.fromStr(rowText(row,"knowledge_points"))); item.put("is_due",JsonBool(rowText(row,"is_due")=="1")); items.add(item)
        }
        let result=JsonObject(); result.put("items",items); result.put("total",JsonInt(Int64.parse(rowText(total,"total")))); result.put("page",JsonInt(page)); result.put("page_size",JsonInt(20)); return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"复习题目暂时无法读取")
}

```

```cangjie

func savedReviewFilters(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts, deleting: Bool): JsonValue {
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        if (deleting) {
            requireMethod(context,"DELETE"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-1]
            if (db.query("SELECT id FROM saved_review_filters WHERE id=? AND user_id=?",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"复习范围不存在") }
            db.query("DELETE FROM saved_review_filters WHERE id=? AND user_id=?",[Some(id),user["id"]]); let result=JsonObject(); result.put("deleted",JsonBool(true)); return result
        }
        if (context.request.method=="POST") {
            let body=readJson(context); let name=stripWhitespace(optionalText(body,"name","",80)); let query=stripWhitespace(optionalText(body,"q","",300)); let subject=optionalText(body,"subject","",30); let scope=optionalText(body,"scope","pending",20)
            if (name.isEmpty() || !["pending","today","completed"].contains(scope)) { throw ApiError(422,"请填写名称并选择有效的复习范围") }
            db.transaction<Unit>({ tx =>
                tx.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE id=? FOR UPDATE",[user["id"]])
                let existing=tx.query("SELECT id FROM saved_review_filters WHERE user_id=? AND name=? AND query_text=? AND subject=? AND scope=?",[user["id"],Some(name),Some(query),Some(subject),Some(scope)]).rows
                if (existing.isEmpty()) {
                    if (Int64.parse(rowText(tx.query("SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM saved_review_filters WHERE user_id=?",[user["id"]]).rows[0],"total"))>=20) { throw ApiError(409,"最多保存20个复习范围，请先移除不再使用的范围") }
                    tx.query("INSERT INTO saved_review_filters (id,user_id,name,query_text,subject,scope,created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),user["id"],Some(name),Some(query),Some(subject),Some(scope)])
                }
                ()
            })
        } else { requireMethod(context,"GET") }
        let result=JsonArray()
        for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM saved_review_filters WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC",[user["id"]]).rows) {
            let item=JsonObject(); for (key in ["id","name","subject","scope"]) { item.put(key,JsonString(rowText(row,key))) }; item.put("q",JsonString(rowText(row,"query_text"))); result.add(item)
        }
        return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"复习范围暂时无法保存")
}

```

```cangjie

public func listReviews(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts, history: Bool): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db); let result = JsonArray()
        if (history) {
            for (row in db.query("SELECT r.* FROM review_records r JOIN review_plans p ON p.id=r.plan_id WHERE p.user_id=? AND r.grade<>'pending' ORDER BY r.created_at DESC,r.id DESC",[user["id"]]).rows) {
                let record = rowJson("review_records",row); let plan = rowJson("review_plans",db.query("SELECT * FROM review_plans WHERE id=?",[row["plan_id"]]).rows[0])
                let questions = db.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id=? AND user_id=?",[sqlValue(plan["question_id"]),user["id"]]).rows
                let details = tutorObject(record,"grading_details"); let rawPath = tutorText(details,"handwriting_image_url"); let path = if (rawPath.isEmpty()) { "" } else { rawPath.split("?",2)[0] }
                if (!path.isEmpty()) { details.put("handwriting_image_url",JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl(path))) }
                record.put("plan",plan); record.put("question",if (questions.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { rowJson("questions",questions[0]) }); result.add(record)
            }
        } else {
            let seen = HashSet<String>()
            for (row in db.query("SELECT * FROM review_plans WHERE user_id=? AND status='pending' AND due_at<TIMESTAMPADD(HOUR,16,DATE(TIMESTAMPADD(HOUR,8,UTC_TIMESTAMP()))) ORDER BY due_at,id",[user["id"]]).rows) {
                let plan = rowJson("review_plans",row); let question = reviewQuestion(db,plan,rowText(user,"id"))
                if (jsonText(question,"status") == "archived") { continue }
                let key = questionDisplayKey(jsonText(question,"question_text"),jsonText(question,"id")); if (seen.contains(key)) { continue }; seen.add(key)
                let visible = JsonObject(); for ((name,value) in question.getFields()) { if (name != "student_answer") { visible.put(name,value) } }
                let figures = queryArray(db,"question_assets","SELECT * FROM question_assets WHERE question_id=? AND kind='question_figure' ORDER BY created_at,id",jsonText(question,"id"))
                for (value in figures.getItems()) { let figure = (value as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); figure.put("url",JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl("/"+jsonText(figure,"path")))) }
                visible.put("question_figures",figures); plan.put("question",visible)
                let version=memoryQuestionVersion(db,question); plan.put("source_version",JsonString(version)); plan.put("pending_attempt",JsonNull())
                let pending=db.query("SELECT * FROM review_records WHERE plan_id=? AND grade='pending' AND COALESCE(JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(grading_details,'$.review_record_state')),'current')<>'superseded' ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[row["id"]]).rows
                if (!pending.isEmpty()) {
                    let record=rowJson("review_records",pending[0])
                    if (tutorText(tutorObject(record,"grading_details"),"memory_question_version")==version) { plan.put("pending_attempt",reviewAttemptReceipt(record,accounts)) }
                }
                if (tutorText(plan,"reason").toAsciiLower().contains("again")) { plan.put("reason",JsonString("本次作答需要重做，安排 1 天后再次复习")) }
                result.add(plan)
            }
        }
        return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

public func attemptReview(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST"); let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id = parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body = readJson(context); let answer = editText(body.get("answer") ?? JsonString(""),12000,trim: true)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT * FROM review_plans WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND status='pending' FOR UPDATE",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"待复习题不存在") }
            let plan = rowJson("review_plans",rows[0]); let question = reviewQuestion(tx,plan,rowText(user,"id"))
            if (jsonText(question,"status") == "archived") { throw ApiError(409,"题目已归档") }
            let version=memoryQuestionVersion(tx,question); let suppliedVersion=tutorText(body,"source_version")
            if (!suppliedVersion.isEmpty() && suppliedVersion!=version) { throw ApiError(409,"原题或参考已更新，请刷新后核对答案") }
            let requestId=if (body.get("request_id").isSome()) { textField(body,"request_id",80) } else { "" }
            let payload=JsonArray(); for (key in ["answer","handwriting_image_url","handwriting_recognition"]) { payload.add(body.get(key) ?? JsonNull()) }; let digest=sha256(payload.toString().toArray())
            if (!requestId.isEmpty()) {
                let previous=tx.query("SELECT * FROM review_records WHERE plan_id=? AND JSON_UNQUOTE(JSON_EXTRACT(grading_details,'$.review_request_id'))=? LIMIT 1",[Some(id),Some(requestId)]).rows
                if (!previous.isEmpty()) {
                    let record=rowJson("review_records",previous[0]); let details=tutorObject(record,"grading_details")
                    if (tutorText(details,"review_record_state")=="superseded") { throw ApiError(409,"已有更新的复习作答，请刷新查看当前判题") }
                    if (tutorText(details,"review_request_digest")!=digest || tutorText(details,"memory_question_version")!=version) { throw ApiError(409,"提交编号对应的作答或原题已变化，请核对后重新提交") }
                    return reviewAttemptReceipt(record,accounts)
                }
            }
            let analyses = tx.query("SELECT * FROM problem_analyses WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at,id LIMIT 1",[sqlValue(plan["question_id"])]).rows
            if (analyses.isEmpty() || rowText(analyses[0],"final_answer").isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422,"这道题还没有标准答案，请先在题目详情中完成分析") }
            let analysis = rowJson("problem_analyses",analyses[0]); let handwriting = validateTutorHandwriting(body,rowText(user,"id"),id,kind: "review")
            let input = JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("grade_answer")); input.put("text",question["question_text"]); input.put("answer",answer); input.put("expected",analysis["final_answer"])
            input.put("subject",question["subject"]); input.put("question_type",question["question_type"]); input.put("options",question["options"])
            input.put("reference_solution",tutorStringArray(tutorRowTexts(queryArray(tx,"reference_steps","SELECT * FROM reference_steps WHERE analysis_id=? ORDER BY step_index",jsonText(analysis,"id")))))
            let grading = algorithmCompute(input); let details = (JsonValue.fromStr(grading.toString()) as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            details.put("memory_question_version",JsonString(version)); details.put("review_request_id",JsonString(requestId)); details.put("review_request_digest",JsonString(digest))
            details.put("handwriting_image_url",handwriting["image_url"]); details.put("handwriting_recognition",handwriting["recognition"])
            tx.query("UPDATE review_records SET grading_details=JSON_SET(grading_details,'$.review_record_state','superseded') WHERE plan_id=? AND grade='pending'",[Some(id)])
            let recordId = newId()
            tx.query("INSERT INTO review_records (id,plan_id,grade,correct,student_answer,expected_answer,feedback,score,grading_details,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'pending',?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(recordId),Some(id),sqlValue(grading["correct"]),sqlValue(answer),sqlValue(analysis["final_answer"]),sqlValue(grading["feedback"]),sqlValue(grading["score"]),Some(details.toString())])
            return reviewAttemptReceipt(rowJson("review_records",tx.query("SELECT * FROM review_records WHERE id=?",[Some(recordId)]).rows[0]),accounts)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

public func gradeReview(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST"); let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id = parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body = readJson(context)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT * FROM review_plans WHERE id=? AND user_id=? FOR UPDATE",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"复习计划不存在") }
            let plan = rowJson("review_plans",rows[0]); if (jsonText(plan,"status") != "pending") { throw ApiError(409,"这次复习已完成，请刷新复习列表") }
            let records = tx.query("SELECT * FROM review_records WHERE id=? AND plan_id=? AND grade='pending'",[Some(tutorText(body,"attempt_id")),Some(id)]).rows
            if (records.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422,"请先提交答案并完成系统判定") }
            let record = rowJson("review_records",records[0]); let details = tutorObject(record,"grading_details")
            if (tutorText(details,"review_record_state")=="superseded") { throw ApiError(409,"已有更新的复习作答，请刷新查看当前判题") }
            if ((details.get("can_update_learning") ?? JsonBool(true)).toString() != "true") { throw ApiError(409,"这次判题仍需核对，不能据此更新复习计划") }
            let currentQuestion=reviewQuestion(tx,plan,rowText(user,"id")); let sourceVersion=tutorText(details,"memory_question_version")
            if (jsonText(currentQuestion,"status")=="archived" || (!sourceVersion.isEmpty() && sourceVersion!=memoryQuestionVersion(tx,currentQuestion))) { throw ApiError(409,"原题或参考已更新，请重新提交作答再评价复习") }
            let grade = tutorText(body,"grade"); let actualGrade = if (body.get("grade").isNone()) { "good" } else { grade }
            let correct = tutorFlag(record,"correct"); var days: Int64 = 1; var label = "还没想起来"
            match (actualGrade) {
                case "again" => ()
                case "hard" => days = if (correct) { 2 } else { 1 }; label = "有点困难"
                case "good" => days = if (correct) { 7 } else { 2 }; label = "已经掌握"
                case "easy" => days = if (correct) { 15 } else { 4 }; label = "很轻松"
                case _ => throw ApiError(422,"请选择有效的复习反馈")
            }
            tx.query("UPDATE review_plans SET status='completed',updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[Some(id)])
            tx.query("UPDATE review_records SET grade=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[Some(actualGrade),sqlValue(record["id"])])
            let nextId = newId(); let reason = "上次作答${if (correct) { "正确" } else { "有误" }}，随后选择了“${label}”"
            tx.query("INSERT INTO review_plans (id,user_id,question_id,due_at,interval_days,reason,status,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,TIMESTAMPADD(DAY,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP()),?,?,'pending',UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(nextId),user["id"],sqlValue(plan["question_id"]),Some(days.toString()),Some(days.toString()),Some(reason)])
            let saved = rowJson("review_records",tx.query("SELECT * FROM review_records WHERE id=?",[sqlValue(record["id"])]).rows[0])
            let savedDetails = tutorObject(saved,"grading_details"); let rawImagePath = tutorText(savedDetails,"handwriting_image_url"); let imagePath = if (rawImagePath.isEmpty()) { "" } else { rawImagePath.split("?",2)[0] }
            if (!imagePath.isEmpty()) { savedDetails.put("handwriting_image_url",JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl(imagePath))) }
            let result = JsonObject(); result.put("record",saved); result.put("next_plan",rowJson("review_plans",tx.query("SELECT * FROM review_plans WHERE id=?",[Some(nextId)]).rows[0])); return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

func servePracticeImage(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): Unit {
    try {
        if (context.request.method!="GET" && context.request.method!="HEAD") { throw ApiError(405,"请求方法不支持") }
        if (!accounts.validMedia(context)) { throw ApiError(403,"资源链接已失效，请返回页面刷新") }
        let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-3]
        let index=try { Int64.parse(parts[parts.size-1]) } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422,"图片序号无效") }
        var stored=""
        try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
            let rows=db.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_questions WHERE id=?",[Some(id)]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"图片不存在") }
            let paths=tutorArray(rowJson("dataset_questions",rows[0]),"image_paths")
            if (index<0 || index>=paths.getItems().size) { throw ApiError(404,"图片不存在") }
            stored=(paths[index] as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
        }
        let working=getWorkingDirectory().toString()
        let project=if (working.endsWith("/backend-cangjie")) { working[..working.size-16] } else { working }
        let configured=getVariable("CANGJIE_QUESTION_BANK_ROOT") ?? "data/question_bank_assets"
        let root=if (configured.startsWith("/")) { configured } else { project+"/"+configured }
        let relative=if (stored.startsWith("/")) { stored } else { project+"/"+stored }
        if (!relative.startsWith(root+"/")) { throw ApiError(404,"图片不存在") }
        serveMedia(context,accounts,storageRoot: Some(root),storagePath: Some(relative[root.size+1..]))
    } catch (error: ApiError) { let body=JsonObject(); body.put("detail",JsonString(error.message)); jsonResponse(context,body,status: error.status) }
    catch (_: Exception) { let body=JsonObject(); body.put("detail",JsonString("图片暂时不可用")); jsonResponse(context,body,status: 503) }
}

// Persist the result, but renew expiring image links whenever it is read.

```

```cangjie

func studyBank(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    let form=insightQuery(context)
    let subject=queryLast(form,"subject"); let band=queryLast(form,"grade_band"); let source=queryLast(form,"source_id"); let search=queryLast(form,"search")
    if (subject.size>100 || band.size>100 || source.size>100 || search.size>300) { throw ApiError(422,"筛选条件过长") }
    let page=if (queryLast(form,"page").isEmpty()) { 1 } else { try { Int64.parse(queryLast(form,"page")) } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422,"页码无效") } }
    if (page<1 || page>10000) { throw ApiError(422,"页码无效") }
    let seed=if (queryLast(form,"shuffle_seed").isEmpty()) { unixNow()%2147483647 } else { try { Int64.parse(queryLast(form,"shuffle_seed")) } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422,"随机顺序参数无效") } }
    if (seed<0 || seed>2147483647) { throw ApiError(422,"随机顺序参数无效") }
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        let grade=bankStudentGrade(db,rowText(user,"id"))
        let filters=bankFilter(subject,band,source,search,grade:grade)
        let browseWhere=bankScopedWhere(grade)
        let result=JsonObject(); result.put("page",JsonInt(page)); result.put("page_size",JsonInt(20)); result.put("grade",JsonString(grade)); result.put("scope_label",JsonString(if (grade.startsWith("高")) { "高中" } else { grade }))
        let items=JsonArray()
        let sourceNames=JsonObject()
        for (sourceRow in db.query("SELECT id,name FROM dataset_sources").rows) {
            sourceNames.put(rowText(sourceRow,"id"),JsonString(rowText(sourceRow,"name")))
        }
        let paging=" LIMIT 20 OFFSET "+((page-1)*20).toString()
        // Shuffle IDs, interleaving subjects so large English datasets cannot monopolize "all".
        // The seeded per-ID key stays stable across pages and query execution plans.
        let randomKey="CRC32(CONCAT(q.id,':',"+seed.toString()+"))"
        let listing=if (subject.isEmpty()) {
            "SELECT q.* FROM dataset_questions q JOIN (SELECT id,subject_rank,random_key FROM (SELECT q.id,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY q.subject ORDER BY "+randomKey+",q.id) AS subject_rank,"+randomKey+" AS random_key"+browseWhere+") mixed ORDER BY subject_rank,random_key,id"+paging+") selected ON selected.id=q.id ORDER BY selected.subject_rank,selected.random_key,selected.id"
        } else { "SELECT q.*"+browseWhere+(if (grade.startsWith("高")) { " ORDER BY q.subject,q.id" } else { " ORDER BY q.grade,q.subject,q.id" })+paging }
        result.put("shuffle_seed",JsonInt(seed))
        for (row in db.query(listing,filters).rows) {
            let value=rowJson("dataset_questions",row)
            value.put("source_name",sourceNames.get(rowText(row,"dataset_source_id")) ?? JsonString("题库")); items.add(bankQuestion(value,accounts))
        }
        result.put("questions",items)
        // One eligibility scan supplies facets, sources and the unsearched count.
        let facetCounts=HashMap<String,JsonObject>(); let sourceCounts=HashMap<String,Int64>()
        var total: Int64=0
        for (row in db.query("SELECT q.subject,q.grade_band,q.dataset_source_id,COUNT(*) AS total"+browseWhere+" GROUP BY q.subject,q.grade_band,q.dataset_source_id",bankFilter("","","","",grade:grade)).rows) {
            let rowSubject=rowText(row,"subject"); let rowBand=rowText(row,"grade_band"); let rowSource=rowText(row,"dataset_source_id")
            let amount=Int64.parse(rowText(row,"total")); let key=rowSubject+"|"+rowBand
            let item=facetCounts.get(key) ?? JsonObject()
            item.put("subject",JsonString(rowSubject)); item.put("grade_band",JsonString(rowBand)); item.put("count",JsonInt(tutorNumber(item,"count")+amount)); facetCounts[key]=item
            sourceCounts[rowSource]=(sourceCounts.get(rowSource) ?? 0)+amount
            if ((subject.isEmpty() || subject==rowSubject) && (band.isEmpty() || band==rowBand) && (source.isEmpty() || source==rowSource)) { total+=amount }
        }
        if (!search.isEmpty()) { total=Int64.parse(rowText(db.query("SELECT COUNT(*) AS total"+browseWhere,filters).rows[0],"total")) }
        result.put("total",JsonInt(total))
        let facets=JsonArray(); for ((_,item) in facetCounts) { facets.add(item) }; result.put("facets",facets)
        let sources=JsonArray()
        for ((id,amount) in sourceCounts) {
            let item=JsonObject(); item.put("id",JsonString(id)); item.put("name",sourceNames.get(id) ?? JsonString("题库")); item.put("count",JsonInt(amount)); sources.add(item)
        }
        result.put("sources",sources); return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"题库暂时无法读取")
}
```

## GET / POST /api/v1/study-sessions

- 新接口映射 `/api/v1/study-sessions`，方法保持 GET, POST

- 响应结构直接构建字段 adaptive_selection, source_name
- 调用闭包 `adaptivePracticeSelection`, `algorithmCompute`, `analysisFingerprint`, `bankFilter`, `bankGradeAlias`, `bankLaterMath`, `bankQuestion`, `bankScopedWhere`, `bankStudentGrade`, `check`, `close`, `constantEqual`, `controlTransaction`, `credentialTag`, `currentUser`, `datasetDisplayOptions`, `datasetImageLayout`, `datasetImageMarkers`, `datasetImageUrls`, `datasetQuestionImages`, `datasetSolutionImages`, `decodeBase64`, `diagnosisFingerprint`, `editNumber`, `editText`, `firstJson`, `fromEnvironment`, `hmac256`, `jsonText`, `mediaUrl`, `newId`, `optionalText`, `ownedStudy`, `paperInteger`, `query`, `queryArray`, `randomBytes`, `read`, `readJson`, `readToken`, `remaining`, `requireMethod`, `retrievalGrade`, `rowJson`, `rowText`, `runeCount`, `sha256`, `sqlValue`, `stripWhitespace`, `studyDetail`, `studyInteger`, `studyItems`, `studyQuestionAvailable`, `studySessionRow`, `studySessions`, `taskFingerprint`, `taskRow`, `taskSnapshot`, `taskStoppedView`, `taskValidateVersion`, `transaction`, `tutorAnswerText`, `tutorArray`, `tutorDataSnapshot`, `tutorFlag`, `tutorNumber`, `tutorObject`, `tutorText`, `unixNow`, `unpaddedBase64`, `urlBase64`, `usableRetrievalCandidate`, `zw_equal`, `zw_hmac`, `zw_media_close`, `zw_media_open`, `zw_media_stat`, `zw_mysql_transaction`, `zw_random`, `zw_sha256`, `zw_stmt_affected`, `zw_stmt_bind`, `zw_stmt_close`, `zw_stmt_column_name`, `zw_stmt_columns`, `zw_stmt_copy`, `zw_stmt_error`, `zw_stmt_execute`, `zw_stmt_length`, `zw_stmt_next`, `zw_stmt_param_count`, `zw_stmt_prepare`, `zw_stmt_value`

以下保留完整处理函数实现，包含分支参数、请求对象读取、验证、返回构造与错误规则。共享 helper 的 SQL、返回键及验证证据在功能清单逐函数索引。字段精确类型还需结合数据库报告中的 column_types 与算法 Pydantic 模型。

```cangjie

func studySessions(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    let creating=context.request.method=="POST"; if (!creating) { requireMethod(context,"GET") }
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let owner=rowText(user,"id")
        if (!creating) {
            let result=JsonArray()
            for (row in db.query("SELECT s.*,(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM study_session_items i WHERE i.session_id=s.id) AS question_count FROM study_sessions s WHERE s.user_id=? ORDER BY s.created_at DESC,s.id DESC LIMIT 50",[Some(owner)]).rows) { result.add(studySessionRow(row)) }; return result
        }
        let grade=bankStudentGrade(db,owner)
        let body=readJson(context); let mode=optionalText(body,"mode","practice",20)
        if (mode!="practice" && mode!="exam") { throw ApiError(422,"请选择练习或模拟考试") }
        let subject=optionalText(body,"subject","",30); let band=optionalText(body,"grade_band","",30); let source=optionalText(body,"source_id","",36)
        let search=optionalText(body,"search","",300)
        let count=studyInteger(body.get("count") ?? JsonInt(5),1,20); let minutes=studyInteger(body.get("minutes") ?? JsonInt(15),1,180)
        let rawIds=body.get("question_ids") ?? JsonArray()
        if (!(rawIds is JsonArray)) { throw ApiError(422,"题目编号格式无效") }
        let ids=(rawIds as JsonArray).getOrThrow()
        if (ids.getItems().size>1) { throw ApiError(422,"单题练习请选择一道题") }
        let strategy=optionalText(body,"selection_strategy","random",20)
        if (strategy!="random" && strategy!="adaptive") { throw ApiError(422,"选题方式无效") }
        let selection=if (strategy=="adaptive" && mode=="practice" && ids.getItems().isEmpty()) { adaptivePracticeSelection(db,owner,subject,band,source,search,count,grade:grade) } else { JsonObject() }
        let selected=tutorArray(selection,"question_ids")
        let taskId=optionalText(body,"learning_task_id","",36)
        if (!taskId.isEmpty() && (mode!="practice" || ids.getItems().size!=1)) { throw ApiError(422,"请选择本次学习推荐的一道题") }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows=if (!taskId.isEmpty()) {
                // Old mistakes keep their original learning context after the student advances a grade.
                let task=taskRow(tx,taskId,owner)
                let plan=tutorObject(tutorObject(task,"student_view"),"recommendation")
                let requested=(editText(ids.getItems()[0],36,minimum:1,trim:true) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
                if (tutorText(task,"status")!="completed" || tutorText(plan,"question_source")!="dataset" || tutorText(plan,"question_id")!=requested) { throw ApiError(409,"请从已完成的学习任务打开推荐题") }
                let snapshot=taskSnapshot(tx,tutorText(task,"question_id"),owner)
                if (!taskValidateVersion(tx,task,snapshot)) { throw ApiError(409,"原题已更新，请重新学习后获取推荐") }
                let candidates=tx.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_questions WHERE id=?",[Some(requested)]).rows
                if (candidates.isEmpty() || !usableRetrievalCandidate(tutorObject(snapshot,"question"),rowJson("dataset_questions",candidates[0]),"practice")) { throw ApiError(409,"推荐题已更新，请重新获取练习") }
                candidates
            } else if (!selected.getItems().isEmpty()) {
                let picked=ArrayList<HashMap<String,Option<String>>>()
                for (id in selected.getItems()) {
                    let params=ArrayList<Option<String>>(bankFilter(subject,band,source,search,grade:grade)); params.add(sqlValue(id))
                    for (row in tx.query("SELECT q.*"+bankScopedWhere(grade)+" AND q.id=?",params.toArray()).rows) { picked.add(row) }
                }
                picked
            } else if (ids.getItems().isEmpty()) {
                tx.query("SELECT q.*"+bankScopedWhere(grade)+" ORDER BY RAND() LIMIT "+count.toString(),bankFilter(subject,band,source,search,grade:grade)).rows
            } else {
                let params=ArrayList<Option<String>>(bankFilter(subject,band,source,search,grade:grade))
                params.add(Some((editText(ids.getItems()[0],36,minimum:1,trim:true) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()))
                tx.query("SELECT q.*"+bankScopedWhere(grade)+" AND q.id=?",params.toArray()).rows
            }
            if (rows.isEmpty() || (ids.getItems().isEmpty() && rows.size<count)) { throw ApiError(422,"当前范围可用题目不足，请减少题数或更换学科") }
            for (row in rows) {
                if (tutorFlag(datasetImageLayout(rowJson("dataset_questions",row)),"review")) { throw ApiError(422,"题图需要核对，请选择其他题目") }
            }
            let id=newId(); let title=if (mode=="exam") { (if (subject.isEmpty()) { "综合" } else { subject })+"模拟试卷" } else { (if (subject.isEmpty()) { "题库" } else { subject })+"练习" }
            tx.query("INSERT INTO study_sessions (id,user_id,mode,title,subject,grade_band,status,duration_seconds,deadline_at,version,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,'active',?,IF(?='exam',TIMESTAMPADD(SECOND,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6)),NULL),1,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(id),Some(owner),Some(mode),Some(title),Some(subject),Some(band),Some((minutes*60).toString()),Some(mode),Some((minutes*60).toString())])
            var position: Int64=0
            for (row in rows) {
                let snapshot=rowJson("dataset_questions",row); let sources=tx.query("SELECT name FROM dataset_sources WHERE id=?",[row["dataset_source_id"]]).rows
                if (!selected.getItems().isEmpty()) { snapshot.put("adaptive_selection",selection) }
                snapshot.put("source_name",JsonString(if (sources.isEmpty()) { "题库" } else { rowText(sources[0],"name") }))
                tx.query("INSERT INTO study_session_items (id,session_id,position,snapshot,student_answer,grading) VALUES (?,?,?,?,NULL,NULL)",[Some(newId()),Some(id),Some(position.toString()),Some(snapshot.toString())]); position++
            }
            return studyDetail(tx,ownedStudy(tx,id,owner),accounts)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"暂时无法创建练习")
}
```

- 认证 Bearer 令牌，currentUser 校验用户及 credential
- 权限 遵循处理函数及所有权 helper，学生业务按 user_id/owner 限定

- 请求路径参数 session_id
- 请求 JSON 直接字段 answers, version
- 响应结构直接构建字段 answered, correct, excluded, max_score, pending, score, total
- 调用闭包 `algorithmCompute`, `bankQuestion`, `check`, `close`, `constantEqual`, `controlTransaction`, `credentialTag`, `currentUser`, `datasetDisplayOptions`, `datasetImageLayout`, `datasetImageMarkers`, `datasetImageUrls`, `datasetQuestionImages`, `datasetSolutionImages`, `decodeBase64`, `editNumber`, `editText`, `fromEnvironment`, `gradeStudyItem`, `hmac256`, `jsonText`, `mediaUrl`, `optionalText`, `ownedStudy`, `paperInteger`, `query`, `read`, `readJson`, `readToken`, `remaining`, `rowText`, `runeCount`, `sqlValue`, `stripWhitespace`, `studyAnswer`, `studyAnswered`, `studyDetail`, `studyInteger`, `studyItems`, `studyQuestionAvailable`, `studySessionApi`, `studySessionRow`, `studyUnavailableGrade`, `transaction`, `tutorAnswerText`, `tutorArray`, `tutorFlag`, `tutorNumber`, `tutorObject`, `tutorText`, `unixNow`, `unpaddedBase64`, `urlBase64`, `zw_equal`, `zw_hmac`, `zw_media_close`, `zw_media_open`, `zw_media_stat`, `zw_mysql_transaction`, `zw_stmt_affected`, `zw_stmt_bind`, `zw_stmt_close`, `zw_stmt_column_name`, `zw_stmt_columns`, `zw_stmt_copy`, `zw_stmt_error`, `zw_stmt_execute`, `zw_stmt_length`, `zw_stmt_next`, `zw_stmt_param_count`, `zw_stmt_prepare`, `zw_stmt_value`

```cangjie

func studySessionApi(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    let method=context.request.method; let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[4]
    let submitting=parts[parts.size-1]=="submit"
    if ((submitting && method!="POST") || (!submitting && method!="GET" && method!="PATCH")) { throw ApiError(405,"请求方法不支持") }
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let owner=rowText(user,"id")
        if (method=="GET") { return studyDetail(db,ownedStudy(db,id,owner),accounts) }
        let body=readJson(context); let version=studyInteger(body.get("version") ?? JsonNull(),1,100000000)
        if (!submitting) {
            let raw=body.get("answers") ?? JsonNull()
            if (!(raw is JsonArray)) { throw ApiError(422,"请提交作答列表") }; let answers=(raw as JsonArray).getOrThrow()
            return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
                let session=ownedStudy(tx,id,owner,lock:true)
                if (jsonText(session,"status")!="active") { throw ApiError(409,"已经交卷，不能修改作答") }
                if (tutorNumber(session,"version")!=version) { throw ApiError(409,"作答已在其他页面更新，请重新读取") }
                if (jsonText(session,"mode")=="exam" && tutorNumber(session,"expired")==1) { throw ApiError(409,"考试时间已到，请交卷") }
                let current=studyItems(tx,id); let allowed=HashSet<String>(); let seen=HashSet<String>()
                for (entry in current.getItems()) { allowed.add(jsonText((entry as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),"id")) }
                if (answers.getItems().size!=current.getItems().size) { throw ApiError(422,"作答题目与试卷不一致") }
                for (rawAnswer in answers.getItems()) {
                    if (!(rawAnswer is JsonObject)) { throw ApiError(422,"作答格式无效") }
                    let answer=(rawAnswer as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let itemId=optionalText(answer,"id","",36)
                    if (!allowed.contains(itemId) || seen.contains(itemId)) { throw ApiError(422,"作答题目与试卷不一致") }; seen.add(itemId)
                    let clean=studyAnswer(answer.get("answer") ?? JsonNull())
                    tx.query("UPDATE study_session_items SET student_answer=? WHERE id=? AND session_id=?",[Some(clean.toString()),Some(itemId),Some(id)])
                }
                tx.query("UPDATE study_sessions SET version=version+1,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(id)])
                return studyDetail(tx,ownedStudy(tx,id,owner),accounts)
            })
        }
        let before=ownedStudy(db,id,owner)
        if (jsonText(before,"status")=="submitted") { return studyDetail(db,before,accounts) }
        if (tutorNumber(before,"version")!=version) { throw ApiError(409,"作答已更新，请重新读取后交卷") }
        let items=studyItems(db,id).getItems(); let results=JsonArray()
        let unavailable=HashSet<String>()
        for (entry in items) { let item=(entry as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); if (!studyQuestionAvailable(db,tutorObject(item,"snapshot"))) { unavailable.add(jsonText(item,"id")) } }
        // Bounded parallel computation outside the transaction; no locks during AI.
        var start: Int64=0
        while (start<items.size) {
            let offset=start; let size=if (items.size-start>4) { 4 } else { items.size-start }
            let jobs=Array(size,{ index =>
                let item=(items[offset+index] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
                let blocked=unavailable.contains(jsonText(item,"id"))
                spawn { if (blocked) { studyUnavailableGrade() } else { gradeStudyItem(item) } }
            })
            for (job in jobs) { results.add(job.get()) }; start+=size
        }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let session=ownedStudy(tx,id,owner,lock:true)
            if (jsonText(session,"status")=="submitted") { return studyDetail(tx,session,accounts) }
            if (tutorNumber(session,"version")!=version) { throw ApiError(409,"作答已更新，本次判分未保存，请重新交卷") }
            var correct: Int64=0; var pending: Int64=0; var answered: Int64=0; var excluded: Int64=0
            for (index in 0..items.size) {
                let item=(items[index] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
                let available=studyQuestionAvailable(tx,tutorObject(item,"snapshot"),lock:true)
                let grading=if (available) { (results.getItems()[index] as JsonObject).getOrThrow() } else { studyUnavailableGrade() }
                if (studyAnswered(item["student_answer"])) { answered++ }
                if (!available) { excluded++ } else if (!tutorFlag(grading,"can_update_learning")) { pending++ } else if (tutorFlag(grading,"correct")) { correct++ }
                tx.query("UPDATE study_session_items SET grading=? WHERE id=? AND session_id=?",[Some(grading.toString()),Some(jsonText(item,"id")),Some(id)])
            }
            let summary=JsonObject(); summary.put("correct",JsonInt(correct)); summary.put("pending",JsonInt(pending)); if (excluded>0) { summary.put("excluded",JsonInt(excluded)) }; summary.put("answered",JsonInt(answered)); summary.put("total",JsonInt(items.size)); summary.put("score",JsonInt(correct*5)); summary.put("max_score",JsonInt((items.size-excluded)*5))
            tx.query("UPDATE study_sessions SET status='submitted',results=?,version=version+1,submitted_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(summary.toString()),Some(id)])
            return studyDetail(tx,ownedStudy(tx,id,owner),accounts)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"练习记录暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie
                    let clean=studyAnswer(answer.get("answer") ?? JsonNull())
                    tx.query("UPDATE study_session_items SET student_answer=? WHERE id=? AND session_id=?",[Some(clean.toString()),Some(itemId),Some(id)])
                }
                tx.query("UPDATE study_sessions SET version=version+1,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(id)])
                return studyDetail(tx,ownedStudy(tx,id,owner),accounts)
            })
        }
        let before=ownedStudy(db,id,owner)
        if (jsonText(before,"status")=="submitted") { return studyDetail(db,before,accounts) }
        if (tutorNumber(before,"version")!=version) { throw ApiError(409,"作答已更新，请重新读取后交卷") }
        let items=studyItems(db,id).getItems(); let results=JsonArray()
        let unavailable=HashSet<String>()
        for (entry in items) { let item=(entry as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); if (!studyQuestionAvailable(db,tutorObject(item,"snapshot"))) { unavailable.add(jsonText(item,"id")) } }
        // Bounded parallel computation outside the transaction; no locks during AI.
        var start: Int64=0
        while (start<items.size) {
            let offset=start; let size=if (items.size-start>4) { 4 } else { items.size-start }
            let jobs=Array(size,{ index =>
                let item=(items[offset+index] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
                let blocked=unavailable.contains(jsonText(item,"id"))
                spawn { if (blocked) { studyUnavailableGrade() } else { gradeStudyItem(item) } }
            })
            for (job in jobs) { results.add(job.get()) }; start+=size
        }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let session=ownedStudy(tx,id,owner,lock:true)
            if (jsonText(session,"status")=="submitted") { return studyDetail(tx,session,accounts) }
            if (tutorNumber(session,"version")!=version) { throw ApiError(409,"作答已更新，本次判分未保存，请重新交卷") }
            var correct: Int64=0; var pending: Int64=0; var answered: Int64=0; var excluded: Int64=0
            for (index in 0..items.size) {
                let item=(items[index] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
                let available=studyQuestionAvailable(tx,tutorObject(item,"snapshot"),lock:true)
                let grading=if (available) { (results.getItems()[index] as JsonObject).getOrThrow() } else { studyUnavailableGrade() }
                if (studyAnswered(item["student_answer"])) { answered++ }
                if (!available) { excluded++ } else if (!tutorFlag(grading,"can_update_learning")) { pending++ } else if (tutorFlag(grading,"correct")) { correct++ }
                tx.query("UPDATE study_session_items SET grading=? WHERE id=? AND session_id=?",[Some(grading.toString()),Some(jsonText(item,"id")),Some(id)])
            }
            let summary=JsonObject(); summary.put("correct",JsonInt(correct)); summary.put("pending",JsonInt(pending)); if (excluded>0) { summary.put("excluded",JsonInt(excluded)) }; summary.put("answered",JsonInt(answered)); summary.put("total",JsonInt(items.size)); summary.put("score",JsonInt(correct*5)); summary.put("max_score",JsonInt((items.size-excluded)*5))
            tx.query("UPDATE study_sessions SET status='submitted',results=?,version=version+1,submitted_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(summary.toString()),Some(id)])
            return studyDetail(tx,ownedStudy(tx,id,owner),accounts)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"练习记录暂时不可用")
}

```

## POST /api/v1/practice/recommend

- 新接口映射 `/api/v1/practice/recommend`，方法保持 POST
- 注册证据 `backend-cangjie/src/main.cj:60`，处理函数 `recommendPractice`

```cangjie

func recommendPractice(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let owner=rowText(user,"id"); let body=readJson(context)
        let id=optionalText(body,"mistake_id","",100,nonempty: true); let mode=practiceMode(body)
        let debug=editBool(body.get("debug") ?? JsonBool(false)).getValue(); let force=editBool(body.get("force_new") ?? JsonBool(false)).getValue()
        let setup=db.transaction<JsonObject>({ tx =>
            let rows=tx.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND status<>'archived'",[Some(id),Some(owner)]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"没有找到这道错题") }
            let question=rowJson("questions",rows[0]); let fingerprint=learningSourceFingerprint(tx,question)
            let setup=JsonObject(); setup.put("question",question); setup.put("fingerprint",JsonString(fingerprint))
            let generatedRows=tx.query("SELECT v.* FROM variant_questions v JOIN tutor_sessions s ON s.id=v.session_id WHERE s.user_id=? AND v.question_id=? AND s.stage='standalone_practice' AND v.verified=1 ORDER BY v.created_at DESC LIMIT 20",[Some(owner),Some(id)]).rows
            if (!generatedRows.isEmpty()) {
                let version=practiceSourceVersion(tx,question)
                for (row in generatedRows) {
                    let item=rowJson("variant_questions",row); let meta=tutorObject(item,"generation_metadata")
                    if (tutorText(meta,"mode")==mode && tutorText(meta,"source_version")==version) {
                        setup.put("generate",JsonBool(true)); return setup
                    }
                }
            }
            var preparation=JsonObject()
            try { preparation=currentPracticePreparation(tx,question,mode) } catch (_: ApiError) { () }

            setup.put("preparation",preparation)
            setup.put("source_version",JsonString(practiceSourceVersion(tx,question)))
            if (!force) {
                let previous=tx.query("SELECT * FROM practice_recommendations WHERE user_id=? AND mistake_id=? AND mode=? ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1",[Some(owner),Some(id),Some(mode)]).rows
                if (!previous.isEmpty()) {
                    let saved=rowJson("practice_recommendations",previous[0]); let trace=tutorObject(saved,"retrieval_trace"); let qid=jsonText(saved,"dataset_question_id")
                    let attempted=tx.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_practice_attempts WHERE user_id=? AND mistake_id=? AND dataset_question_id=? AND mode=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[Some(owner),Some(id),Some(qid),Some(mode)]).rows
                    let questions=tx.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_questions WHERE id=?",[Some(qid)]).rows
                    if (tutorText(trace,"source_question_fingerprint")==fingerprint && tutorText(trace,"target_knowledge_id")==tutorText(preparation,"target_knowledge_id") && !questions.isEmpty() && usableRetrievalCandidate(question,rowJson("dataset_questions",questions[0]),"practice")) {
                        let selected=rowJson("dataset_questions",questions[0])
                        var valid=practiceProofValid(tx,question,preparation,selected,trace)
                        if (!valid) {
                            let checks=practiceAligned(tx,question,preparation,practiceCandidate(selected)); valid=practiceAdmitted(checks,qid)
                            if (valid) { savePracticeProof(tx,question,preparation,selected,trace,practiceAlignmentMethod(checks,qid)); tx.query("UPDATE practice_recommendations SET retrieval_trace=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[Some(trace.toString()),sqlValue(saved["id"])]) }
                        }
                        if (valid && !attempted.isEmpty()) {
                            let details=tutorObject(rowJson("dataset_practice_attempts",attempted[0]),"grading_details")
                            valid=!tutorObject(details,"practice_receipt").getFields().isEmpty() && jsonEquivalent(tutorObject(tutorObject(details,"alignment_proof"),"context"),practiceProofContext(tx,question,preparation,selected))
                        }
                        if (valid) {
                            let result=practicePayload(accounts,selected,mode,trace,tutorArray(selected,"knowledge_points"))
                            if (!attempted.isEmpty()) {
                                let record=rowJson("dataset_practice_attempts",attempted[0]); let receipt=tutorObject(tutorObject(record,"grading_details"),"practice_receipt")
                                if (!receipt.getFields().isEmpty()) { result.put("previous_result",refreshedPracticeReceipt(accounts,selected,receipt)); result.put("previous_answer",record["student_answer"]) }
                            }
                            if (debug) { result.put("debug",trace) }; setup.put("cached",result); return setup
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            return setup
        })
        if (let Some(cached) <- setup.get("cached")) { return cached }
        if (tutorFlag(setup,"generate")) { return generatedPractice(db,owner,tutorObject(setup,"question"),mode,force) }
        let question=tutorObject(setup,"question"); let fingerprint=jsonText(setup,"fingerprint")
        let preparation=tutorObject(setup,"preparation")
        let excluded=JsonArray(); let seen=HashSet<String>()
        for (table in ["dataset_practice_attempts","practice_recommendations"]) {
            for (row in db.query("SELECT dataset_question_id FROM ${table} WHERE user_id=? AND mistake_id=?",[Some(owner),Some(id)]).rows) {
                let qid=rowText(row,"dataset_question_id"); if (!seen.contains(qid)) { seen.add(qid); excluded.add(JsonString(qid)) }
            }
        }
        if (tutorText(preparation,"target_knowledge_id").isEmpty()) { return generatedPractice(db,owner,question,mode,force) }
        var ranked=JsonArray(); var checked=JsonArray()
        try {
            ranked=tutorArray(retrievalCandidates(db,question,preparation,excluded,"practice",mode),"ranked")
            checked=practiceAligned(db,question,preparation,ranked)
        } catch (_: ApiError) { return generatedPractice(db,owner,question,mode,force) }
        let checks=checked
        let admitted=JsonArray()
        for (value in ranked.getItems()) { let row=(value as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); if (practiceAdmitted(checks,jsonText(tutorObject(row,"question"),"id"))) { admitted.add(row) } }
        if (admitted.getItems().isEmpty()) { return generatedPractice(db,owner,question,mode,force) }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let current=tx.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND status<>'archived' FOR UPDATE",[Some(id),Some(owner)]).rows
            if (current.isEmpty() || learningSourceFingerprint(tx,rowJson("questions",current[0]))!=fingerprint) { throw ApiError(409,"题目已修改，请重新获取推荐题") }
            if (practiceSourceVersion(tx,rowJson("questions",current[0]))!=jsonText(setup,"source_version")) { throw ApiError(409,"题目分析或诊断已更新，请重新获取练习") }
            let candidate=(admitted[0] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let selected=tutorObject(candidate,"question"); let trace=JsonObject()
            if (tutorText(currentPracticePreparation(tx,rowJson("questions",current[0]),mode),"target_knowledge_id")!=tutorText(preparation,"target_knowledge_id")) { throw ApiError(409,"学习目标已更新，请重新获取练习") }
            let fresh=tx.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_questions WHERE id=? AND subject=? AND usable_for_practice=1",[sqlValue(selected["id"]),sqlValue(question["subject"])]).rows
            if (fresh.isEmpty() || !usableRetrievalCandidate(question,rowJson("dataset_questions",fresh[0]),"practice") || !jsonEquivalent(rowJson("dataset_questions",fresh[0]),selected)) { throw ApiError(409,"推荐题已更新，请重新获取") }
            for (table in ["practice_recommendations","dataset_practice_attempts"]) {
                if (!tx.query("SELECT id FROM ${table} WHERE user_id=? AND mistake_id=? AND dataset_question_id=? LIMIT 1",[Some(owner),Some(id),sqlValue(selected["id"])]).rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(409,"推荐已变化，请重新获取练习题") }
            }
            trace.put("source_question_fingerprint",JsonString(fingerprint))
            trace.put("alignment_method",JsonString(practiceAlignmentMethod(checks,jsonText(selected,"id"))))
            for (key in ["knowledge_match","target_knowledge_id","target_knowledge_name","target_skill","error_match"]) { trace.put(key,preparation[key]) }
            let difficulty=tutorText(selected,"difficulty"); trace.put("difficulty_match",JsonString(if (difficulty.isEmpty()) { "同年级" } else { difficulty }))
            trace.put("question_type_difference",JsonBool(jsonText(selected,"question_type")!=jsonText(question,"question_type")))
            for (key in ["dense_rank","sparse_rank","fusion_rank"]) { trace.put(key,candidate[key]) }
            for (key in ["structure_similarity","reranker_score","mode_score"]) { trace.put(key,tutorObject(candidate,"score_trace")[key]) }
            savePracticeProof(tx,question,preparation,selected,trace,practiceAlignmentMethod(checks,jsonText(selected,"id")))
            tx.query("INSERT INTO practice_recommendations (id,user_id,mistake_id,dataset_question_id,mode,retrieval_rank,retrieval_trace,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(owner),Some(id),sqlValue(selected["id"]),Some(mode),sqlValue(candidate["fusion_rank"]),Some(trace.toString())])
            let result=practicePayload(accounts,selected,mode,trace,tutorArray(candidate,"knowledge_points")); if (debug) { result.put("debug",trace) }; return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```
