## POST /api/v1/practice/attempt

- 新接口映射 `/api/v1/practice/attempt`，方法保持 POST
- 注册证据 `backend-cangjie/src/main.cj:61`，处理函数 `attemptPractice`
- 认证 Bearer 令牌，currentUser 校验用户及 credential
- 权限 遵循处理函数及所有权 helper，学生业务按 user_id/owner 限定
- 成功状态 200，错误状态证据 401, 404, 405, 409, 413, 422, 502, 503
- 请求路径参数 无
- 请求 JSON 直接字段 duration_seconds, mistake_id, question_id, student_answer
- 响应结构直接构建字段 alignment_method, alignment_proof, answer, correct, dataset_question_id, expected, grading_method, handwriting_image_url, handwriting_recognition, hint_level, independent_answer, learning_note, mastery, mastery_before, operation, options, practice_attempt_id, question_type, reference_solution, solution, strategy, subject, target_knowledge_name, text
- 调用闭包 `algorithmCompute`, `applyLearningEvidence`, `attemptGeneratedPractice`, `attemptPractice`, `check`, `close`, `cognitionFirstLabel`, `constantEqual`, `controlTransaction`, `credentialTag`, `currentPracticePreparation`, `currentUser`, `datasetDisplayOptions`, `datasetImageLayout`, `datasetImageMarkers`, `datasetImageUrls`, `datasetSolutionImages`, `decodeBase64`, `editNumber`, `editText`, `firstJson`, `fromEnvironment`, `hmac256`, `isClosed`, `jsonEquivalent`, `jsonText`, `learningFingerprintJson`, `learningKnowledgePoint`, `learningSourceFingerprint`, `mediaRoot`, `mediaUrl`, `memoryDatasetVersion`, `memoryEvidenceSource`, `memoryQuestionVersion`, `newId`, `optionalText`, `paperInteger`, `practiceAdmitted`, `practiceAligned`, `practiceAlignmentMethod`, `practiceCandidate`, `practiceMode`, `practiceProofContext`, `practiceProofValid`, `practiceServed`, `practiceSourceVersion`, `query`, `queryArray`, `randomBytes`, `read`, `readAt`, `readJson`, `readMedia`, `readToken`, `refreshedPracticeReceipt`, `remaining`, `requireMethod`, `retrievalGrade`, `retrievalPreparation`, `retrievalPreparationInput`, `rowJson`, `rowText`, `runeCount`, `savePracticeProof`, `sha256`, `sqlValue`, `stripWhitespace`, `transaction`, `tutorAnswerText`, `tutorArray`, `tutorFlag`, `tutorObject`, `tutorText`, `unixNow`, `unpaddedBase64`, `urlBase64`, `usableRetrievalCandidate`, `validStoragePath`, `validateTutorHandwriting`, `zw_equal`, `zw_hmac`, `zw_media_close`, `zw_media_open`, `zw_media_read`, `zw_media_stat`, `zw_mysql_transaction`, `zw_random`, `zw_sha256`, `zw_stmt_affected`, `zw_stmt_bind`, `zw_stmt_close`, `zw_stmt_column_name`, `zw_stmt_columns`, `zw_stmt_copy`, `zw_stmt_error`, `zw_stmt_execute`, `zw_stmt_length`, `zw_stmt_next`, `zw_stmt_param_count`, `zw_stmt_prepare`, `zw_stmt_value`

以下保留完整处理函数实现，包含分支参数、请求对象读取、验证、返回构造与错误规则。共享 helper 的 SQL、返回键及验证证据在功能清单逐函数索引。字段精确类型还需结合数据库报告中的 column_types 与算法 Pydantic 模型。

```cangjie

func attemptPractice(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body=readJson(context)
        let owner=rowText(user,"id"); let mistakeId=optionalText(body,"mistake_id","",100); let id=optionalText(body,"question_id","",100); let mode=practiceMode(body)
        let answer=body.get("student_answer") ?? { => throw ApiError(422,"请提交作答") }()
        let duration=paperInteger(body.get("duration_seconds") ?? JsonInt(0),0,7200)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let mistakes=tx.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND status<>'archived' FOR UPDATE",[Some(mistakeId),Some(owner)]).rows
            if (mistakes.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"没有找到这道错题") }
            let mistake=rowJson("questions",mistakes[0])
            let generated=tx.query("SELECT v.* FROM variant_questions v JOIN tutor_sessions s ON s.id=v.session_id WHERE v.id=? AND v.question_id=? AND s.user_id=? AND s.stage='standalone_practice' FOR UPDATE",[Some(id),Some(mistakeId),Some(owner)]).rows
            if (!generated.isEmpty()) { validateTutorHandwriting(body,owner,id,kind: "practice"); return attemptGeneratedPractice(tx,owner,mistake,rowJson("variant_questions",generated[0]),mode,answer,duration) }
            let questions=tx.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_questions WHERE id=? FOR UPDATE",[Some(id)]).rows
            if (questions.isEmpty() || rowText(questions[0],"usable_for_auto_grade")!="1") { throw ApiError(422,"这道题不能自动判分") }
            let question=rowJson("dataset_questions",questions[0])
            let existing=tx.query("SELECT * FROM dataset_practice_attempts WHERE user_id=? AND mistake_id=? AND dataset_question_id=? AND mode=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE",[Some(owner),Some(mistakeId),Some(id),Some(mode)]).rows
            if (!existing.isEmpty()) {
                practiceServed(tx,owner,mistake,id,mode)
                let record=rowJson("dataset_practice_attempts",existing[0]); let details=tutorObject(record,"grading_details"); let receipt=tutorObject(details,"practice_receipt")
                let context=practiceProofContext(tx,mistake,currentPracticePreparation(tx,mistake,mode),question)
                if (jsonEquivalent(tutorObject(tutorObject(details,"alignment_proof"),"context"),context) && jsonEquivalent(record["student_answer"],answer) && !receipt.getFields().isEmpty()) { return refreshedPracticeReceipt(accounts,question,receipt) }
                throw ApiError(409,"这道练习已经完成，请换一道题继续练习")
            }
            let handwriting=validateTutorHandwriting(body,owner,id,kind: "practice")
            let served=practiceServed(tx,owner,mistake,id,mode); let trace=tutorObject(served,"retrieval_trace"); let answerSeen=tutorFlag(trace,"answer_revealed")
            let preparation=currentPracticePreparation(tx,mistake,mode)
            if (!usableRetrievalCandidate(mistake,question,"practice") || tutorText(trace,"target_knowledge_id").isEmpty() || tutorText(trace,"target_knowledge_id")!=tutorText(preparation,"target_knowledge_id")) { throw ApiError(409,"学习目标或题目资格已变化，请重新获取练习") }
            if (!practiceProofValid(tx,mistake,preparation,question,trace)) {
                let checks=practiceAligned(tx,mistake,preparation,practiceCandidate(question))
                if (!practiceAdmitted(checks,id)) { throw ApiError(409,"这道练习与当前学习目标不匹配，请重新获取练习；本次未更新学习记录") }
                savePracticeProof(tx,mistake,preparation,question,trace,practiceAlignmentMethod(checks,id))
            }
            let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("grade_answer")); input.put("text",question["question_text"]); input.put("answer",answer); input.put("expected",question["answer"])
            input.put("subject",question["subject"]); input.put("question_type",question["question_type"]); input.put("options",question["options"])
            let solution=JsonArray(); for (line in jsonText(question,"solution").split("\n")) { let clean=stripWhitespace(line); if (!clean.isEmpty()) { solution.add(JsonString(clean)) } }; input.put("reference_solution",solution)
            let grading=algorithmCompute(input); let decisive=tutorFlag(grading,"can_update_learning")
            tx.query("UPDATE practice_recommendations SET retrieval_trace=JSON_SET(retrieval_trace,'$.answer_revealed',true),updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?",[sqlValue(served["id"])])
            let target=tutorText(trace,"target_knowledge_id")
            let knowledgeRows=tx.query("SELECT * FROM knowledge_points WHERE id=? AND subject=? FOR UPDATE",[Some(target),sqlValue(mistake["subject"])]).rows
            if (knowledgeRows.isEmpty() || rowText(knowledgeRows[0],"name")!=tutorText(preparation,"target_knowledge_name")) { throw ApiError(409,"练习知识点已变化，请重新获取练习") }
            let knowledge=if (knowledgeRows.isEmpty()) { JsonObject() } else { rowJson("knowledge_points",knowledgeRows[0]) }
            let preferred=tutorText(knowledge,"name"); let point=learningKnowledgePoint(tx,mistake,preferred: preferred,includeAnalysis: false)
            let old=tx.query("SELECT mastery_score FROM mastery_states WHERE user_id=? AND subject=? AND knowledge_point=? LIMIT 1",[Some(owner),Some(stripWhitespace(jsonText(mistake,"subject"))),Some(point)]).rows
            let before: JsonValue=if (old.isEmpty()) { JsonFloat(0.0) } else { rowJson("mastery_states",old[0])["mastery_score"] }
            var mastery: JsonValue=JsonNull(); var practiceAttemptId=""
            if (decisive) {
                let sources=tx.query("SELECT `key` FROM dataset_sources WHERE id=?",[sqlValue(question["dataset_source_id"])]).rows
                let source=if (sources.isEmpty()) { "unknown" } else { rowText(sources[0],"key") }
                let details=(JsonValue.fromStr(grading.toString()) as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); details.put("independent_answer",JsonBool(!answerSeen)); details.put("handwriting_image_url",JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl(jsonText(handwriting,"image_url")))); details.put("handwriting_recognition",handwriting["recognition"])
                details.put("alignment_method",tutorObject(trace,"alignment_proof")["method"]); details.put("target_knowledge_name",preparation["target_knowledge_name"])
                details.put("alignment_proof",trace["alignment_proof"])
                practiceAttemptId=newId()
                tx.query("INSERT INTO dataset_practice_attempts (id,user_id,mistake_id,dataset_question_id,source_dataset,knowledge_id,mode,student_answer,correct,duration_seconds,grading_details,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(practiceAttemptId),Some(owner),Some(mistakeId),Some(id),Some(source),if (knowledgeRows.isEmpty()) { None } else { sqlValue(knowledge["id"]) },Some(mode),Some(answer.toString()),sqlValue(grading["correct"]),Some(duration.toString()),Some(details.toString())])
                let evidence=JsonObject(); evidence.put(if (mode=="reinforce") { "near_transfer" } else { "far_transfer" },grading["correct"]); evidence.put("hint_level",JsonInt(0)); evidence.put("strategy",JsonString("independent_dataset_practice")); evidence.put("dataset_question_id",JsonString(id)); evidence.put("practice_attempt_id",JsonString(practiceAttemptId))
                if (!answerSeen) { let learning=applyLearningEvidence(tx,owner,mistake,evidence,preferred: preferred,includeAnalysis: false); mastery=tutorObject(learning,"mastery_after")["mastery_score"] }
            }
            let result=JsonObject(); result.put("correct",if (decisive) { grading["correct"] } else { JsonNull() })
            for (key in ["verdict","can_update_learning","confidence","evidence","feedback"]) { result.put(key,grading[key]) }
            if (answerSeen) { result.put("learning_note",JsonString("已看过参考答案，这次练习不重复计入掌握情况。")) }
            result.put("answer",question["answer"]); result.put("solution",question["solution"])
            for ((key,value) in datasetSolutionImages(question,accounts).getFields()) { result.put(key,value) }
            result.put("mastery_before",before); result.put("mastery",mastery); result.put("grading_method",grading["method"])
            if (!practiceAttemptId.isEmpty()) { tx.query("UPDATE dataset_practice_attempts SET grading_details=JSON_SET(grading_details,'$.practice_receipt',JSON_EXTRACT(?,'$')) WHERE id=?",[Some(result.toString()),Some(practiceAttemptId)]) }
            return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

- 调用闭包 `algorithmCompute`, `algorithmImage`, `bytesMatch`, `check`, `close`, `constantEqual`, `controlTransaction`, `credentialTag`, `currentUser`, `datasetDisplayOptions`, `datasetImageLayout`, `datasetImageMarkers`, `decodeBase64`, `ensureTutorVariant`, `findBytes`, `firstJson`, `fromEnvironment`, `headerParameters`, `hmac256`, `jsonEquivalent`, `jsonText`, `learningFingerprintJson`, `learningSourceFingerprint`, `mediaRoot`, `mediaUrl`, `newId`, `practiceHandwritingContext`, `practiceServed`, `practiceSourceVersion`, `publicTutorActivity`, `query`, `queryArray`, `randomBytes`, `read`, `readImageUpload`, `readToken`, `remaining`, `requireMethod`, `retrievalCandidates`, `retrievalGrade`, `retrievalPreparation`, `retrievalPreparationInput`, `reviewQuestion`, `rowJson`, `rowText`, `runeCount`, `sha256`, `sqlValue`, `stripWhitespace`, `teachingTemplates`, `transaction`, `tutorActivity`, `tutorActivityForSession`, `tutorAnswerText`, `tutorArray`, `tutorDataSnapshot`, `tutorFlag`, `tutorKnowledge`, `tutorNumber`, `tutorObject`, `tutorOptions`, `tutorRowTexts`, `tutorStringArray`, `tutorStrings`, `tutorText`, `tutorVariantRetrieval`, `unixNow`, `unpaddedBase64`, `uploadHandwriting`, `urlBase64`, `usableRetrievalCandidate`, `validStoragePath`, `writeMedia`, `zw_equal`, `zw_hmac`, `zw_media_close`, `zw_media_open`, `zw_media_stat`, `zw_media_write`, `zw_mysql_transaction`, `zw_random`, `zw_sha256`, `zw_stmt_affected`, `zw_stmt_bind`, `zw_stmt_close`, `zw_stmt_column_name`, `zw_stmt_columns`, `zw_stmt_copy`, `zw_stmt_error`, `zw_stmt_execute`, `zw_stmt_length`, `zw_stmt_next`, `zw_stmt_param_count`, `zw_stmt_prepare`, `zw_stmt_value`

```cangjie

public func uploadHandwriting(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db); let upload = readImageUpload(context)
        let kind = upload.fields.get("context_type") ?? ""; let id = upload.fields.get("context_id") ?? ""; let activityId = upload.fields.get("activity_id") ?? ""
        if (kind != "tutor" && kind != "review" && kind != "practice") { throw ApiError(422,"不支持的答题场景") }
        let visible = db.transaction<JsonObject>({ tx =>
            if (kind == "practice") { return practiceHandwritingContext(tx,rowText(user,"id"),upload.fields.get("mistake_id") ?? "",id,upload.fields.get("mode") ?? "reinforce") }
            if (kind == "review") {
                let plans = tx.query("SELECT * FROM review_plans WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND status='pending'",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
                if (plans.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"待复习题不存在") }
                let question = reviewQuestion(tx,rowJson("review_plans",plans[0]),rowText(user,"id"))
                if (!tutorArray(question,"options").getItems().isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422,"选择题请直接选择选项") }
                let result = JsonObject(); result.put("subject",question["subject"]); result.put("text",question["question_text"]); return result
            }
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"学习会话不存在") }
            let session = rowJson("tutor_sessions",rows[0]); let snapshot = tutorDataSnapshot(tx,jsonText(session,"question_id"),user["id"])
            let question = (snapshot["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            if (tutorFlag(tutorObject(session,"state"),"question_changed") || jsonText(question,"status") == "archived") { throw ApiError(409,"题目已变化或归档，请重新开始学习") }
            let value = tutorActivityForSession(tx,session,snapshot)
            if (!(value is JsonObject)) { throw ApiError(409,"当前练习已经变化，请刷新后重新上传") }
            let activity = (value as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            if (tutorText(activity,"id") != activityId) { throw ApiError(409,"当前练习已经变化，请刷新后重新上传") }
            let activityType = tutorText(activity,"type")
            if (activityType == "single_choice" || activityType == "multiple_choice" || activityType == "explanation" || activityType == "teach_back") { throw ApiError(422,"当前练习不需要上传手写过程") }
            let result = JsonObject(); result.put("subject",question["subject"]); result.put("text",activity["question"]); return result
        })
        let normalize = JsonObject(); normalize.put("operation",JsonString("normalize")); normalize.put("image_base64",JsonString(toBase64String(upload.bytes)))
        let image = algorithmImage(algorithmCompute(normalize)); let owner = rowText(user,"id")
        let imageId = newId().replace("-",""); let path = "uploads/answers/${owner}/${kind}/${id}/handwriting-${imageId}.jpg"
        writeMedia(path,image)
        let input = JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("recognize_handwriting")); input.put("image_base64",JsonString(toBase64String(image)))
        input.put("subject",visible["subject"]); input.put("text",visible["text"])
        var computed = JsonObject()
        try { computed = algorithmCompute(input) } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(503,"照片已保留，但暂时无法识别，请稍后重试或使用文字输入") }
        let result = JsonObject(); result.put("image_url",JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl("/"+path))); result.put("recognition",computed["recognition"]); return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

以下保留完整处理函数实现，包含分支参数、请求对象读取、验证、返回构造与错误规则。共享 helper 的 SQL、返回键及验证证据在功能清单逐函数索引。字段精确类型还需结合数据库报告中的 column_types 与算法 Pydantic 模型。

```cangjie

func sessionVisualization(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    let writing=context.request.method=="POST"
    if (!writing) { requireMethod(context,"GET") }
    let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body=if (writing) { readJson(context,defaultEmpty:true) } else { JsonObject() }
        let values=body.get("values") ?? JsonObject()
        if (!(values is JsonObject)) { throw ApiError(422,"演示参数格式不正确") }
        let observation=optionalText(body,"observation","",12000)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let visible=visualizationContext(tx,id,rowText(user,"id")); let session=tutorObject(visible,"session"); let snapshot=tutorObject(visible,"snapshot")
            let question=tutorObject(snapshot,"question")
            if (writing && (tutorFlag(tutorObject(session,"state"),"question_changed") || jsonText(question,"status")=="archived")) { throw ApiError(409,"题目已变化或归档，请重新开始学习") }
            let config=visualizationConfig(question,tutorStringArray(tutorKnowledge(snapshot)),(values as JsonObject).getOrThrow())
            let specValue=config["spec"]
            if (!(specValue is JsonObject)) { throw ApiError(404,"这道题暂时没有匹配的互动演示") }
            let spec=(specValue as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            if (!writing) {
                let result=JsonObject(); let summary=JsonObject()
                for (key in ["id","title","question_text"]) { summary.put(key,question[key]) }
                result.put("question",summary); result.put("spec",spec); return result
            }
            let visualId=newId()
            tx.query("INSERT INTO visualization_specs (id,session_id,type,title,spec,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(visualId),Some(id),sqlValue(spec["type"]),sqlValue(spec["title"]),Some(spec.toString())])
            if (!observation.isEmpty()) {
                let interactionId=newId()
                tx.query("INSERT INTO visualization_interactions (id,visualization_id,user_id,`values`,observation,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(interactionId),Some(visualId),user["id"],Some(values.toString()),Some(observation)])
                let input=JsonObject(); input.put("type",spec["type"]); input.put("values",values)
                let output=JsonObject(); output.put("interaction_id",JsonString(interactionId)); output.put("observation",JsonString(observation)); output.put("expected_observation",spec["expected_observation"])
                recordTutorTool(tx,id,"visualization_evidence","observe_visualization",input,output)
            }
            return rowJson("visualization_specs",tx.query("SELECT * FROM visualization_specs WHERE id=?",[Some(visualId)]).rows[0])
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

func systemApi(context: HttpContext, kind: String): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,if (kind=="probe") { "POST" } else { "GET" })
    if (kind=="probe") { return systemState(probe:true)["provider"] }
    if (kind=="providers") { return systemState()["provider"] }
    if (kind=="voice") { return systemState()["voice"] }
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        db.query("SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM users")
        let state=systemState(); let provider=tutorObject(state,"provider"); let result=JsonObject()
        result.put("status",JsonString("healthy")); result.put("database",JsonString("mysql")); result.put("ai",provider["last_status"]); result.put("image_recognition",JsonBool(tutorNumber(provider,"vision_entries")>0)); return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

func transcribeVoice(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let owner=rowText(user,"id"); let form=Form(context.request.url.rawQuery ?? "")
        let questionId=queryLast(form,"question_id"); let workId=queryLast(form,"work_id"); validateVoiceContext(db,owner,questionId,workId)
        let upload=readImageUpload(context,audio:true); let parts=upload.filename.split("."); var suffix=".webm"
        if (parts.size>1) { let candidate="."+parts[parts.size-1].toAsciiLower(); for (allowed in [".webm",".wav",".mp3",".m4a",".ogg",".mp4"]) { if (candidate==allowed) { suffix=candidate } } }
        let path="uploads/voice/${owner}/${newId()}${suffix}"; writeMedia(path,upload.bytes)
        let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("voice_transcribe")); input.put("audio_base64",JsonString(toBase64String(upload.bytes))); input.put("audio_suffix",JsonString(suffix))
        var computed=JsonObject()
        try { computed=algorithmCompute(input) }
        catch (_: Exception) {
            let metadata=JsonObject(); metadata.put("model",JsonString("faster-whisper-small")); insertVoice(db,owner,questionId,workId,"stt",path,"",metadata,failed:true)
            throw ApiError(502,"语音转写失败，请稍后重试或使用文字输入")
        }
        let metadata=tutorObject(computed,"metadata"); let text=jsonText(computed,"text")
        let record=db.transaction<JsonObject>({ tx =>
            let saved=insertVoice(tx,owner,questionId,workId,"stt",path,text,metadata)
            if (!workId.isEmpty()) { tx.query("UPDATE student_works SET explanation=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=? AND user_id=?",[Some(text),Some(workId),Some(owner)]) }
            return saved
        })
        record.put("text",JsonString(text)); record.put("bytes",JsonInt(upload.bytes.size)); record.put("editable",JsonBool(true)); record.put("metadata",metadata); record.put("audio_url",JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl("/"+path))); return record
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

func synthesizeVoice(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let owner=rowText(user,"id"); let body=readJson(context)
        let text=stripWhitespace(optionalText(body,"text","",12000))
        if (text.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(400,"朗读文本不能为空") }
        let questionId=if ((body.get("question_id") ?? JsonNull()) is JsonNull) { "" } else { optionalText(body,"question_id","",36) }; validateVoiceContext(db,owner,questionId,"")
        let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("voice_synthesize")); input.put("text",JsonString(text)); input.put("voice",JsonString(optionalText(body,"voice","zh-CN-XiaoxiaoNeural",100)))
        let computed=algorithmCompute(input); let data=fromBase64String(jsonText(computed,"audio_base64")).getOrThrow()
        if (data.isEmpty() || data.size>64*1024*1024 || sha256(data)!=jsonText(computed,"sha256")) { throw ApiError(502,"语音合成结果无效") }
        let metadata=tutorObject(computed,"metadata"); let path="uploads/voice/${owner}/tts-${newId()}.mp3"; writeMedia(path,data)
        try {
            let record=insertVoice(db,owner,questionId,"","tts",path,text,metadata)
            record.put("text",JsonString(text)); record.put("browser_fallback",JsonBool(false)); record.put("metadata",metadata); record.put("audio_url",JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl("/"+path))); return record
        } catch (error: Exception) { try { removeMedia(path) } catch (_: Exception) {}; throw error }
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

func papersApi(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    let creating=context.request.method=="POST"; if (!creating) { requireMethod(context,"GET") }
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        if (!creating) { return queryArray(db,"papers","SELECT * FROM papers WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY created_at,id",rowText(user,"id")) }
        let body=paperPayload(readJson(context),true)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let id=newId(); let filters=tutorObject(body,"filters"); var ids=tutorArray(body,"question_ids")
            if (ids.getItems().isEmpty()) {
                let candidates=paperCandidatesData(tx,rowText(user,"id"),filters); let chosen=JsonArray(); var count: Int64=0
                for (candidate in candidates.getItems()) {
                    if (count>=tutorNumber(body,"limit")) { break }; chosen.add((candidate as JsonObject).getOrThrow()["id"]); count++
                }
                ids=chosen
            }
            let items=JsonArray()
            for (questionId in ids.getItems()) {
                let item=JsonObject(); item.put("question_id",questionId); var score: JsonValue=body["default_score"]
                for (scored in tutorArray(body,"items").getItems()) { let entry=(scored as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); if (entry["question_id"].toString()==questionId.toString()) { score=entry["score"] } }
                item.put("score",score); items.add(item)
            }
            tx.query("INSERT INTO papers (id,user_id,title,filters,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(id),user["id"],Some(if (body.get("title").isSome()) { jsonText(body,"title") } else { "我的错题练习" }),Some(filters.toString())])
            insertPaperItems(tx,id,rowText(user,"id"),items); return ownedPaper(tx,id,rowText(user,"id"))
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

func paperCandidates(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    let form=Form(context.request.url.rawQuery ?? ""); let raw=JsonObject()
    for (key in ["subject","knowledge_point","error_type"]) { let value=queryLast(form,key); if (!value.isEmpty()) { raw.put(key,JsonString(value)) } }
    for (key in ["mastery_min","mastery_max"]) {
        let value=queryLast(form,key)
        if (!value.isEmpty()) { try { raw.put(key,JsonValue.fromStr(value)) } catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422,"掌握度范围无效") } }
    }
    let due=queryLast(form,"due_review"); if (!due.isEmpty()) { raw.put("due_review",JsonString(due)) }
    let filters=paperFilters(raw)
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) { let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); return paperCandidatesData(db,rowText(user,"id"),filters) }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

func exportPaper(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body=readJson(context,defaultEmpty:true); let kind=if (body.get("kind").isNone()) { "question" } else { tutorText(body,"kind") }
        if (kind!="question" && kind!="answer" && kind!="excel") { throw ApiError(422,"不支持的导出格式") }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let paper=ownedPaper(tx,id,rowText(user,"id")); let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("render_paper")); input.put("paper_title",paper["title"]); input.put("paper_kind",JsonString(kind)); input.put("paper_questions",exportQuestions(tx,paper,kind!="excel"))
            if (kind=="answer") {
                var index: Int64=0
                for (value in tutorArray(input,"paper_questions").getItems()) {
                    index++; let item=(value as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
                    if (stripWhitespace(tutorText(item,"answer")).isEmpty() || stripWhitespace(tutorText(item,"explanation")).isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(409,"第${index}题缺少答案或解析，请先补齐分析后再导出答案 PDF") }
                }
            }
            let rendered=algorithmCompute(input); let bytes=fromBase64String(jsonText(rendered,"file_base64")).getOrThrow()
            if (bytes.isEmpty() || bytes.size>64*1024*1024 || sha256(bytes)!=jsonText(rendered,"sha256")) { throw ApiError(503,"导出文件校验失败") }
            let jobId=newId(); let suffix=if (kind=="excel") { "xlsx" } else { "pdf" }; let path="reports/${id}-${kind}-${jobId}.${suffix}"
            writeMedia(path,bytes)
            try {
                tx.query("INSERT INTO export_jobs (id,paper_id,kind,status,path,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,'completed',?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(jobId),Some(id),Some(kind),Some(path)])
                let result=rowJson("export_jobs",tx.query("SELECT * FROM export_jobs WHERE id=?",[Some(jobId)]).rows[0]); result.put("url",JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl("/"+path))); return result
            } catch (error: Exception) { try { removeMedia(path) } catch (_: Exception) {}; throw error }
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

func downloadPaper(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): Unit {
    var started=false
    try {
        requireMethod(context,"GET"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
        let form=Form(context.request.url.rawQuery ?? ""); let requested=queryLast(form,"kind"); let kind=if (requested.isEmpty()) { "question" } else { requested }
        try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
            let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); ownedPaper(db,id,rowText(user,"id"))
            let jobs=db.query("SELECT * FROM export_jobs WHERE paper_id=? AND kind=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[Some(id),Some(kind)]).rows
            if (jobs.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"请先生成导出文件") }
            let path=rowText(jobs[0],"path"); let data=readMedia(path); let suffix=if (kind=="excel") { "xlsx" } else { "pdf" }
            context.responseBuilder.header("Content-Type",if (kind=="excel") { "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet" } else { "application/pdf" })
                .header("Content-Disposition","attachment; filename=\"${id}-${kind}.${suffix}\"").header("Content-Length",data.size.toString())
            started=true; HttpResponseWriter(context).write(data)
        }
    } catch (error: ApiError) {
        if (started) { throw error }; let body=JsonObject(); body.put("detail",JsonString(error.message)); jsonResponse(context,body,status:error.status)
    } catch (error: Exception) {
        if (started) { throw error }; let body=JsonObject(); body.put("detail",JsonString("导出文件暂时不可用")); jsonResponse(context,body,status:500)
    }
}

```

```cangjie

func paperApi(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    let method=context.request.method
    if (method!="GET" && method!="PATCH" && method!="DELETE") { throw ApiError(405,"请求方法不支持") }
    let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-1]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body=if (method=="PATCH") { paperPayload(readJson(context),false) } else { JsonObject() }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            if (method=="DELETE") {
                let rows=tx.query("SELECT id FROM papers WHERE id=? AND user_id=?",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
                let result=JsonObject(); result.put("deleted",JsonBool(!rows.isEmpty()))
                if (rows.isEmpty()) { return result }
                tx.query("DELETE FROM export_jobs WHERE paper_id=?",[Some(id)]); tx.query("DELETE FROM paper_items WHERE paper_id=?",[Some(id)]); tx.query("DELETE FROM papers WHERE id=?",[Some(id)]); return result
            }
            ownedPaper(tx,id,rowText(user,"id"))
            if (method=="PATCH") {
                if (let Some(title) <- body.get("title")) { tx.query("UPDATE papers SET title=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[sqlValue(title),Some(id)]) }
                if (let Some(filters) <- body.get("filters")) { tx.query("UPDATE papers SET filters=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[Some(filters.toString()),Some(id)]) }
                if (body.get("items").isSome()) { tx.query("DELETE FROM paper_items WHERE paper_id=?",[Some(id)]); insertPaperItems(tx,id,rowText(user,"id"),tutorArray(body,"items")) }
            }
            return paperDetail(tx,accounts,ownedPaper(tx,id,rowText(user,"id")))
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

## POST /api/v1/variants/{variant_id}/attempt

- 新接口映射 `/api/v1/variants/{variant_id}/attempt`，方法保持 POST

- 调用闭包 `algorithmCompute`, `applyLearningEvidence`, `canonicalTutorStage`, `check`, `close`, `cognitionFirstLabel`, `completeTutorLearning`, `constantEqual`, `controlTransaction`, `credentialTag`, `currentUser`, `datasetDisplayOptions`, `datasetImageLayout`, `datasetImageMarkers`, `decodeBase64`, `editNumber`, `ensureTutorVariant`, `firstJson`, `fromEnvironment`, `hmac256`, `jsonText`, `learningKnowledgePoint`, `memoryDatasetVersion`, `memoryEvidenceSource`, `memoryQuestionVersion`, `newId`, `optionalText`, `query`, `queryArray`, `randomBytes`, `read`, `readJson`, `readToken`, `recordTutorTool`, `remaining`, `requireMethod`, `retrievalCandidates`, `retrievalGrade`, `retrievalPreparation`, `retrievalPreparationInput`, `rowJson`, `rowText`, `runeCount`, `sha256`, `sqlValue`, `stripWhitespace`, `teachingTemplates`, `transaction`, `tutorActivity`, `tutorAnswerText`, `tutorArray`, `tutorDataSnapshot`, `tutorFlag`, `tutorKnowledge`, `tutorNumber`, `tutorObject`, `tutorOptions`, `tutorStringArray`, `tutorStrings`, `tutorText`, `tutorVariantRetrieval`, `unixNow`, `unpaddedBase64`, `urlBase64`, `usableRetrievalCandidate`, `variantAttempt`, `zw_equal`, `zw_hmac`, `zw_media_close`, `zw_media_open`, `zw_media_stat`, `zw_mysql_transaction`, `zw_random`, `zw_sha256`, `zw_stmt_affected`, `zw_stmt_bind`, `zw_stmt_close`, `zw_stmt_column_name`, `zw_stmt_columns`, `zw_stmt_copy`, `zw_stmt_error`, `zw_stmt_execute`, `zw_stmt_length`, `zw_stmt_next`, `zw_stmt_param_count`, `zw_stmt_prepare`, `zw_stmt_value`

以下保留完整处理函数实现，包含分支参数、请求对象读取、验证、返回构造与错误规则。共享 helper 的 SQL、返回键及验证证据在功能清单逐函数索引。字段精确类型还需结合数据库报告中的 column_types 与算法 Pydantic 模型。

```cangjie

func variantAttempt(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body=readJson(context)
        let answer=optionalText(body,"answer","",12000)
        let elapsedValue=editNumber(body.get("elapsed_seconds") ?? JsonInt(0))
        if (elapsedValue<0.0 || elapsedValue>2147483647.0) { throw ApiError(422,"作答时长无效") }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let variants=tx.query("SELECT v.* FROM variant_questions v JOIN tutor_sessions s ON s.id=v.session_id WHERE v.id=? AND s.user_id=?",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (variants.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"迁移题不存在或无权访问") }
            let variant=rowJson("variant_questions",variants[0]); let sessionId=jsonText(variant,"session_id")
            let session=rowJson("tutor_sessions",tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id=?",[Some(sessionId)]).rows[0])
            let state=tutorObject(session,"state"); session.put("state",state)
            let transfer=jsonText(variant,"transfer_type"); let stage=canonicalTutorStage(jsonText(session,"stage")); session.put("stage",JsonString(stage))
            if (stage!="${transfer}_transfer") { throw ApiError(409,"这道练习与当前教学阶段不匹配") }
            let current=tutorText(state,"${transfer}_variant_id")
            if (!current.isEmpty() && current!=id) { throw ApiError(409,"这道练习已经变化，请刷新") }
            let snapshot=tutorDataSnapshot(tx,jsonText(session,"question_id"),user["id"]); let question=tutorObject(snapshot,"question")
            if (tutorFlag(state,"question_changed") || jsonText(question,"status")=="archived") { throw ApiError(409,"题目已变化或归档，请重新开始学习") }
            let hintValue=editNumber(body.get("hint_level") ?? session["hint_level"])
            if (hintValue<0.0 || hintValue>2147483647.0) { throw ApiError(422,"提示等级无效") }
            let input=JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("grade_answer")); input.put("text",variant["question_text"]); input.put("answer",JsonString(answer)); input.put("expected",variant["answer"])
            input.put("reference_solution",tutorArray(variant,"reference_solution")); input.put("subject",question["subject"]); input.put("question_type",question["question_type"])
            let grading=algorithmCompute(input); let decisive=tutorFlag(grading,"can_update_learning"); let correct=tutorFlag(grading,"correct")
            var feedback=jsonText(grading,"feedback"); let branch="${transfer}_transfer_${if (!decisive) { "review_required" } else if (correct) { "pass" } else { "fail" }}"
            var attempt: JsonValue=JsonNull()
            if (decisive) {
                let attemptId=newId()
                tx.query("INSERT INTO variant_attempts (id,variant_id,user_id,answer,correct,elapsed_seconds,hint_level,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(attemptId),Some(id),user["id"],Some(answer),sqlValue(grading["correct"]),Some(Int64(elapsedValue).toString()),Some(Int64(hintValue).toString())])
                attempt=rowJson("variant_attempts",tx.query("SELECT * FROM variant_attempts WHERE id=?",[Some(attemptId)]).rows[0])
                let count=tutorNumber(state,"${transfer}_attempts")+1; state.put("${transfer}_attempts",JsonInt(count)); state.put("branch",JsonString(branch))
                if (transfer=="near" && correct) { state.put("near_transfer",JsonBool(true)); session.put("stage",JsonString("far_transfer")); ensureTutorVariant(tx,session,snapshot,"far") }
                else if (transfer=="near" && count>=2) {
                    state.put("near_transfer",JsonBool(false)); state.put("assisted",JsonBool(true))
                    let explanation=if (tutorText(variant,"explanation").isEmpty()) { "先对照题目条件，再按顺序完成关键步骤。" } else { tutorText(variant,"explanation") }
                    state.put("near_confirmation",tutorActivity("near-remediation-${id}","看懂这一步就继续","根据刚才的讲解，这道题的参考结论是什么？",[("expected",tutorAnswerText(variant["answer"])),("unsure","我还不能确定")],["expected"],"",explanation))
                    feedback="先看这道题的简短讲解，再完成一个简单确认。"
                } else if (transfer=="far" && (correct || count>=2)) {
                    state.put("far_transfer",JsonBool(correct))
                    if (!correct) { state.put("transfer_insufficient",JsonBool(true)); state.put("assisted",JsonBool(true)) }
                    completeTutorLearning(tx,session,snapshot,!correct)
                    if (!correct) { feedback="这次换一种题目还不稳定，本轮先结束，并提高后续复习优先级。" }
                }
                tx.query("UPDATE tutor_sessions SET stage=?,state=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[sqlValue(session["stage"]),Some(state.toString()),Some(sessionId)])
            }
            let userMeta=JsonObject(); userMeta.put("branch",JsonString(branch)); userMeta.put("variant_id",JsonString(id))
            let assistantMeta=JsonObject(); assistantMeta.put("branch",JsonString(branch))
            if (!decisive) { userMeta.put("grading",grading); userMeta.put("attempt_consumed",JsonBool(false)); assistantMeta.put("grading_verdict",grading["verdict"]) }
            else { assistantMeta.put("correct",JsonBool(correct)) }
            for ((role,content,turnStage,metadata) in [("user",answer,"${transfer}_transfer",userMeta),("assistant",feedback,jsonText(session,"stage"),assistantMeta)]) {
                tx.query("INSERT INTO tutor_turns (id,session_id,role,content,stage,metadata_json,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(sessionId),Some(role),Some(content),Some(turnStage),Some(metadata.toString())])
            }
            let result=JsonObject(); result.put("attempt",attempt); result.put("correct",if (decisive) { JsonBool(correct) } else { JsonNull() }); result.put("branch",JsonString(branch)); result.put("stage",session["stage"]); result.put("feedback",JsonString(feedback)); result.put("grading",grading)
            if (decisive) {
                let toolInput=JsonObject(); toolInput.put("variant_id",JsonString(id)); toolInput.put("answer",JsonString(answer))
                let toolOutput=JsonObject(); toolOutput.put("correct",JsonBool(correct)); toolOutput.put("stage",session["stage"]); recordTutorTool(tx,sessionId,branch,"grade_answer",toolInput,toolOutput)
            }
            for ((name,table) in [("mastery","mastery_states"),("misconception","misconception_states")]) {
                let rows=if (decisive) { tx.query("SELECT * FROM ${table} WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY updated_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[user["id"]]).rows } else { tx.query("SELECT * FROM ${table} WHERE 1=0").rows }
                result.put(name,if (rows.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { rowJson(table,rows[0]) })
            }
            return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie
func readVisualization(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {

    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
```

```cangjie

func tutorDialogue(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body=readJson(context); let message=stripWhitespace(optionalText(body,"message","",12000))
        if (message.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422,"请输入你的想法") }
        let snapshot=db.transaction<JsonObject>({ tx => dialogueSnapshot(tx,id,user["id"]) }); let input=JsonObject(); let domain=dialogueContext(snapshot,message)
        input.put("operation",JsonString("tutor_dialogue_prepare")); input.put("dialogue_context",domain); input.put("dialogue_memory_rows",snapshot["memory"])
        let preparation=algorithmCompute(input); let plan=tutorObject(preparation,"plan"); let knowledge=tutorText(plan,"tool_name")=="knowledge_search"
        let cards=if (knowledge) { dialogueKnowledge(db,jsonText(preparation,"knowledge_query"),jsonText(domain,"subject"),jsonText(domain,"grade")) } else { JsonArray() }
        input.put("operation",JsonString("tutor_dialogue_finish")); input.put("dialogue_preparation",preparation); input.put("dialogue_cards",cards)
        let images=JsonArray(); let seen=HashSet<String>()
        for (value in tutorArray(snapshot,"assets").getItems()) {
            let path=jsonText((value as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),"path")
            if (seen.contains(path)) { continue }; seen.add(path)
            try { images.add(JsonString(toBase64String(readMedia(path)))) } catch (error: ApiError) { if (error.status!=404) { throw error } }
        }
        input.put("images_base64",images); let computed=algorithmCompute(input)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let current=dialogueSnapshot(tx,id,user["id"])
            if (!jsonEquivalent(snapshot,current)) { throw ApiError(409,"题目、会话或学习记录已变化，请刷新后重试") }
            if (knowledge && !jsonEquivalent(cards,dialogueKnowledge(tx,jsonText(preparation,"knowledge_query"),jsonText(domain,"subject"),jsonText(domain,"grade")))) { throw ApiError(409,"知识卡已更新，请重试") }
            let session=tutorObject(current,"session"); let state=tutorObject(session,"state"); let stage=jsonText(session,"stage"); let graph=tutorObject(computed,"graph"); let agent=tutorObject(computed,"agent"); let tool=tutorObject(agent,"tool"); let verification=tutorObject(agent,"verification")
            state.put("last_message",JsonString(message)); state.put("repeated_count",domain["repeated_count"])
            for (key in ["branch","strategy","next_goal","mastery_proven"]) { state.put(key,graph[key]) }
            if (state.get("understanding_passed").isNone()) { state.put("understanding_passed",JsonBool(false)) }
            state.put("dialogue_stage",graph["stage"]); state.put("agent_verification",verification["status"]); state.put("agent_memory_refs",plan["memory_source_ids"])
            if (tutorText(plan,"tool_name")=="visualization") { state.put("visualization_type",tutorObject(tool,"evidence").get("visualization_type") ?? JsonNull()) }
            tx.query("UPDATE tutor_sessions SET stage=?,turn_count=?,hint_level=?,state=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[Some(stage),sqlValue(domain["turn_count"]),sqlValue(graph["hint_level"]),Some(state.toString()),Some(id)])
            let userMeta=JsonObject(); userMeta.put("input_stage",JsonString(stage)); let metadata=JsonObject()
            for (key in ["branch","strategy","next_goal","mastery_proven"]) { metadata.put(key,graph[key]) }
            metadata.put("knowledge_sources",computed.get("knowledge_sources") ?? JsonArray()); metadata.put("tool_name",plan["tool_name"]); metadata.put("decision_verified",verification["status"]); metadata.put("planner_status",agent["planner_status"])
            for ((role,text,meta) in [("user",message,userMeta),("assistant",jsonText(computed,"reply"),metadata)]) { tx.query("INSERT INTO tutor_turns (id,session_id,role,content,stage,metadata_json,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(id),Some(role),Some(text),Some(stage),Some(meta.toString())]) }
            let output=tutorObject(tool,"evidence"); output.put("stage",JsonString(stage)); output.put("next_goal",graph["next_goal"]); output.put("executed",tool["executed"])
            let details=JsonObject(); details.put("memory_manifest",memoryAuditManifest(tutorObject(snapshot,"memory_snapshot"))); for (key in ["observation","plan","verification","planner_status"]) { details.put(key,agent[key]) }
            recordTutorTool(tx,id,jsonText(graph,"branch"),jsonText(plan,"tool_name"),tutorObject(tool,"consumed_input"),output,runResult:details)
            let result=JsonObject(); result.put("message",computed["reply"]); result.put("stage",JsonString(stage)); result.put("hint_level",graph["hint_level"]); for ((key,value) in metadata.getFields()) { result.put(key,value) }; return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

func teachBackTutor(context: HttpContext,accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body=readJson(context)
        let explanation=optionalText(body,"explanation","",12000)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows=tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id=? AND user_id=? FOR UPDATE",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"教学会话不存在或无权访问") }
            let session=rowJson("tutor_sessions",rows[0]); let stage=canonicalTutorStage(jsonText(session,"stage"))
            if (stage!="teach_back") { throw ApiError(409,"请先完成理解检查并进入讲清阶段") }
            let snapshot=tutorDataSnapshot(tx,jsonText(session,"question_id"),user["id"]); let question=tutorObject(snapshot,"question"); let state=tutorObject(session,"state"); session.put("state",state)
            if (tutorFlag(state,"question_changed") || jsonText(question,"status")=="archived") { throw ApiError(409,"题目已变化或归档，请重新开始学习") }
            let analysis=snapshot.get("analysis") ?? JsonNull()
            let points=if (analysis is JsonObject) { tutorArray((analysis as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),"knowledge_points") } else { tutorArray(question,"knowledge_points") }
            let assessment=evaluateTutorTeachBack(explanation,question,points,tutorStringArray(tutorRowTexts(snapshot["references"])))
            let passed=tutorFlag(assessment,"passed"); let count=tutorNumber(state,"teach_back_attempts")+1; let assisted = !passed && count>=2
            let branch=if (passed) { "teach_back_pass" } else { "teach_back_fail" }; var feedback=jsonText(assessment,"feedback")
            state.put("teach_back",JsonBool(passed)); state.put("teach_back_passed",JsonBool(passed)); state.put("teach_back_attempts",JsonInt(count)); state.put("branch",JsonString(branch))
            state.put("next_goal",JsonString(if (passed || assisted) { "完成一道相似题" } else { "补充一个关键理由" }))
            if (assisted) { state.put("assisted",JsonBool(true)) }
            session.put("stage",JsonString(stage))
            if (passed || assisted) {
                session.put("stage",JsonString("near_transfer")); ensureTutorVariant(tx,session,snapshot,"near")
                if (!passed) { feedback="这次还少一个关键点，我先记录下来，我们继续练一道相似题。" }
            }
            tx.query("UPDATE tutor_sessions SET stage=?,state=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[sqlValue(session["stage"]),Some(state.toString()),Some(id)])
            let userMeta=JsonObject(); userMeta.put("branch",JsonString(branch)); let assistantMeta=JsonObject(); assistantMeta.put("branch",JsonString(branch)); assistantMeta.put("passed",JsonBool(passed))
            for ((role,content,turnStage,metadata) in [("user",explanation,"teach_back",userMeta),("assistant",feedback,jsonText(session,"stage"),assistantMeta)]) {
                tx.query("INSERT INTO tutor_turns (id,session_id,role,content,stage,metadata_json,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(id),Some(role),Some(content),Some(turnStage),Some(metadata.toString())])
            }
            let input=JsonObject(); input.put("explanation",JsonString(explanation)); input.put("subject",question["subject"])
            let output=JsonObject(); output.put("passed",JsonBool(passed)); output.put("stage",session["stage"]); output.put("matched_concepts",assessment["matched_concepts"])
            recordTutorTool(tx,id,branch,"evaluate_teach_back",input,output)
            let result=JsonObject(); result.put("passed",JsonBool(passed)); result.put("assisted",JsonBool(assisted)); result.put("branch",JsonString(branch)); result.put("stage",session["stage"]); result.put("feedback",JsonString(feedback)); result.put("next_goal",state["next_goal"]); return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie
func finishTutor(context: HttpContext,accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db); readJson(context,defaultEmpty:true)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows=tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id=? AND user_id=? FOR UPDATE",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"教学会话不存在或无权访问") }
            let session=rowJson("tutor_sessions",rows[0]); session.put("stage",JsonString(canonicalTutorStage(jsonText(session,"stage"))))
            if (jsonText(session,"stage")!="finished") { throw ApiError(409,"请先完成讲清、相似题和综合应用") }
            let result=JsonObject()
            if (jsonText(session,"status")=="completed") {
                result.put("session",publicTutorSession(session)); result.put("mastery",JsonNull())
                let plans=tx.query("SELECT * FROM review_plans WHERE user_id=? AND question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",[user["id"],sqlValue(session["question_id"])]).rows
                result.put("review",if (plans.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { rowJson("review_plans",plans[0]) }); return result
            }
            let snapshot=tutorDataSnapshot(tx,jsonText(session,"question_id"),user["id"]); let state=tutorObject(session,"state"); session.put("state",state)
            if (tutorFlag(state,"question_changed") || jsonText(tutorObject(snapshot,"question"),"status")=="archived") { throw ApiError(409,"题目已变化或归档，请重新开始学习") }
            let insufficient=tutorFlag(state,"transfer_insufficient") || (state.get("far_transfer") ?? JsonNull()).toString()=="false"
            let learning=completeTutorLearning(tx,session,snapshot,insufficient)
            tx.query("UPDATE tutor_sessions SET stage=?,state=?,updated_at=UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id=?",[sqlValue(session["stage"]),Some(state.toString()),Some(id)])
            let saved=rowJson("tutor_sessions",tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id=?",[Some(id)]).rows[0])
            result.put("session",publicTutorSession(saved)); result.put("mastery",learning["mastery"]); result.put("review",learning["review"]); return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

public func answerTutorActivity(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST"); let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id = parts[parts.size-3]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body = readJson(context,maxBytes: 2 * 1024 * 1024)
        let answer = body.get("answer") ?? JsonString("")
        if (let Some(items) <- (answer as JsonArray)) {
            if (items.getItems().size > 100) { throw ApiError(422,"选项数量过多") }
            for (item in items.getItems()) { editText(item,12000) }
        } else { editText(answer,12000) }
        let working = (editText(body.get("working") ?? JsonString(""),12000,trim: true) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
        let elapsedNumber = editNumber(body.get("elapsed_seconds") ?? JsonInt(0))
        if (elapsedNumber < 0.0 || elapsedNumber > 2147483647.0) { throw ApiError(422,"作答时长无效") }
        let elapsed = Int64(elapsedNumber)
        let unknown = editBool(body.get("unknown") ?? JsonBool(false)).toString() == "true" || tutorAnswerText(answer) == "__unknown__"
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"教学会话不存在或无权访问") }
            let session = rowJson("tutor_sessions",rows[0]); let state = tutorObject(session,"state"); session.put("state",state)
            let stage = canonicalTutorStage(jsonText(session,"stage")); session.put("stage",JsonString(stage))
            if (stage != "understanding" && stage != "teach_back" && stage != "near_transfer" && stage != "far_transfer") { throw ApiError(409,"当前阶段不能继续作答") }
            let snapshot = tutorDataSnapshot(tx,jsonText(session,"question_id"),user["id"]); let question = (snapshot["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            if (tutorFlag(state,"question_changed") || jsonText(question,"status") == "archived") { throw ApiError(409,"题目已变化或归档，请重新开始学习") }
            let current = tutorActivityForSession(tx,session,snapshot)
            if (!(current is JsonObject)) { throw ApiError(409,"当前小任务已经变化，请刷新后继续") }
            let activity = (current as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            if (tutorText(body,"activity_id") != jsonText(activity,"id")) { throw ApiError(409,"当前小任务已经变化，请刷新后继续") }
            let handwriting = validateTutorHandwriting(body,rowText(user,"id"),id)
            var passed = false; var feedback = ""; var transfer = JsonObject()
            let compressed = tutorText(tutorObject(state,"teaching_policy"),"mode") == "compressed"
            if (stage == "near_transfer" || stage == "far_transfer") {
                transfer = answerTutorTransfer(tx,session,snapshot,answer,unknown,elapsed); passed = tutorFlag(transfer,"passed"); feedback = tutorText(transfer,"feedback")
            } else if (stage == "teach_back") {
                let assessment = evaluateTutorTeachBack(stripWhitespace(tutorAnswerText(answer)),question,tutorStringArray(tutorKnowledge(snapshot)),tutorStringArray(tutorRowTexts(snapshot["references"])))
                passed = tutorFlag(assessment,"passed"); let count = tutorNumber(state,"teach_back_attempts")+1; state.put("teach_back_attempts",JsonInt(count))
                if (passed || count >= 2) {
                    state.put("teach_back",JsonBool(passed)); state.put("teach_back_passed",JsonBool(passed))
                    if (!passed) { state.put("assisted",JsonBool(true)) }
                    session.put("stage",JsonString("near_transfer")); state.put("next_goal",JsonString("完成一道相似题"))
                    feedback = if (passed) { "讲清楚了，接着做一道相似题。" } else { "这次还少一个关键点，我先记录下来，我们继续练一道相似题。" }
                } else { feedback = jsonText(assessment,"feedback") }
            } else if (compressed && tutorText(activity,"type") == "explanation") {
                state.put("answer_reveal_acknowledged",JsonBool(true)); passed = true
                if (tutorAnswerText(answer) == "need_help") { state.put("targeted_check_pending",JsonBool(true)); feedback = "只确认一个易错点。" }
                else {
                    state.put("understanding_passed",JsonBool(true)); state.put("understood",JsonBool(true)); state.put("near_transfer",JsonNull())
                    session.put("stage",JsonString("near_transfer")); state.put("next_goal",JsonString("做一道相似题")); feedback = "好，直接练一道。"
                }
            } else if (compressed && tutorFlag(state,"targeted_check_pending")) {
                let source = tutorObject(tutorObject(state,"teaching_brief"),"check")
                passed = !unknown && tutorMicroPassed(source,answer,question)
                let attempts = tutorObject(state,"activity_attempts"); let key = tutorText(source,"id"); let count = tutorNumber(attempts,key)+1
                attempts.put(key,JsonInt(count)); state.put("activity_attempts",attempts)
                if (passed || count >= 2 || unknown) {
                    if (!passed) { state.put("assisted",JsonBool(true)) }
                    state.put("targeted_check_pending",JsonBool(false)); state.put("understanding_passed",JsonBool(true)); state.put("understood",JsonBool(true))
                    state.put("near_transfer",JsonNull()); session.put("stage",JsonString("near_transfer")); state.put("next_goal",JsonString("做一道相似题"))
                    feedback = if (passed) { "答对了，练一道类似的。" } else { "记住这个条件，我们直接练一道。" }
                } else { let hint = tutorText(source,"hint"); feedback = academicSummary(if (hint.isEmpty()) { "再用刚才的关键条件判断一次。" } else { hint },70) }
            } else {
                let plan = tutorArray(state,"understanding_plan"); let index = tutorNumber(state,"understanding_index")
                if (index < 0 || index >= plan.getItems().size) { throw ApiError(409,"活动计划已变化，请刷新") }
                let source = (plan[index] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
                if (tutorFlag(state,"understanding_confirmation")) {
                    state.put("assisted",JsonBool(true)); state.put("understanding_confirmation",JsonBool(false)); state.put("understanding_index",JsonInt(index+1)); passed = true
                    feedback = "记住这个判断方法，我们继续下一个小问题。"
                } else {
                    passed = !unknown && tutorMicroPassed(source,answer,question)
                    let attempts = tutorObject(state,"activity_attempts"); let key = jsonText(source,"id"); let count = tutorNumber(attempts,key)+1
                    attempts.put(key,JsonInt(count)); state.put("activity_attempts",attempts)
                    if (passed) { state.put("understanding_index",JsonInt(index+1)); feedback = "答对了，我们继续。" }
                    else if (unknown || count >= 2) { state.put("understanding_confirmation",JsonBool(true)); state.put("assisted",JsonBool(true)); feedback = "先看一个最短讲解，再确认一次就继续。" }
                    else { feedback = tutorText(source,"hint"); if (!feedback.isEmpty()) { feedback = academicSummary(feedback,70) } }
                }
                if (tutorNumber(state,"understanding_index") >= plan.getItems().size) {
                    state.put("understanding_passed",JsonBool(true)); state.put("understood",JsonBool(true)); state.put("next_goal",JsonString("用自己的话说一遍")); feedback = "已经抓住这个关键点。"
                }
            }
            if (jsonText(session,"stage") == "near_transfer") { ensureTutorVariant(tx,session,snapshot,"near") }
            state.put("activity_feedback",JsonString(feedback)); state.put("branch",JsonString("activity_${jsonText(session,"stage")}_${if (passed) { "pass" } else { "continue" }}"))
            tx.query("UPDATE tutor_sessions SET stage = ?,state = ?,turn_count = turn_count+1,updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?",[Some(jsonText(session,"stage")),Some(state.toString()),Some(id)])
            let userMeta = JsonObject(); userMeta.put("activity_id",activity["id"]); userMeta.put("activity_type",activity["type"])
            userMeta.put("working",JsonString(if (working.isEmpty()) { "" } else { shortTitle(working,500) })); userMeta.put("handwriting_image_url",handwriting["image_url"]); userMeta.put("handwriting_recognition",handwriting["recognition"]); userMeta.put("grading",tutorObject(transfer,"grading"))
            if (let Some(consumed) <- transfer.get("attempt_consumed")) { userMeta.put("attempt_consumed",consumed) }; if (tutorFlag(transfer,"unknown")) { userMeta.put("unknown",JsonBool(true)) }
            let assistantMeta = JsonObject(); assistantMeta.put("activity_id",activity["id"]); assistantMeta.put("passed",JsonBool(passed)); assistantMeta.put("assisted",JsonBool(tutorFlag(state,"assisted")))
            for ((role,content,metadata) in [("user",tutorAnswerLabel(activity,answer),userMeta),("assistant",if (tutorText(transfer,"assistant_content").isEmpty()) { feedback } else { tutorText(transfer,"assistant_content") },assistantMeta)]) {
                tx.query("INSERT INTO tutor_turns (id,session_id,role,content,stage,metadata_json,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(id),Some(role),Some(content),Some(jsonText(session,"stage")),Some(metadata.toString())])
            }
            let saved = rowJson("tutor_sessions",tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id = ?",[Some(id)]).rows[0])
            return tutorSessionPayload(tx,accounts,saved,snapshot)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}
```

## POST /api/v1/tutor/sessions/{session_id}/advance

- 新接口映射 `/api/v1/tutor/sessions/{session_id}/advance`，方法保持 POST

- 响应结构直接构建字段 activity_feedback, branch, event, from, message, next_goal, stage, state, teach_back, teach_back_attempts, teach_back_passed, to, understanding_passed, understood
- 调用闭包 `algorithmCompute`, `canonicalTutorStage`, `check`, `close`, `constantEqual`, `controlTransaction`, `credentialTag`, `currentUser`, `datasetDisplayOptions`, `datasetImageLayout`, `datasetImageMarkers`, `decodeBase64`, `ensureTutorVariant`, `firstJson`, `fromEnvironment`, `hmac256`, `jsonEquivalent`, `jsonText`, `mediaUrl`, `navigateTutor`, `newId`, `previousTutorStage`, `publicTutorActivity`, `publicTutorProgress`, `query`, `queryArray`, `randomBytes`, `read`, `readJson`, `readToken`, `recordTutorTool`, `remaining`, `requireMethod`, `retrievalCandidates`, `retrievalGrade`, `retrievalPreparation`, `retrievalPreparationInput`, `rowJson`, `rowText`, `sqlValue`, `stripWhitespace`, `teachingTemplates`, `transaction`, `transitionTutorStage`, `tutorActivity`, `tutorActivityForSession`, `tutorAnswerText`, `tutorArray`, `tutorDataSnapshot`, `tutorFlag`, `tutorKnowledge`, `tutorNumber`, `tutorObject`, `tutorOptions`, `tutorRowTexts`, `tutorSessionPayload`, `tutorStringArray`, `tutorStrings`, `tutorText`, `tutorVariantRetrieval`, `tutorVisualization`, `unixNow`, `unpaddedBase64`, `urlBase64`, `usableRetrievalCandidate`, `zw_equal`, `zw_hmac`, `zw_media_close`, `zw_media_open`, `zw_media_stat`, `zw_mysql_transaction`, `zw_random`, `zw_stmt_affected`, `zw_stmt_bind`, `zw_stmt_close`, `zw_stmt_column_name`, `zw_stmt_columns`, `zw_stmt_copy`, `zw_stmt_error`, `zw_stmt_execute`, `zw_stmt_length`, `zw_stmt_next`, `zw_stmt_param_count`, `zw_stmt_prepare`, `zw_stmt_value`

```cangjie

public func navigateTutor(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts, rewind: Bool): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id = parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        let body = if (rewind) { JsonObject() } else { readJson(context) }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"教学会话不存在或无权访问") }
            let session = rowJson("tutor_sessions",rows[0]); let state = tutorObject(session,"state"); session.put("state",state)
            let snapshot = tutorDataSnapshot(tx,jsonText(session,"question_id"),user["id"])
            let question = (snapshot["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            if (jsonText(question,"status") == "archived" || tutorFlag(state,"question_changed")) { throw ApiError(409,"题目已变化或归档，请重新开始学习") }
            let current = canonicalTutorStage(jsonText(session,"stage"))
            let compressed = tutorText(tutorObject(state,"teaching_policy"),"mode") == "compressed"
            var target = ""
            try {
                if (rewind) { target = if (compressed && current == "near_transfer") { "understanding" } else { previousTutorStage(current) } }
                else {
                    if (tutorText(body,"target_stage") != "teach_back") { throw ApiError(422,"只支持进入讲清阶段") }
                    target = transitionTutorStage(current,"understanding_advance",tutorFlag(state,"understanding_passed"))
                }
            } catch (error: IllegalArgumentException) { throw ApiError(409,error.message) }
            let branch = if (rewind) { "rewind_${current}_to_${target}" } else { "understanding_advance" }
            let input = JsonObject(); input.put("from",JsonString(current))
            let output = JsonObject(); output.put("to",JsonString(target))
            let message = "现在请用自己的话讲一遍：用了什么规则，为什么能用，又怎样得到结论。"
            if (!rewind) {
                state.put("next_goal",JsonString("用自己的话说明规则、依据和结论"))
                input.put("event",JsonString("understanding_advance"))
                tx.query("INSERT INTO tutor_turns (id,session_id,role,content,stage,metadata_json,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'assistant',?,'teach_back',?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(id),Some(message),Some(##"{"branch":"understanding_advance","explicit_transition":true}"##)])
            } else {
                if (current == "teach_back" || current == "near_transfer") {
                    state.put("teach_back",JsonNull()); state.put("teach_back_passed",JsonNull()); state.put("teach_back_attempts",JsonInt(0))
                }
                if (current == "teach_back") {
                    state.put("understanding_passed",JsonBool(true)); state.put("understood",JsonBool(true)); state.put("next_goal",JsonString("回顾已经理解的关键点"))
                } else {
                    for (key in ["near_transfer","near_confirmation","far_transfer"]) { state.put(key,JsonNull()) }
                    for (key in ["near_attempts","far_attempts"]) { state.put(key,JsonInt(0)) }
                    state.put("next_goal",JsonString(if (current == "near_transfer") { "重新用自己的话说清关键判断" } else { "重新完成一道相似题" }))
                    if (compressed && current == "near_transfer") {
                        for (key in ["understanding_passed","understood","answer_reveal_acknowledged","targeted_check_pending"]) { state.put(key,JsonBool(false)) }
                        state.put("next_goal",JsonString("重新查看答案讲解"))
                    }
                }
                let label = if (target == "understanding") { "理解" } else if (target == "teach_back") { "讲清" } else { "练习" }
                state.put("activity_feedback",JsonString("已返回“${label}”。"))
            }
            state.put("branch",JsonString(branch)); session.put("stage",JsonString(target))
            tx.query("UPDATE tutor_sessions SET stage = ?,state = ?,updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?",[Some(target),Some(state.toString()),Some(id)])
            recordTutorTool(tx,id,branch,if (rewind) { "rewind_tutor_stage" } else { "transition_tutor_stage" },input,output)
            if (rewind) {
                let saved = rowJson("tutor_sessions",tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id = ?",[Some(id)]).rows[0])
                return tutorSessionPayload(tx,accounts,saved,snapshot)
            }
            let result = JsonObject(); result.put("stage",JsonString(target)); result.put("message",JsonString(message)); result.put("next_goal",state["next_goal"])
            return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

public func tutorVariant(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id = parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body = readJson(context,defaultEmpty: true)
        let transfer = (editText(body.get("transfer_type") ?? JsonString("near"),10) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
        if (transfer != "near" && transfer != "far") { throw ApiError(422,"迁移类型必须是 near 或 far") }
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"教学会话不存在或无权访问") }
            let session = rowJson("tutor_sessions",rows[0]); let stage = canonicalTutorStage(jsonText(session,"stage"))
            if (stage != transfer+"_transfer") { throw ApiError(409,"当前教学阶段不能生成这类练习") }
            session.put("stage",JsonString(stage))
            let snapshot = tutorDataSnapshot(tx,jsonText(session,"question_id"),user["id"])
            let item = ensureTutorVariant(tx,session,snapshot,transfer); let result = JsonObject()
            for (key in ["id","question_id","session_id","transfer_type","question_text","verified","created_at","updated_at"]) { result.put(key,item[key]) }
            return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

public func readTutor(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id = parts[parts.size-1]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows = tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"教学会话不存在或无权访问") }
            let session = rowJson("tutor_sessions",rows[0]); let questionId = jsonText(session,"question_id")
            let snapshot = tutorDataSnapshot(tx,questionId,user["id"])
            let state = tutorObject(session,"state"); session.put("state",state)
            session.put("stage",JsonString(canonicalTutorStage(jsonText(session,"stage"))))
            if (!state.getFields().contains("understanding_passed")) { state.put("understanding_passed",JsonBool(false)) }
            if (!state.getFields().contains("assisted")) { state.put("assisted",JsonBool(false)) }
            if (tutorText(tutorObject(state,"teaching_policy"),"mode") == "compressed" && !tutorObject(state,"teaching_brief").getFields().isEmpty()) {
                for (key in ["answer_reveal_acknowledged","targeted_check_pending"]) {
                    if (!state.getFields().contains(key)) { state.put(key,JsonBool(false)) }
                }
            } else if (tutorArray(state,"understanding_plan").getItems().isEmpty()) {
                let question = (snapshot["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
                state.put("understanding_plan",buildUnderstandingPlan(jsonText(question,"question_text"),jsonText(question,"subject"),jsonText(question,"question_type"),tutorKnowledge(snapshot),tutorRowTexts(snapshot["references"])))
                state.put("understanding_index",JsonInt(0)); state.put("activity_attempts",JsonObject())
            }
            tx.query("UPDATE tutor_sessions SET stage = ?,state = ? WHERE id = ?",[Some(jsonText(session,"stage")),Some(state.toString()),Some(id)])
            return tutorSessionPayload(tx,accounts,session,snapshot)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

public func startTutor(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db); let body = readJson(context)
        let id = if (let Some(value) <- body.get("question_id")) { (editText(value,36,minimum: 1) as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue() } else { "" }
        let snapshot = db.transaction<JsonObject>({ tx => startTutorSnapshot(tx,id,user["id"]) })
        let question = (snapshot["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(); let analysis = (snapshot["analysis"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        let expected = diagnosisFingerprint(snapshot); let steps = tutorRowTexts(snapshot["steps"])
        let policy = adaptiveTeachingPolicy(jsonText(question,"question_type"),!(question["options"] as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems().isEmpty(),!steps.isEmpty(),jsonText(analysis,"final_answer"))
        var brief: JsonValue = JsonNull()
        if (jsonText(policy,"mode") == "compressed") {
            let input = JsonObject(); input.put("operation",JsonString("teaching_brief")); input.put("text",question["question_text"])
            for (key in ["student_answer","options"]) { input.put(key,question[key]) }
            input.put("question_type",policy["question_type"]); input.put("correct_answer",analysis["final_answer"])
            input.put("student_steps",tutorStringArray(steps)); input.put("knowledge_points",tutorStringArray(tutorKnowledge(snapshot)))
            input.put("diagnosis",if (snapshot["diagnosis"] is JsonObject) { snapshot["diagnosis"] } else { JsonObject() })
            brief = algorithmCompute(input)["brief"]
            if (!(brief is JsonObject)) { throw ApiError(503,"教学简报不完整") }
        }
        let state = initialTutorState(snapshot,policy,brief)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let current = startTutorSnapshot(tx,id,user["id"])
            if (!jsonEquivalent(diagnosisFingerprint(current),expected)) { throw ApiError(409,"题目或诊断已变化，请重新开始学习") }
            let sessionId = newId(); let opening = if (jsonText(policy,"mode") == "compressed") { "先看清正确答案和刚才选项的关键差别。" } else { "我们先只解决一个小问题。选出你认为正确的答案就可以。" }
            tx.query("INSERT INTO tutor_sessions (id,user_id,question_id,stage,hint_level,turn_count,mastery_score,state,status,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,'understanding',0,0,0,?,'active',UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(sessionId),user["id"],Some(id),Some(state.toString())])
            tx.query("INSERT INTO tutor_turns (id,session_id,role,content,stage,metadata_json,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'assistant',?,'understanding',?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",[Some(newId()),Some(sessionId),Some(opening),Some(##"{"branch":"opening","next_goal":"解释首错步骤"}"##)])
            let toolOutput = JsonObject(); toolOutput.put("diagnosis_id",if (let Some(diagnosis) <- (snapshot["diagnosis"] as JsonObject)) { diagnosis["id"] } else { JsonNull() })
            let runResult = JsonObject(); runResult.put("branch",JsonString("opening")); runResult.put("diagnosis_id",toolOutput["diagnosis_id"])
            let toolInput = JsonObject(); toolInput.put("question_id",JsonString(id)); let runId = newId()
            tx.query("INSERT INTO agent_runs (id,session_id,agent_name,status,result,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'mindtrace_tutor','completed',?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(runId),Some(sessionId),Some(runResult.toString())])
            tx.query("INSERT INTO agent_tool_calls (id,agent_run_id,tool_name,input_json,output_json,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'diagnosis_lookup',?,?,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",[Some(newId()),Some(runId),Some(toolInput.toString()),Some(toolOutput.toString())])
            let session = rowJson("tutor_sessions",tx.query("SELECT * FROM tutor_sessions WHERE id = ?",[Some(sessionId)]).rows[0])
            return tutorSessionPayload(tx,accounts,session,snapshot)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

public func diagnoseQuestion(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id = parts[parts.size - 2]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        let initial = db.transaction<JsonObject>({ tx => diagnosisSnapshot(tx,id,user["id"]) })
        if (initial["analysis"] is JsonNull) { analyzeQuestion(context,accounts) }
        let snapshot = db.transaction<JsonObject>({ tx => diagnosisSnapshot(tx,id,user["id"]) })
        if (snapshot["analysis"] is JsonNull) { throw ApiError(409,"题目分析已变化，请重新诊断") }
        let computed = algorithmCompute(diagnosisInput(snapshot))
        let diagnosis = (computed["diagnosis"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let current = diagnosisSnapshot(tx,id,user["id"])
            if (!jsonEquivalent(diagnosisFingerprint(snapshot),diagnosisFingerprint(current))) { throw ApiError(409,"题目、作答或分析已变化，请重新诊断") }
            let diagnosisId = newId()
            let values = ArrayList<Option<String>>([Some(diagnosisId),Some(id)])
            for (key in ["first_error_step","first_error_text","error_type","root_misconception","propagated_errors","strategy_candidates","confidence","error_location","missing_prerequisites","recommended_strategy"]) { values.add(sqlValue(diagnosis[key])) }
            tx.query("INSERT INTO diagnoses (id,question_id,first_error_step,first_error_text,error_type,root_misconception,propagated_errors,strategy_candidates,confidence,error_location,missing_prerequisites,recommended_strategy,user_confirmed,correction,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,0,JSON_OBJECT(),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",values.toArray())
            saveDiagnosticEvidence(tx,diagnosisId,snapshot,computed)
            syncKnowledgeLinks(tx,(snapshot["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),(snapshot["analysis"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),diagnosis: Some(diagnosis))
            tx.query("UPDATE questions SET status = 'diagnosed',updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?",[Some(id)])
            let result = rowJson("diagnoses",tx.query("SELECT * FROM diagnoses WHERE id = ?",[Some(diagnosisId)]).rows[0])
            result.put("evidence",computed["alignment"]); result.put("counterfactual",computed["counterfactual"])
            result.put("evidence_diagnosis",computed.get("evidence_diagnosis") ?? JsonNull())
            return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie
public func diagnosisRoute(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    if (context.request.method == "GET") { return getDiagnosis(context,accounts) }

}
```
