```cangjie

public func counterfactualQuestion(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"POST")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id = parts[parts.size - 3]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        let initial = db.transaction<(JsonObject,Option<JsonValue>)>({ tx =>
            let snapshot = diagnosisSnapshot(tx,id,user["id"])
            if (snapshot["analysis"] is JsonNull || snapshot["diagnosis"] is JsonNull) { throw ApiError(404,"题目诊断不存在") }
            let diagnosis = (snapshot["diagnosis"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            return (snapshot,cachedCounterfactual(tx,jsonText(diagnosis,"id")))
        })
        if (let Some(cached) <- initial[1]) { return cached }
        let snapshot = initial[0]
        let input = diagnosisInput(snapshot,includeImages: false)
        input.put("operation",JsonString("counterfactual"))
        let computed = algorithmCompute(input)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let current = diagnosisSnapshot(tx,id,user["id"])
            if (!jsonEquivalent(diagnosisFingerprint(current),diagnosisFingerprint(snapshot))) { throw ApiError(409,"题目或诊断已变化，请重新验证") }
            let diagnosis = (current["diagnosis"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow()
            let diagnosisId = jsonText(diagnosis,"id")
            if (let Some(cached) <- cachedCounterfactual(tx,diagnosisId)) { return cached }
            saveDiagnosticEvidence(tx,diagnosisId,current,computed,includeAlignment: false)
            syncKnowledgeLinks(tx,(current["question"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),(current["analysis"] as JsonObject).getOrThrow(),diagnosis: Some(diagnosis))
            return safeCounterfactual(computed["counterfactual"],diagnosis:Some(diagnosis))
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

- 调用闭包 `algorithmCompute`, `algorithmImage`, `bytesMatch`, `check`, `close`, `collapseWhitespace`, `constantEqual`, `controlTransaction`, `credentialTag`, `currentUser`, `decodeBase64`, `findBytes`, `finishRecognitionFailure`, `fromEnvironment`, `headerParameters`, `hmac256`, `ingestQuestion`, `invalidateQuestionLearning`, `isClosed`, `jsonText`, `mediaRoot`, `newId`, `nullableJsonText`, `query`, `queryLast`, `randomBytes`, `read`, `readAt`, `readImageUpload`, `readMedia`, `readToken`, `recognitionFingerprint`, `remaining`, `removeMedia`, `requireMethod`, `rowText`, `runRecognition`, `runeCount`, `sha256`, `shortTitle`, `stripWhitespace`, `transaction`, `unixNow`, `unpaddedBase64`, `uploadedImageSuffix`, `urlBase64`, `validStoragePath`, `writeMedia`, `zw_equal`, `zw_hmac`, `zw_media_close`, `zw_media_open`, `zw_media_read`, `zw_media_remove`, `zw_media_stat`, `zw_media_write`, `zw_mysql_transaction`, `zw_random`, `zw_sha256`, `zw_stmt_affected`, `zw_stmt_bind`, `zw_stmt_close`, `zw_stmt_column_name`, `zw_stmt_columns`, `zw_stmt_copy`, `zw_stmt_error`, `zw_stmt_execute`, `zw_stmt_length`, `zw_stmt_next`, `zw_stmt_param_count`, `zw_stmt_prepare`, `zw_stmt_value`

```cangjie

public func ingestQuestion(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context, "POST")
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context, db)
        let upload = readImageUpload(context)
        let form = Form(context.request.url.rawQuery ?? "")
        let subject = queryLast(form, "subject")
        let actualSubject = if (subject.isEmpty()) { "数学" } else { subject }
        if (runeCount(actualSubject) > 30) { throw ApiError(422, "学科名称过长") }
        let input = JsonObject()
        input.put("operation", JsonString("normalize"))
        input.put("image_base64", JsonString(toBase64String(upload.bytes)))
        let normalized = algorithmCompute(input)
        if (jsonText(normalized, "kind") != "original") { throw ApiError(503, "原图结果无效") }
        let bytes = algorithmImage(normalized)
        let id = newId()
        let jobId = newId()
        let path = "uploads/${id}/original.jpg"
        let uploadedPath = "uploads/${id}/uploaded." + uploadedImageSuffix(upload.bytes)
        let uploadedHash = sha256(upload.bytes)
        try {
            writeMedia(path, bytes)
            writeMedia(uploadedPath, upload.bytes)
            db.transaction<Unit>({ tx =>
                tx.query("INSERT INTO questions (id,user_id,subject,title,question_text,status,confirmed,favorite,tags,formulas,options,question_type,knowledge_points,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,'',?,0,0,JSON_ARRAY(),JSON_ARRAY(),JSON_ARRAY(),'综合题',JSON_ARRAY(),UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",
                    [Some(id), user["id"], Some(actualSubject), Some(upload.filename), Some(if (upload.deferred) { "awaiting_crop" } else { "processing" })])
                tx.query("INSERT INTO question_assets (id,question_id,kind,path,sha256,bbox,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'original',?,?,JSON_ARRAY(),UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",
                    [Some(newId()), Some(id), Some(path), Some(jsonText(normalized, "sha256"))])
                tx.query("INSERT INTO question_assets (id,question_id,kind,path,sha256,bbox,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'uploaded_original',?,?,JSON_ARRAY(),UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",
                    [Some(newId()), Some(id), Some(uploadedPath), Some(uploadedHash)])
                if (!upload.deferred) {
                    tx.query("INSERT INTO processing_jobs (id,question_id,kind,status,progress,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,'ingest','pending',0,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())", [Some(jobId), Some(id)])
                }
                ()
            })
        } catch (error: Exception) {
            try { removeMedia(path) } catch (_: Exception) {}
            try { removeMedia(uploadedPath) } catch (_: Exception) {}
            throw error
        }
        if (!upload.deferred) { spawn { runRecognition(jobId, id, path) } }
        let result = JsonObject()
        result.put("question_id", JsonString(id))
        result.put("job_id", if (upload.deferred) { JsonNull() } else { JsonString(jobId) })
        result.put("status", JsonString(if (upload.deferred) { "awaiting_crop" } else { "pending" }))
        return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503, "数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

- 调用闭包 `algorithmCompute`, `algorithmImage`, `check`, `close`, `collapseWhitespace`, `constantEqual`, `controlTransaction`, `credentialTag`, `currentUser`, `decodeBase64`, `finishRecognitionFailure`, `fromEnvironment`, `hmac256`, `invalidateQuestionLearning`, `isClosed`, `jsonText`, `mediaRoot`, `newId`, `nullableJsonText`, `query`, `randomBytes`, `read`, `readAt`, `readMedia`, `readToken`, `recognitionFingerprint`, `remaining`, `removeMedia`, `requireMethod`, `retryJob`, `rowJson`, `rowText`, `runRecognition`, `sha256`, `shortTitle`, `stripWhitespace`, `transaction`, `unixNow`, `unpaddedBase64`, `urlBase64`, `validStoragePath`, `writeMedia`, `zw_equal`, `zw_hmac`, `zw_media_close`, `zw_media_open`, `zw_media_read`, `zw_media_remove`, `zw_media_stat`, `zw_media_write`, `zw_mysql_transaction`, `zw_random`, `zw_sha256`, `zw_stmt_affected`, `zw_stmt_bind`, `zw_stmt_close`, `zw_stmt_column_name`, `zw_stmt_columns`, `zw_stmt_copy`, `zw_stmt_error`, `zw_stmt_execute`, `zw_stmt_length`, `zw_stmt_next`, `zw_stmt_param_count`, `zw_stmt_prepare`, `zw_stmt_value`

```cangjie

public func retryJob(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context, "POST")
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/")
    let id = parts[parts.size - 2]
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context, db)
        let owned = db.query("SELECT j.question_id FROM processing_jobs j JOIN questions q ON q.id = j.question_id WHERE j.id = ? AND q.user_id = ?", [Some(id), user["id"]]).rows
        if (owned.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404, "任务不存在或无权访问") }
        let questionId = rowText(owned[0], "question_id")
        let result = db.transaction<(JsonObject, Option<String>, Bool)>({ tx =>
            let questions = tx.query("SELECT status FROM questions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? FOR UPDATE", [Some(questionId), user["id"]]).rows
            if (questions.isEmpty() || rowText(questions[0], "status") == "archived") { throw ApiError(404, "题目不存在或已归档") }
            let jobs = tx.query("SELECT * FROM processing_jobs WHERE id = ? AND question_id = ? FOR UPDATE", [Some(id), Some(questionId)]).rows
            if (jobs.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404, "任务不存在") }
            let job = jobs[0]
            if (rowText(job, "status") != "failed") { throw ApiError(409, "任务仍在处理或已完成，请刷新后查看结果") }
            let kind = rowText(job, "kind")
            if (kind != "ingest" && kind != "crop" && kind != "ocr" && kind != "reprocess") { throw ApiError(409, "该任务不支持识别重试") }
            let active = tx.query("SELECT * FROM processing_jobs WHERE question_id = ? AND status IN ('pending','running') AND kind IN ('ingest','crop','ocr','reprocess') ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1", [Some(questionId)]).rows
            if (!active.isEmpty()) {
                let response = rowJson("processing_jobs", active[0])
                response.put("resumed", JsonBool(true))
                return (response, None, false)
            }
            let assets = tx.query("SELECT path,kind FROM question_assets WHERE question_id = ? AND (kind = 'crop' OR (kind = 'original' AND ? = 'ingest')) ORDER BY (kind = 'crop') DESC,created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1", [Some(questionId), Some(kind)]).rows
            if (assets.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(422, "缺少识别图片，请重新框选题目") }
            tx.query("UPDATE processing_jobs SET status = 'pending',progress = 0,error = NULL,updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?", [Some(id)])
            tx.query("UPDATE questions SET status = 'processing',updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?", [Some(questionId)])
            let updated = tx.query("SELECT * FROM processing_jobs WHERE id = ?", [Some(id)]).rows[0]
            return (rowJson("processing_jobs", updated), Some(rowText(assets[0], "path")), rowText(assets[0], "kind") == "crop")
        })
        if (let Some(path) <- result[1]) {
            let crop = result[2]
            spawn { runRecognition(id, questionId, path, requireCrop: crop) }
        }
        return result[0]
    }
    throw ApiError(503, "数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie
public func getJob(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
```

- 成功状态 200，错误状态证据 403, 405, 416
- 请求路径参数 无

- 响应结构直接构建字段 detail
- 调用闭包 `constantEqual`, `hmac256`, `isClosed`, `jsonResponse`, `mediaContentType`, `mediaRange`, `readAt`, `serveMedia`, `unixNow`, `validMedia`, `zw_equal`, `zw_hmac`, `zw_media_read`

以下保留完整处理函数实现，包含分支参数、请求对象读取、验证、返回构造与错误规则。共享 helper 的 SQL、返回键及验证证据在功能清单逐函数索引。字段精确类型还需结合数据库报告中的 column_types 与算法 Pydantic 模型。

```cangjie

public func serveMedia(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts, storageRoot!: Option<String> = None, storagePath!: Option<String> = None): Unit {
    var started = false
    try {
        let method = context.request.method
        if (method != "GET" && method != "HEAD") {
            context.responseBuilder.header("Allow", "GET, HEAD")
            throw ApiError(405, "请求方法不支持")
        }
        if (!accounts.validMedia(context)) { throw ApiError(403, "资源链接已失效，请返回页面刷新") }
        let path = storagePath ?? context.request.url.path[1..]
        let root = storageRoot ?? (getVariable("CANGJIE_MEDIA_ROOT") ?? "media")
        try (file = MediaFile(root, path)) {
            context.responseBuilder.header("ETag", file.etag).header("Accept-Ranges", "bytes")
                .header("Cache-Control", "private, max-age=0, must-revalidate")
                .header("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
                .header("Content-Type", mediaContentType(path))
            for (tag in (context.request.headers.getFirst("If-None-Match") ?? "").split(",")) {
                let clean = tag.trimAscii()
                if (clean == "*" || clean == file.etag || clean == "W/" + file.etag) {
                    context.responseBuilder.status(304).body("")
                    return
                }
            }
            if (method == "HEAD") {
                context.responseBuilder.header("Content-Length", file.length.toString()).body(HeadOnlyBody())
                return
            }
            var offset: Int64 = 0
            var remaining = file.length
            let range = context.request.headers.getFirst("Range") ?? ""
            let ifRange = context.request.headers.getFirst("If-Range") ?? file.etag
            // A server may ignore multipart ranges; respond with the full resource.
            if (!range.isEmpty() && !range.contains(",") && ifRange == file.etag) {
                try {
                    let (start, count) = mediaRange(range, file.length)
                    offset = start
                    remaining = count
                    context.responseBuilder.status(206).header("Content-Range", "bytes ${start}-${start + count - 1}/${file.length}")
                } catch (error: ApiError) {
                    context.responseBuilder.header("Content-Range", "bytes */${file.length}")
                    throw error
                }
            }
            context.responseBuilder.header("Transfer-Encoding", "chunked")
            let writer = HttpResponseWriter(context)
            while (remaining > 0) {
                let capacity = if (remaining > 65536) { 65536 } else { remaining }
                let buffer = Array<UInt8>(capacity, repeat: 0)
                let count = file.readAt(buffer, offset)
                if (count <= 0) { throw IllegalStateException("文件读取中断") }
                started = true
                writer.write(buffer[..count])
                offset += count
                remaining -= count
            }
        }
    } catch (error: ApiError) {
        if (started) { throw error }
        let result = JsonObject(); result.put("detail", JsonString(error.message))
        jsonResponse(context, result, status: error.status)
    } catch (error: Exception) {
        if (started) { throw error }
        let result = JsonObject(); result.put("detail", JsonString("资源暂时不可用"))
        jsonResponse(context, result, status: 500)
    }
}

```

```cangjie
            let writer = HttpResponseWriter(context)
            while (remaining > 0) {
                let capacity = if (remaining > 65536) { 65536 } else { remaining }
                let buffer = Array<UInt8>(capacity, repeat: 0)
                let count = file.readAt(buffer, offset)
                if (count <= 0) { throw IllegalStateException("文件读取中断") }
                started = true
                writer.write(buffer[..count])
                offset += count
                remaining -= count
            }
        }
    } catch (error: ApiError) {
        if (started) { throw error }
        let result = JsonObject(); result.put("detail", JsonString(error.message))
        jsonResponse(context, result, status: error.status)
    } catch (error: Exception) {
        if (started) { throw error }
        let result = JsonObject(); result.put("detail", JsonString("资源暂时不可用"))
        jsonResponse(context, result, status: 500)
    }
}

```

```cangjie

public func questionList(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context, "GET")
    let form = try { Form(context.request.url.rawQuery ?? "") }
        catch (_: Exception) { throw ApiError(422, "查询参数格式不正确") }
    let subject = queryLast(form, "subject")
    let search = queryLast(form, "search")
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context, db)
        let sql = StringBuilder()
        let params = ArrayList<Option<String>>([user["id"]])
        sql.append("SELECT q.* FROM questions q WHERE q.user_id = ? AND q.status <> 'archived'")
        if (!subject.isEmpty()) { sql.append(" AND q.subject = ?"); params.add(Some(subject)) }
        if (!search.isEmpty()) {
            sql.append(" AND (q.question_text LIKE ? OR q.title LIKE ? OR CAST(q.knowledge_points AS CHAR) LIKE ? OR CAST(q.tags AS CHAR) LIKE ? OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM diagnoses d WHERE d.question_id = q.id AND (d.root_misconception LIKE ? OR d.error_type LIKE ? OR d.first_error_text LIKE ?")
            for (_ in 0..7) { params.add(Some("%${search}%")) }
            let codes = HashSet<String>()
            for (item in errorTaxonomy.getItems()) {
                let pair = (item as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems()
                let code = (pair[0] as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
                let label = (pair[1] as JsonString).getOrThrow().getValue()
                if ((search.contains(label) || label.contains(search)) && !codes.contains(code)) {
                    codes.add(code)
                    sql.append(" OR d.error_type = ?")
                    params.add(Some(code))
                }
            }
            sql.append(")))")
        }
        sql.append(" ORDER BY q.created_at DESC")
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let result = JsonArray()
            let seen = HashSet<String>()
            for (row in tx.query(sql.toString(), params.toArray()).rows) {
                let id = rowText(row, "id")
                let key = questionDisplayKey(row["question_text"] ?? "", id)
                if (seen.contains(key)) { continue }
                seen.add(key)
                let item = rowJson("questions", row)
                let diagnosis = tx.query("SELECT root_misconception FROM diagnoses WHERE question_id = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1", [Some(id)]).rows
                item.put("diagnosis_summary", JsonString(if (diagnosis.isEmpty()) { "等待完成错因诊断" } else { rowText(diagnosis[0], "root_misconception") }))
                let review = tx.query("SELECT due_at FROM review_plans WHERE question_id = ? AND user_id = ? AND status = 'pending' ORDER BY due_at LIMIT 1", [Some(id), user["id"]]).rows
                item.put("review_due_at", if (review.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { rowJson("review_plans", review[0])["due_at"] })
                var knowledge = row["knowledge_points"] ?? "[]"
                if (knowledge == "null" || (JsonValue.fromStr(knowledge) as JsonArray).getOrThrow().getItems().isEmpty()) { knowledge = "[\"__none__\"]" }
                let mastery = tx.query("SELECT mastery_score FROM mastery_states WHERE user_id = ? AND subject = ? AND JSON_CONTAINS(?, JSON_QUOTE(knowledge_point)) ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT 1", [user["id"], row["subject"], Some(knowledge)]).rows
                let score = if (mastery.isEmpty()) { 0.0 } else { Float64.parse(rowText(mastery[0], "mastery_score")) }
                item.put("mastery_score", JsonFloat(score))
                item.put("mastery_status", JsonString(if (score >= 0.75) { "基本稳定" } else if (score >= 0.5) { "继续巩固" } else { "需要巩固" }))
                let asset = tx.query("SELECT path FROM question_assets WHERE question_id = ? AND kind IN ('crop','original') ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1", [Some(id)]).rows
                item.put("thumbnail_url", if (asset.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { JsonString(accounts.mediaUrl("/" + rowText(asset[0], "path"))) })
                let job = tx.query("SELECT id,status FROM processing_jobs WHERE question_id = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1", [Some(id)]).rows
                item.put("latest_job_id", if (job.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { JsonString(rowText(job[0], "id")) })
                item.put("latest_job_status", if (job.isEmpty()) { JsonNull() } else { JsonString(rowText(job[0], "status")) })
                result.getItems().add(item)
            }
            return result
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503, "数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

- 调用闭包 `bytesMatch`, `close`, `constantEqual`, `controlTransaction`, `copyStudyFigures`, `createManualQuestion`, `credentialTag`, `currentUser`, `datasetDisplayOptions`, `datasetImageLayout`, `datasetImageMarkers`, `decodeBase64`, `fromEnvironment`, `hmac256`, `isClosed`, `jsonText`, `manualArray`, `mediaRoot`, `newId`, `optionalAnswer`, `optionalText`, `ownedStudy`, `query`, `randomBytes`, `read`, `readAt`, `readJson`, `readToken`, `removeMedia`, `requireMethod`, `rowText`, `runeCount`, `sha256`, `stripWhitespace`, `studyItems`, `studyMistakeSource`, `studyQuestionAvailable`, `studySessionRow`, `textField`, `transaction`, `tutorAnswerText`, `tutorArray`, `tutorFlag`, `tutorObject`, `tutorText`, `unixNow`, `unpaddedBase64`, `uploadedImageSuffix`, `urlBase64`, `validStoragePath`, `writeMedia`, `zw_equal`, `zw_hmac`, `zw_media_close`, `zw_media_open`, `zw_media_read`, `zw_media_remove`, `zw_media_stat`, `zw_media_write`, `zw_mysql_transaction`, `zw_random`, `zw_sha256`, `zw_stmt_affected`, `zw_stmt_bind`, `zw_stmt_close`, `zw_stmt_column_name`, `zw_stmt_columns`, `zw_stmt_copy`, `zw_stmt_error`, `zw_stmt_execute`, `zw_stmt_length`, `zw_stmt_next`, `zw_stmt_param_count`, `zw_stmt_prepare`, `zw_stmt_value`

```cangjie

public func createManualQuestion(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context, "POST")
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context, db)
        if (rowText(user, "role") != "student") { throw ApiError(403, "请使用学生账号记录错题") }
        let body = readJson(context, maxBytes: 8 * 1024 * 1024)
        let source = studyMistakeSource(db,rowText(user,"id"),body)
        let text = textField(body, "question_text", 12000)
        let subject = textField(body, "subject", 30, fallback: Some("数学"))
        let kind = textField(body, "question_type", 50, fallback: Some("综合题"))
        if (kind != "综合题" && kind != "单选题" && kind != "多选题") { throw ApiError(422, "题目类型不正确") }
        var grade: Option<String> = None
        if (let Some(value) <- body.get("grade")) {
            if (let Some(item) <- (value as JsonString)) {
                if (runeCount(item.getValue()) > 30) { throw ApiError(422, "年级过长") }
                grade = Some(item.getValue())
            } else if (!(value is JsonNull)) { throw ApiError(422, "年级格式不正确") }
        }
        let answer = optionalAnswer(body)
        let options = manualArray(body, "options", 8)
        if ((kind == "综合题" && !options.getItems().isEmpty()) ||
            (kind != "综合题" && options.getItems().size < 2)) {
            throw ApiError(422, "题目类型和选项数量不匹配")
        }
        let labels = HashSet<String>()
        let validatedOptions = JsonArray()
        for (item in options.getItems()) {
            if (let Some(option) <- (item as JsonObject)) {
                let label = textField(option, "label", 1, trim: false)
                if (!"ABCDEFGH".contains(label) || labels.contains(label)) { throw ApiError(422, "选项标记无效或重复") }
                labels.add(label)
                let clean = JsonObject()
                clean.put("label", JsonString(label))
                clean.put("text", JsonString(textField(option, "text", 12000)))
                validatedOptions.getItems().add(clean)
            } else { throw ApiError(422, "选项格式不正确") }
        }
        let steps = ArrayList<String>()
        for (item in manualArray(body, "student_steps", 30).getItems()) {
            if (let Some(value) <- (item as JsonString)) {
                let clean = stripWhitespace(value.getValue())
                if (clean.isEmpty() || runeCount(clean) > 12000) { throw ApiError(422, "步骤文字长度不正确") }
                steps.add(clean)
            } else { throw ApiError(422, "步骤必须为文字") }
        }
        let title = StringBuilder()
        var count: Int64 = 0
        for (rune in text.runes()) {
            if (count >= 80) { break }
            title.append(rune)
            count++
        }
        let id = newId()
        let workId = newId()
        let studentGrade = grade
        let copied=ArrayList<String>()
        let savedId=try { db.transaction<String>({ tx =>
            let itemId=optionalText(body,"study_item_id","",36)
            if (!source.getFields().isEmpty()) {
                ownedStudy(tx,optionalText(body,"study_session_id","",36),rowText(user,"id"),lock:true)
                let previous=tx.query("SELECT id FROM questions WHERE user_id=? AND status<>'archived' AND JSON_CONTAINS(tags,JSON_QUOTE(?)) LIMIT 1",[user["id"],Some("study-item:"+itemId)]).rows
                if (!previous.isEmpty()) { return rowText(previous[0],"id") }
            }
            tx.query("INSERT INTO questions (id,user_id,subject,grade,title,question_text,student_answer,status,confirmed,favorite,tags,formulas,options,question_type,knowledge_points,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,'confirmed',1,0,JSON_ARRAY(),JSON_ARRAY(),?,?,JSON_ARRAY(),UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",
                [Some(id), user["id"], Some(subject), studentGrade, Some(title.toString()), Some(text),
                 if (answer.isEmpty()) { None } else { Some(answer) }, Some(validatedOptions.toString()), Some(kind)])
            tx.query("INSERT INTO student_works (id,question_id,user_id,answer,explanation,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,'',UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6))",
                [Some(workId), Some(id), user["id"], Some(answer)])
            for (index in 0..steps.size) {
                let step = steps[index]
                tx.query("INSERT INTO student_steps (id,work_id,step_index,text,bbox,raw_text,normalized_text,formula,confidence,source,user_confirmed,created_at,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,JSON_ARRAY(),?,?,?,1,'user',1,UTC_TIMESTAMP(),UTC_TIMESTAMP())",
                    [Some(newId()), Some(workId), Some(index.toString()), Some(step), Some(step), Some(step),
                     Some(if (step.contains("=")) { step } else { "" })])
            }
            if (!source.getFields().isEmpty()) {
                let tags=JsonArray(); tags.add(JsonString("study-item:"+itemId))
                tx.query("UPDATE questions SET tags=?,knowledge_points=? WHERE id=?",[Some(tags.toString()),Some(tutorArray(source,"knowledge_points").toString()),Some(id)])
                copyStudyFigures(tx,id,source,copied)
            }
            return id
        }) } catch (error: Exception) {
            for (path in copied) { try { removeMedia(path) } catch (_: Exception) {} }
            throw error
        }
        let result = JsonObject()
        result.put("id", JsonString(savedId))
        result.put("status", JsonString("confirmed"))
        return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503, "数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

func learningJourney(context: HttpContext,accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    requireMethod(context,"GET"); let parts=context.request.url.path.split("/"); let id=parts[parts.size-2]
    try (db=Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user=accounts.currentUser(context,db)
        return db.transaction<JsonValue>({ tx =>
            let rows=tx.query("SELECT * FROM questions WHERE id=? AND user_id=? AND status<>'archived'",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows
            if (rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404,"题目不存在") }
            let data=JsonObject(); data.put("question",rowJson("questions",rows[0]))
            for ((key,table) in [("analysis","problem_analyses"),("diagnosis","diagnoses"),("job","processing_jobs"),("ocr","ocr_runs")]) { data.put(key,firstJson(queryArray(tx,table,"SELECT * FROM ${table} WHERE question_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1",id))) }
            for ((key,table,column) in [("sessions","tutor_sessions","question_id"),("practices","dataset_practice_attempts","mistake_id"),("plans","review_plans","question_id")]) {
                let values=JsonArray(); let filter=if (key=="plans") { " AND status='pending' ORDER BY due_at,id" } else { " ORDER BY created_at DESC,id DESC" }
                for (row in tx.query("SELECT * FROM ${table} WHERE ${column}=? AND user_id=?"+filter+" LIMIT 50",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows) { values.add(rowJson(table,row)) }; data.put(key,values)
            }
            let reviews=JsonArray(); for (row in tx.query("SELECT r.* FROM review_records r JOIN review_plans p ON p.id=r.plan_id WHERE p.question_id=? AND p.user_id=? ORDER BY r.created_at DESC,r.id DESC LIMIT 50",[Some(id),user["id"]]).rows) { reviews.add(rowJson("review_records",row)) }; data.put("reviews",reviews)
            let now=rowText(tx.query("SELECT UTC_TIMESTAMP(6) AS now").rows[0],"now").replace(" ","T")+"+00:00"
            return addTaskJourney(tx,buildLearningJourney(data,now),rowText(user,"id"),id)
        })
    }
    throw ApiError(503,"数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie

public func questionRoute(context: HttpContext, accounts: Accounts): JsonValue {
    let parts = context.request.url.path.split("/")
    let id = parts[parts.size - 1]
    if (context.request.method == "GET") { return questionDetail(context, accounts, id) }
    if (context.request.method == "PATCH") { return questionEdit(context, accounts, id) }
    if (context.request.method != "DELETE") {
        context.responseBuilder.header("Allow", "GET, PATCH, DELETE")
        throw ApiError(405, "请求方法不支持")
    }
    try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
        let user = accounts.currentUser(context, db)
        db.transaction<Unit>({ tx =>
            let owned = tx.query("SELECT id FROM questions WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? FOR UPDATE",
                [Some(id), user["id"]])
            if (owned.rows.isEmpty()) { throw ApiError(404, "内容不存在或无权访问") }
            tx.query("UPDATE questions SET status = 'archived', updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE id = ?",
                [Some(id)])
            tx.query("UPDATE review_plans SET status = 'cancelled', updated_at = UTC_TIMESTAMP() WHERE question_id = ? AND status = 'pending'",
                [Some(id)])
            ()
        })
        let result = JsonObject()
        result.put("deleted", JsonBool(true))
        return result
    }
    throw ApiError(503, "数据库暂时不可用")
}

```

```cangjie
    server.distributor.register("/livez", { context =>

    })
    server.distributor.register("/readyz", { context =>
        context.responseBuilder.header("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
        if (context.request.method != "GET") {
            context.responseBuilder.status(405).header("Allow", "GET").body("")
        } else {
            try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
                db.query("SELECT id FROM users LIMIT 1")
                let version = db.query("SELECT version FROM cangjie_schema_versions WHERE version = ?", [Some("010_study_sessions")])
                if (version.rows.size != 1) { throw DatabaseException(2000) }
                context.responseBuilder.body(##"{"database":"mysql","schema":"010_study_sessions","status":"database_ready","ai":"not_connected"}"##)
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                context.responseBuilder.status(503).body(##"{"status":"database_unavailable"}"##)
            }
        }
        ()
    })
    println("错题帮仓颉独立后端 http://127.0.0.1:${port}")
```

## GET /readyz

- 新接口映射 `/readyz`，方法保持 GET
- 注册证据 `backend-cangjie/src/main.cj:112`，处理函数 `main 内联健康处理`
- 认证 公开入口，当前实现未调用 currentUser
- 权限 遵循处理函数及所有权 helper，学生业务按 user_id/owner 限定
- 成功状态 200，错误状态证据 见内联处理与公共规则
- 请求路径参数 无
- 请求 JSON 直接字段 以以下源代码读取行为为准，可能无 JSON 或使用 multipart
- 响应结构直接构建字段 由返回 helper/数据库行构建，见调用闭包与 rowJson 类型字典
- 调用闭包 

以下保留完整处理函数实现，包含分支参数、请求对象读取、验证、返回构造与错误规则。共享 helper 的 SQL、返回键及验证证据在功能清单逐函数索引。字段精确类型还需结合数据库报告中的 column_types 与算法 Pydantic 模型。

```cangjie
    server.distributor.register("/readyz", { context =>
        context.responseBuilder.header("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
        if (context.request.method != "GET") {
            context.responseBuilder.status(405).header("Allow", "GET").body("")
        } else {
            try (db = Database.fromEnvironment()) {
                db.query("SELECT id FROM users LIMIT 1")
                let version = db.query("SELECT version FROM cangjie_schema_versions WHERE version = ?", [Some("010_study_sessions")])
                if (version.rows.size != 1) { throw DatabaseException(2000) }
                context.responseBuilder.body(##"{"database":"mysql","schema":"010_study_sessions","status":"database_ready","ai":"not_connected"}"##)
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                context.responseBuilder.status(503).body(##"{"status":"database_unavailable"}"##)
            }
        }
        ()
    })
    println("错题帮仓颉独立后端 http://127.0.0.1:${port}")
    server.serve()
    return 0
}
```

## 序列化与前端兼容约束

row_json.cj 按 column_types.json/task_column_types.json 将 SQL 字符串转换成 JSON、bool、int、float、datetime，空值为 null。日期为 T 分隔及 +00:00，部分 admin 字段存在不同格式，应按接口保留。questions 标题会对截断 TeX 前缀进行响应修复。前端 request 自动取 data，401 清令牌并重定向，hydrateMedia 递归补全 *_url/url。上传响应是字符串 JSON，下载与图片依赖签名 URL。学习任务和练习场有版本冲突 409、轮询、202、已提交重复请求返回历史结果等依赖。
